**`30:C7D6` FadeSaveZpToWram** — copies ZP `$0A-$29` → `$6000`, `SwitchBank16(13)`, `26:$8C3D(A=4)`, waits 30 frames on `$09`, `26:$8C49(A=4)`, `SwitchBank16(8)`, restores `$6000`→`$0A`. `$8C3D` sets `$11=0,$19=$10`; `$8C49` sets `$11=$40,$19=$F0` → opposite step directions (fade out/in, tentative).

**Sound driver (bank 24/25, session 2) (VERIFIED self-emu, s39/s41)** — `search_bytes.py "8D ?? 40"`: the only swappable-bank `STA $40xx` writes are in bank 24 (19 hits `24:8042-8EBB`); bank 28 has none. Layers:

| Addr | Name | Role |
|------|------|------|
| `30:C125` | PlaySound | **Game API**, 85 callers (banks 14/15/19/23/26/27/30/31). A=id. Y saved/returned via `$010F`. Ids `<$20` = music: skipped if == `$0102`, else `$0102`=id. `$80-$9F` → `AND #$7F` (forced re-issue). `≥$A0` / `$20-$7F` → SFX. Sets `$0101` lock, appends to FIFO `$0103+$0100`; when 5 queued, SFX overwrite slot 5, music is dropped. (`C120` is a 2nd entry used by bank 6 data? — xrefs `06:80FE/84FE` are likely false positives.) |
| `31:F7FE` | SoundUpdate | **Corrected s41:** `F4CF` is inside **IRQ handler 1** (`F488`, the gameplay HUD split; wrapped by `$06`=1, then runs the missed NMI-mode routine `F6AD` if `$07`≠0), not the NMI. Other callers: IRQ handler 5 (`F611`) and the JMP tails `F6DC/F76B/F77F` of the NMI-mode routines (`F6AD`: `JMP (JmpTable31_F6C2[irq_mode])`; mode 0/7 `F6D6` calls it only when PPUMASK shadow `$01`&`$18`=0, i.e. rendering off and no IRQ). Run s41: `F4CF` 103×, `F6DC` 34×, `F77F` 2107× (title, mode 3). **Double tick (s42 tour):** on the frame an area load turns rendering off, the IRQ still fires (`F4CF` at line 191) and then the NMI's mode-0 tail runs SoundUpdate again (`F6DC` at line ~247), so music/SFX advance 2 ticks in that frame, once per area load; if both ticks find a queued command, 2 `SndCommand`s run in one frame. The port must reproduce this (one extra driver tick per area transition). Mode 8's table entry is `$0000`. Writes MMC3 R6=24, R7=25 directly (no shadow update). If `$0102` bit7 and music idle (`$0111=$FF`, `$0168-$016A`=0?) re-issues music. Else if not locked and queue non-empty: `$0110`←`$0103`, `JSR SndCommand`, shift FIFO, `DEC $0100`. Then R7=25, `JSR SndTick`, restore R6/R7 from `$6E/$6F` and `$8000` from `$50`. → **one sound command per frame**. |
| `24:8020` | SndInit | silence: `$F0`=0, `$0111/$0112`=`$FF`, `$0114-$0119`=0, `$FB`=0, `$4015`=0. Called once from Reset (`31:F325`). |
| `24:8048` | SndCommand | sets `$F0` bit0 (busy), dispatches `$0110`: `$FF` → `$FE=$BC` (none); bit7 → pause toggle (`$F0` bit5, `$4015`←`$FB`, re-init 4 channels via `$8465/$8388`); `<$20` → SndStartMusic `$80E4`; `≥$20` → SndStartSfx `$8094`. |
| `24:8094` | SndStartSfx | R7←bank **12** (`$0C`); header ptr = `$9FC0+id*2` → for id≥`$20` this lands in `12:$A000+(id−$20)*2` (SFX pointer table lives at start of bank 12). Byte 0 = priority: rejected if > `$0112`. Sets `$FB=$0A` (pulse2+noise?), inits channels 4 and 3 via `$8169`, flags `$0115/$0117` bit7. Ignored if `$F0` bit6. |
| `24:80E4` | SndStartMusic | `$8177` check, `$0111`=id&`$7F`, clears `$FE/$FF`, inits 4 channel streams `$812A` (Y=1,3,5,7 → stream ptr pairs). |
| `24:8186` | SndTick | per-frame playback; exits if `$F0` bit0 set; stops music on `$F0` bit7 (sets `$0111`=`$FF`, `$4015`←`$FB`). |
| `24:8000` | SndOpcodeTable | **Corrected session 3:** this IS a jump table — the stream-byte opcode dispatch (`24:8347` SndReadOp: `op = byte>>4`, `JMP (SndOpcodeTable+op*2)`). See "Sound data format" below. |

Sound RAM: `$0100-$0112` (queue + state), `$0114-$0119` channel flags, `$F0` flags, `$F3`, `$F5/$F6` data ptr, `$FB` `$4015` shadow, `$FD-$FF`. **Port note:** sound can be implemented as a FIFO of ids consumed one per frame; music ids `$00-$1F`, SFX `$20-$9F` (data in bank 12 `$A000`), bank 25 holds tick-time data.

**Sound data format (session 3)** — tables decoded with `tools/decode_tables.py`.

*Channels* (X index, arrays 6 wide at `$0114+X`…): 0=SQ1, 1=SQ2, 2=TRI, 3=NOISE (music); 4=SFX on SQ2, 5=SFX on NOISE. `SndChnTypeTable` `24:87A6` = `00 01 02 03 01 03`, reg offset `87AC` = `0 4 8 C 4 C`, `$4015` mask `87B2`. While an SFX plays, music chn 1/3 get `$0114` bit7 (muted, stream keeps running).

*Headers*
- Music: `SndMusicTable` `24:8C0C` ptr16[32], ids `$00-$1F`; null (ignored) ids `$02 $05 $10`. Header = 1 byte (unused by code, always `01` so far) + 4 stream ptr16 (SQ1, SQ2, TRI, NOISE). Id `$00` (`$8C4C`) = silence: each stream `53 40 00`.
- SFX: `SfxPtrTable` = file bank 12 offset 0, seen at `$A000` via `SetR7(12)` (`SndStartSfx` indexes `$9FC0+id*2`, so id `$20` → entry 0). 88 entries → ids `$20-$77`; null ids (all 88 checked, session 4): `$22 $2D $34 $35 $38 $44 $46 $4D $4F $59 $70 $71 $73 $77`. First header at `$A0B0` = end of the 88-entry table, confirming the count. Header = priority byte (lower = more important; rejected if > `$0112`, `$FF` = idle) + 2 stream ptr16 (SQ2, NOISE). Stream ptrs are stored as `$8xxx` and `+$20` hi (`SndInitSfxChannel` `24:8169`) — data assembled for bank 12's native window.

*Per-channel RAM* (`+X`): `$0114` flags (b0 active, b4 rest/hold, b5 vibrato, b6 envelope, b7 stolen-by-SFX), `$011A/$0120` stream ptr, `$0126/$0132/$013E` loop1 ptr/count, `$012C/$0138/$0144` loop2 ptr/count, `$014A` frames left, `$0150` note length, `$0156` octave×16, `$015C` envelope#<<4 | step, `$0162` vibrato depth, `$0168` `$4000` shadow (duty/vol), `$016E` sweep, `$0174/$017A` period lo/hi. `$0113` = next music id (chain). `$FC` = per-frame reg-dirty bits (b0 vol, b1 sweep, b2 lo, b3 hi). `$FE` = fade timer (`$BC` on cmd `$FF`; decrements volumes via `24:8609`). `$FF` = DMC-pending/`$4015` DMC bit.

*Stream bytes* — `SndReadOp` `24:8347`: opcode = high nibble → `SndOpcodeTable` `24:8000`. Ops are read in a loop until one "ends the frame" (sets `$F2`), then the channel waits `$0150` frames.

| Byte | Handler | Meaning |
|------|---------|---------|
| `0x`/`1x` | `8364` | end of stream. Music: `$0113` = byte&`$1F` → when all 4 channels end, music id `$0113` restarts (≠0 = loop/chain, 0 = stop). SFX: channel released. |
| `2x` | `83E5` | **note** x, ends frame |
| `3x` | `8422` | octave: `$0156` = (x−1)·`$10` (x=1..8) |
| `4x` | `8436` | rest/hold, ends frame; x=0 → channel silenced, else const volume x |
| `50-6F` | `847D` | note length = `SndDurationTable[byte−$50]` (`24:8AB8`: `05 40 38 01 14 02 28 03 50 04 1E 1C 07 2A 13 15 06 08 0C 09 18 0A 30 0B 60 12 24 0D 20 0E 10 0F` frames) |
| `7x` | `848A` | legato pitch change (no retrigger unless hi period changes); x=7 = tie; ends frame |
| `8x` | `8532` | base volume x (skipped during fade) |
| `9x` | `854D` | duty / const-volume bits of `$4000` shadow |
| `A0 lo hi` | `8584` | jump to ptr (also stores return in loop2 slot) |
| `A1 n` / `A2 n` | `8584` | loop1 / loop2 start, count n |
| `A4` / `A3`,`A5+` | `8584` | loop1 end / loop2 end (dec count, jump back if ≠0) |
| `Bx yy` | `83E2` | sweep on (yy) + note x |
| `Cx yy` | `8395` | sweep reg = yy (x bit0: 1=off, 0=on), ends frame |
| `Dx` | `83C2` | note x with octave-dependent detune `$83DA` (`0C 06 04 03 02 02 01 01`); on NOISE chn → DMC drum #x (`SndDmcTable` `24:8BD8`, 4-byte `$4010-$4013` records) |
| `Ex` | `85F7` | envelope #x (`$015C`=x<<4, enables `$0114` b6) |
| `Fx` | `8685` | vibrato depth x·8; x<8 on, x=8 off, x>8 = empty frame |

*Op `9x` exact (`SndOpDuty` `24:854D`, session 4)* — writes the high nibble of the `$0168` reg-0 shadow (low nibble = volume kept), sets `$FC` b0:
- **TRI** (type 2): high nibble = x verbatim → `$4008` bit7 (control/halt) + linear-reload bits 4-6 (`$0168 = x<<4 | vol`).
- **SQ/NOISE**: x bit0 → const-volume bit4; x bits1-2 → duty bits6-7; bit5 (length halt) always set. So `$4000` high nibble = `(x&6)<<5 | (x&1)<<4 | $20`. If x bit0 = 0 (hardware envelope), the software-envelope flag `$0114` b6 is cleared.

*Vibrato tick (`SndVibratoTick` `24:86AA`, session 4) (VERIFIED self-emu, s41: 2463/2463 calls match; the phase used is the incremented one; e=0 reads the `$8724` word)* — called per frame from `24:82F0` when `$0114` b5. Skipped if b7 (SFX-stolen); if software envelope active (b6), waits until env step (`$015C`&`$0F`) = `$F`. `INC $FD`. `$0162` = depth x<<3 | phase(0-7); phase = (phase+1)&7; p = x odd ? phase>>1 : phase, then p&=3 (x odd = half speed). `e = SndVibStepTable[(x>>1)*4 + p]` (`24:8714`: `00 02 00 04 / 00 06 00 08 / 00 0A 00 0C / 00 0E 00 10`); e=0 → offset 0, else **Y = octave×16 + e + 2** and offset = s16 at `SndVibOffsetTable` `24:8724+Y`. Period lo written to `$4002+reg` = (`$0174` + offset) **only if** the hi byte would be unchanged (no hi write, base `$0174/$017A` untouched — non-cumulative).
- Offset table is 8 s16 per octave row starting at `$8726+oct*16`: oct0 `+4 −4 +8 −8 +12 −12 +16 −16`, oct1 `+2 −2 +4 −4 +6 −6 +8 −8`, oct2 `+1 −1 +2 −2 +3 −3 +4 −4`, oct3 `+1 −1 +1 −1 +2 −2 +2 −2`, oct4-7 `+1 −1` repeated.
- **Original off-by-one** (replicate in port): because of `+2` instead of `+0`, phase1 reads the *negative* of pair k and phase3 reads the *positive* of pair k+1 — e.g. depth 0/1, octave 0: cycle `0, −4, 0, +8`. Depth 6/7 phase3 reads the next octave row's first word; at octave 7 that is `$87A6` = `SndChnTypeTable` bytes `00 01` → **+256** (harmless only if unused).

*Pitch*: `SndPeriodLo/Hi` `24:87B8`/`24:8838`, 128 entries indexed `octave*16 + x`. **The note nibble is the note's hex letter**: `C`=C, `D`=D, `E`=E, `F`=F, `A`=A, `B`=B, plus `0`=G, `1`=C♯, `2`=D♯, `4`=F♯, `5`=G♯, `6`=A♯ (`3 7 8 9` = 0, unused). Row 0: C=`$6AC` (65.4 Hz, C2) … B=`$388`; each row = +1 octave. NOISE chn uses x directly as noise period.

*Envelopes*: `$84AF` steps `$015C` through `SndEnvTableTonal` `24:88B8` (noise: `24:89B8`), 16 envelopes × 16 steps; value low nibble = volume, bits6-7 = duty override, bit4 = hold last step (or back up 2 during fade).

*Tick timing + flow exact (session 26)* — `SndTick` `24:8186` runs music chn 0-3 (`SndChannelTick` `24:8276`), then SFX chn 4/5 (R7←12 first) **only if `$FD` < 20** — `$FD` counts stream ops read this frame (`INC $FD` in `SndReadOp`) plus vibrato ticks, so a music frame that reads ≥20 ops delays SFX by one frame.
- Per channel: `DEC $014A` first; only when it hits 0 are ops read (until one sets `$F2`), then `$014A` ← `$0150` (note length; 0 = 256). Music streams start with `$014A`=1, so the first ops play on the first tick. Each note/rest/legato/sweep-reg/end therefore lasts exactly `$0150` frames.
- When all 4 music chn have b0 clear, the music id in `$0113` is restarted (`24:8222`, re-reads the header), 0 → `$0111`=`$FF` (stopped).
- **Flow ops exact** (`SndOpFlow` `24:8584`): `A1 n`/`A2 n` save the address after the count byte + count n (0 = 256); `A4` = `DEC` loop1 count, jump back if ≠0 (body plays n times); `A3`/`A5-AF` = same on loop2 (X+6 slots). `A0 lo hi` = **call**: return address (after the 3 bytes) goes into the *loop2* ptr slot and the loop2 count is set to the stream offset+2 (always ≥2), so a later `A3` with count≠1 after `DEC` returns there. A0 and loop2 share state: a call inside an `A2` loop overwrites it. SFX (X≥4) add `$20` to the target hi byte (`ADC #$1F` with C=1).
- **Octave op quirk** (`24:8422`): reads `$842D+x` — x=1..8 → `$00..$70`; x=0 reads the RTS byte `$60` (octave 7); x≥9 reads code bytes (`E6 F2 BD 14 01 09 10`).
- **Note** (`2x`/`Bx`/`Dx`): period = `SndPeriodLo/Hi[oct*16+x]` (+detune `$83DA[oct]` added to lo with carry for `Dx`), `$FC` |= `$0C` → lo + hi written; the hi write (`$8331`) resets env step (`$015C` &= `$F0`), vibrato phase (`$0162` &= `$F8`) and clears the rest flag b4. NOISE chn: period = x, hi = 0 (`JSR $8651`).
- **Rest/hold** (`4x` at `24:8436`): sets b4 (envelope + vibrato frozen). x=0 → `$4015` channel bit cleared (`24:8465`, keeps DMC bit; skipped while stolen). x≠0 → `$4000` = (`$0168`&`$F0`)|x written immediately — except SQ2/NOISE music while an SFX holds `$FB`≠0.
- **Legato** (`7x`, x≠7): new period; hi written (= retrigger + env reset) only if the hi byte changed.
- **Envelope tick** (`24:84AF`, per frame unless b4/b7): reads `SndEnvTable[$015C]` (tonal `24:88B8` / noise `24:89B8`), then step++ unless step = `$F`. Entry bit4 = **end marker**: freezes (step ← `$F`, nothing written); during a fade (`$FE`≠0) it backs up 2 steps and re-reads instead (sustain loop). Otherwise vol = **`SndVolumeTable` `24:8AD8`[base_vol·16 + env_vol]** (16×16 volume-scaling table, ends at `SndDmcTable` `$8BD8`); `$4000` = (entry bits6-7 duty if ≠0, + `$0168`&`$30`) or (`$0168`&`$F0`), \| vol.
- **`SndVolumeTable` `24:8AD8`** = `max(env + base − 15, 1)` when env>0 and base>0, else 0 (row = base vol, column = env vol).
- **Envelope op** (`Ex`, `24:85F7`): `$015C` = x<<4 (step 0), sets b6. **Vibrato op** (`Fx`, `24:8685`) always stores `$0162` = x·8 first; x<8 → b5 on, x=8 → b5 off, **x≥9 → ends the frame (1 note-length wait) but leaves `$0162` = x·8**, so with vibrato still on the next ticks index `SndVibStepTable` out of range (quirk; port reads the same bytes).
- **Drums / DMC** (`Dx` on music NOISE, `24:863B`): x=0 → `$FF`=0 + `$4013`=0 (drums off); x≠0 → `$FF` = `$10`\|x = **drum x armed**. Every later NOISE note (`2x`, `24:8651`) while `$FF`&`$0F`≠0 re-triggers DMC record x: `$4013`=0, `$4015`=`$0F`, then `$4010-$4013` ← `SndDmcTable[x]`; the channel tick writes `$4015` = `$FF`\|`$0F` (keeps DMC enabled). SFX NOISE (X=5) ignores drums. `SndDmcTable` `24:8BD8` (13 × 4 bytes, record 0 unreachable): rates `0F 0F 0E 0D 0C 0B 0E 06 08 09 0A 0B 0C`, `$4011` = 0 (rec 0-7) / `$2A` (rec 8-12), all `$4012`=`$E8` `$4013`=`$3E` → **the only DMC sample is `31:FA00-FDE0`** (993 bytes = `$3E`·16+1, address `$C000`+`$E8`·64); the drum kit is that one sample at different pitches. This explains the `31:FDE0` boundary of the stale-copy filler.
- **Fade**: `24:8609` per step: TRI is cut immediately (`$0114`/`$0168` ← 0), other active music chn base vol −1 (min 0).
- **Fade quirk**: `$FE` is decremented once per *channel tick* (4-6×/frame), `$FE&$3F`=0 → `24:8609` steps volumes down and `$FE`←`$BC`.

*Ids used by PlaySound callers* (`search_bytes.py "A9 ?? 20 25 C1" --disasm`, 67 immediate sites):
- Music: `$00` (19:B91C, silence), `$0C` (15:B310), `$12` ×8 (bank 15: A4B7 A68F A99A ACB0 AE0E AF82 B186), `$80` (15:BAAE → forced `$00` = stop), `$93` ×3 (19:BC38, 30:D0A6, 30:D42D → forced `$13`).
- SFX: `$21 $23×2 $24 $25×4 $26 $29×7 $2F $31×3 $36×2 $39×3 $3A $3D×2 $3E $3F×2 $43 $44 $45 $48 $4B×2 $4E×2 $54 $56 $5C $5D $5F $64 $66 $68 $6B×2 $6E $6F $76` (`$29` is the most common — 15:BAF3, 19:BFDA, 27:BB32, 30:D0AE/D0C8/D1CA/D48F). Note `$44` is a null SFX entry (26:92D6 → silently ignored).
- The other ~18 PlaySound callers load A from RAM/tables (not enumerated).

*Music ids by caller (session 4)* — headers: `$0C` `24:9F4B`, `$12` `25:A5DB`, `$13` `25:A796` (all byte0 `01`).
- **`$13` = interrupting jingle** ("fanfare"). Always issued as `$93` right after the caller sets `snd_cur_music` (`$0102`) bit7, so when `$13` ends (every stream ends `00` = stop, ~150 frames ≈ 2.5 s; SQ1: `93 8F E9 60 34 2D 5C 2E 2F 54 20 65 2A 50 41 20 54 2A 56 77 53 40 00`) `SoundUpdate` re-issues the previous track. Played *instead of* an SFX on a special case: `30:D090` if `$06C3=$0D && $06E3=$20` (else SFX `$29`); `30:D41C` if `$07DC&$F0=$40` (else SFX `$3F`); also `19:BC38`. Likely an item/achievement jingle — confirm when `$06C3/$06E3/$07DC` are named.
- **`$12` = entity-triggered track.** 8 sites in bank 15 (n=7, entity logic), each an entity handler doing `STA $05A0` (value `$00`/`$10`) then, only if `$07D5≠0`, `PlaySound $12`. Uses loops (`A1`/`A4`) → a full looping piece; likely boss/encounter music (one per boss-type entity).
- **`$0C`** — single site `15:B310`, entity handler that first far-calls bank 14 `$800E`, sets `$07E8=$38`, calls `15:8C3D` (A=`$10`); a scene/event theme. Semantics TBD with entity tracing.

**Port note:** this is enough to write a JS sequencer: per channel, read ops until a frame-ending op, count down `SndDurationTable` frames, apply envelope table steps per frame, map note nibble via period table to APU square/triangle/noise.

**Main loop (`30:C900`) (VERIFIED self-emu, s41; state sequence 2→3→0→1 and sub_states 8/1/9/`$12`/3 VERIFIED vs FCEUX s91)** — spins on `$09` (set by NMI), then dispatches `MainStateTable[$40]` (`30:C91F`) through the ZP trampoline `$10: JMP abs`, clears `$09`, repeats. States: 0=`C957` 1=`CAB6` 2=`C931` (initial) 3=`C939` 4=`C944` 5=`C94C` — **corrected s63: the table has 9 entries**, 6/7 = `$0000` (null, `30:C92B/C92D`) and **8 = `C9DA`** (`30:C92F`, continue from save). Names and setters: "Main states (session 63)" under State Machine.

**NMI (`31:F3B6`) (VERIFIED self-emu, s41)** — returns immediately if `$60≠0`, or if `$06≠0` (lag: sets `$07`). Else, **only if `frame_ready` `$09`=0** (the main loop finished its frame), OAM DMA from `$0200` and copy `$02-$05` → `$07D8-$07DB`; with `irq_mode` `$51` = 7/9 the copy instead takes **object slot `$13`'s position** (`$83/$A3/$C3/$E3` = x lo/hi, y lo/hi), so the split follows that object. Then always: `JSR $C67D` (PPU queue), `PaletteUpload` `F8CB`, PPUMASK ← `$01`, `JSR $F6AD` (NMI-mode routine `JMP (JmpTable31_F6C2[$51])`), `INC $09`. In the s41 run, 14 of 2457 NMIs hit with `$09`≠0 and skipped the DMA (the previous frame's OAM is shown again).

**NMI tail in full (session 64, static).** Order every non-skipped NMI: `PHA`, read PPUSTATUS (resets the `$2005/$2006` latch) → `$60`/`$06` checks → save X/Y → [DMA + `$07D8-DB` copy] → `PpuQueueFlush` → `PaletteUpload` (X=0) → PPUMASK ← `ppumask_shadow` → `NmiModeDispatch` → `INC frame_ready` → restore, `RTI` (shares the `RTI` at `F421` = `IrqDefault`).
- **`PpuQueueFlush` `30:C67D`** drains the ring `ppu_queue` `$6200` from read index `$0A` to write index `$0B` (both step 4, `& $1F` → 8 records). Record = `hi, lo, len, src`: `hi` b6 set → PPUCTRL b2 (+32, vertical column); `hi`/`lo` → PPUADDR (b7/b6 are ignored by the PPU's 14-bit address). `len` b7 **clear** → copy `len` bytes from `wram_buf` `$6000+src` (4× unrolled loop; `len`=0 copies 256); `len` b7 **set** → **attribute path `C6E7`**: 64 bytes from `attr_shadow` `$6100+src` (8 groups of 8, `src` += 8; `len`'s low bits are not used). After each record `src` is overwritten with `$FF` (consumed marker) and `$0A` advances. `hi` b7 set → **chain**: flush the next record in the same NMI; b7 clear → stop after this record, so without the chain bit **only one record goes out per NMI** (the attribute path never chains). `WaitPpuQueue` `30:C676` spins until `$0A`=`$0B`.
- **`NmiModeDispatch` `31:F6AD`** clears `$57` (the handler-0 latch) and jumps through `JmpTable31_F6C2[irq_mode·2]`. `ASL` drops b7, so `irq_mode` b7 (`$80`/`$86`, set by `PauseScreen` `15:BC4D`, bank 16 menu `16:80AB/87A0/8823`, `31:E4AB`) only **suppresses `PaletteUpload`** (`F8CB` returns early) and keeps the low mode. Mode 8 = `$0000` → **`JMP ($0000)` crash if `irq_mode`=8** (never set). `STA $C000` in these routines is the MMC3 IRQ latch (dis.py shows the bank-30 label `BitMask`).

| `irq_mode` | routine | scroll / CHR | IRQ armed (handler, latch) | sound | setters |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0, 7 | `NmiMode0` `F6D6` | `NmiScrollSet` (scroll from `$07D8-DB`, `$07DB` forced to 1 when `$07DA` ≥ `$E8`) + `ApplyChrPlay` `$07F0-F5` | rendering off (`$01`&`$18`=0) → none, `JMP SoundUpdate`. Else H1 at `$BE` if the NT-Y bit (`$00` b1) is set or `$EF`−`$07DA` ≥ `$BC`; otherwise H0 at `$EF`−`$07DA` (the vertical-wrap line; H0 re-arms H1 at **`$BC`**−`$57`, corrected s65 — the 2 lines make up for the counter reload, so the HUD still starts at line `$BE` = 190) | only when rendering off (else H1's `F4CF`) | 0: area load, menu/message close and other resets (~25 `LDA #0` sites); 7: bosses/Bow of Truth `15:A885 B2F1`, `27:A416` (scroll follows slot `$13`, see above) |
| 1, 9 | `NmiMode1` `F731` | same as mode 0 | **H2 `F4E5`** at latch ((`$07DA`&7) ^ 7)+7 = 14 − fine Y | none here (H2 chain) | 1: `ShowMessage` `30:D367`, `30:D8A3 DBAA DFC7`, `16:9535`; 9 = `ShowMessage` `30:D371` when the mode was 7 |
| 2 | `NmiMode2` `F751` | PPUCTRL = shadow with NT 2 (`$2800`), scroll 0,0, `ApplyChrAlt` `$58-5D` | rendering on → `NmiMode0HudIrq` (H1 at `$BE`) | rendering off → `JMP SoundUpdate` | pause/status screen `15:BCD2 BD77`, `30:CF2A`, `27:BCE8` |
| 3 | `NmiMode3` `F771` | PPUMASK, `PpuScrollWrite`, `ApplyChrPlay` | **IRQ off** | always `JMP SoundUpdate` | reset `F2E5`, title `18:9FD7` (+ copies `20:/22:9FD7`), `19:ABBC`, ending `17:A01D` |
| 4 | `NmiMode4` `F782` | `PpuScrollWrite`, `ApplyChrPlay`; R1 alt = `NmiMode4R1Table` `F7B5[$58]` (`00 1C×7 17 15 12 11 11 0E 0E 0D`), R2 alt = 0 | `$58`≠0 → **H4 `F5E1`** at line 4 (also `STA $F451` = odd `$E000-$FFFF` → a stray MMC3 IRQ-enable write; probably meant `STA $57`). `$58`=0 → BG off (`$01` &= `$F7`), IRQ off | none in the NMI; **corrected s65:** the last H4 pass falls into H5 `F60E`, which calls `SoundUpdate` | **only `31:FE01`/`FE18`, which are dead**: `ShopWalkIn` `30:DA0C` calls `$FE00` and `ShopWalkOut` `30:DA72` calls `$FE17`, both a bare `RTS` sitting right before the bodies. The bodies step `$58` `$0B`→6 and 6→1 (4 frames each, `30:C176` = wait Y frames) = a **disabled shop-door/curtain effect** |
| 5 | `NmiMode5` `F7C5` | `PpuScrollWrite`, `ApplyChrPlay` | **H6 `F691`** at latch `$58` | none (H6 chain) | cold-boot intro `18:803A` |
| 6 | `NmiMode6` `F7DB` | PPUCTRL = shadow, scroll 0,0, `ApplyChrAlt` | **H7 `F61A`** at `$77` (line 119); `$57`=`$77` | none (H7 chain) | equipment/item menu `16:8034 80CC` |

Handlers 3 `F54B` and 5 `F60E` are not armed by any NMI mode, so they can only be reached as a chain from another handler (see "IRQ handlers in full" below). The game never runs `SoundUpdate` from the NMI while the HUD split is on; that is why it ticks twice on the frame an area load turns rendering off (Verification Log, s42).

**IRQ (`31:F443`)** — ack/re-enable MMC3 IRQ, `JMP ($0054)`. Handler table `IrqHandlerTable` at `31:F433` (8 entries) selected by `SetIrqHandler` (`31:F424`, A=index). Handler 0 (`F455`) does a delayed mid-frame scroll split from `$07D8`. **(VERIFIED self-emu, s41)** — every IRQ dispatch went to a table entry. **Handler 1 `F488` is the gameplay HUD split** (line 190 in `tools.emu`): `IRQOFF`, delay `$07FE`+3, PPUMASK ← (`$01`&`$0E`)\|`$08` (sprites off), PPUADDR `$2B00` + scroll X 0 (nametable 2's bottom = HUD, matching `HudRedraw` `$2B39…`), CHR R0=`$3C` R1=`$38` (HUD font set), then `SoundUpdate` (with `$06`=1) and the missed NMI-mode routine if `$07`≠0. The title uses handler 6 `F691` (line 137).

**IRQ handlers in full (session 65, static).** Every handler starts with `DelayX` (`$07FE` `irq_delay`+1 loops) so its PPU writes land in hblank, and ends with `PLA×3`/`RTI`. Splits use **`$2006` writes only** (plus one `$2005` for fine X), so coarse X/Y and the nametable come from the address. A `$2xxx` address also sets fine Y to 2 (address bit 13 → `t` bit 13), which is ordinary 2C02 behaviour that the emulator must reproduce. `$57` is the running count of lines already used this frame; each handler subtracts it from its absolute target line. **Nametable plan this implies:** the playfield is NT0 only. NT2 holds the message window (rows 0-8, `$2800`) and the HUD (rows 24-29, `$2B00`). The PPU's own vertical wrap past row 29 would land in NT2, which is why H0 exists.

| # | addr | name (new) | what it does at the split line | next IRQ armed | armed by |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `F455` | `IrqHandler0` (wrap) | PPUADDR `$2000 + (scroll_x>>3)` = NT0 row 0 at the current coarse X, `$2005` ← `$07D8` (fine X). The playfield's vertical wrap | H1 at `$BC`−`$57` (HUD, line 190) | `NmiMode0` (latch `$EF`−`$07DA`), H3 |
| 1 | `F488` | `IrqHandler1Hud` | HUD: sprites off, NT2 `$2B00`, X 0, CHR R0/R1 = `$3C/$38`, `SoundUpdate` + missed NMI mode (s41) | none (IRQ off) — end of every chain except 4/6 | `NmiMode0HudIrq` (modes 0/2/7), H0, H3, H7 |
| 2 | `F4E5` | `IrqMsgWindowTop` | 5 `NOP`s more delay, PPUADDR `$2800` (NT2 row 0), X 0, `ApplyChrAlt` (`$58-$5D` = message font set, e.g. `$3C/$38` from `ShowMessage` `30:D356`) | **H3 after `$48`** (72 lines = 9 tile rows), `$57` += `$46`. Precomputes the resume address: row = ((`$07DA`+8+`$57`) wrapped at 240) >> 3 → `$5E/$5F` = `NtRowAddrTable[row]` \| (`$07D8`>>3); then `$57` += 8 | `NmiMode1` (modes 1/9) at latch 14 − fine Y (the first tile-row boundary ≥ line 7) |
| 3 | `F54B` | `IrqMsgWindowEnd` | PPUADDR ← `$5E/$5F` (the NT0 row the playfield would show at this line), `$2005` ← `$07D8`, `ApplyChrPlay` (back to the play CHR) | wrap line W = `$EF`−`$07DA`: if W < `$BC` and W ≥ `$57` → H0 at W−`$57` (`$57` = W); else H1 at `$BE`−`$57` | H2 only |
| 4 | `F5E1` | `IrqRowStretch` (dead) | PPUADDR ← `NtRowAddrTable[$5A]`, `$5A`++ (next NT0 row), `$59`-- | while `$59` ≥ 0: itself again after latch `$58` (one row every `$58`+1 lines); then falls into H5 | `NmiMode4` (mode 4, only from the dead `31:FE01/FE18`) |
| 5 | `F60E` | `IrqOffSound` | `IRQOFF`, `SoundUpdate` | none | never by `SetIrqHandler`; only H4's `BMI` at `F5FE` (the table entry is unused) |
| 6 | `F691` | `IrqHandler6` (intro) | `IRQOFF`, PPUADDR ← `$59`:`$5A` | none | `NmiMode5`, latch `$58`. Cold-boot intro `18:803E-8046`: `$58`=`$89`, `$59`=`$28`, `$5A`=0 → **at line 137 switch to NT2 row 0** (matches s41's line 137) |
| 7 | `F61A` | `IrqMenuChrSplit` | CHR R0 ← `$5E`, R1 ← `$5F` (BG pattern switch, no scroll change) | H1 at `$BC`−`$57` (HUD) | `NmiMode6` (`$57`=`$77`, line 119). Equipment menu `16:8026/802A`: `$5E/$5F` = `$3C/$38` → the lower part uses the HUD font set, the top uses the alt set `$58-$5D`. Web: `mainloop.js` `irqMenuChrSplit` (s98) |

`NtRowAddrTable` `31:F5A1` = 32 words `$2000, $2020 … $23E0` (NT0 row addresses, rows 30/31 = attribute area, never indexed by H2 because the row is < 30). H2 reads it at `F5A2,Y`/`F5A1,Y` (hi/lo), H4 at the same pair with Y = `$5A`·2.

**Which screen uses which chain:** gameplay = M0: [H0 →] H1. Message box over gameplay = M1/9: H2 → H3 → [H0 →] H1. Pause/status = M2: whole screen NT2 + alt CHR → H1. Title/menus/ending = M3: no IRQ. Cold-boot intro = M5: H6. Equipment menu = M6: H7 → H1. Dead shop-door effect = M4: H4×n → H5. **H4 effect, corrected reading of the s64 note:** `$58` is the line interval and `NmiMode4R1Table[$58]` is the row count (`$58`=7 → 28 rows × 8 lines = normal picture; > 7 stretches, < 7 squashes, 0 = BG off). The bodies step `$58` `$0B`→6 (unsquash) and 6→1 (squash), so it is a **vertical squash/stretch transition**, not a CHR curtain (`$59`/`$5A` are row count/row index here, not CHR banks).

**Object sprite pass (`28:800E` ObjSpriteDraw, session 5) (VERIFIED self-emu, s39/s41)** — reached via `SwitchBank16(14)` from `C1B4/CB71/E4CD` and far-call `FF8C`.
1. `$07FF` (obj_draw_rot) `DEC`; order = `ObjDrawOrderTable` `28:83DC + ObjDrawOrderOfs[$07FF&3]` — **4 fixed 32-slot permutations** (not a simple rotation): `04 05 06 07 02 01 03 08…1F 00`, a shuffled one, another, and `02 01 1F 1E … 03 00`. Slot 0 is drawn last in 3 of 4; slots 1/2 near the front.
2. OAM write ptr `$10` starts at 4 (sprite 0 reserved for split). For each slot X with `$04A0,X≠0`: sx = (`$70`,`$90`) − (`$02`,`$03`), hi must be 0. Store `$05C0`. sy = (`$B0`,`$D0`) − (`$04`,`$05`) with 240-row wrap (extra −16 on borrow), hi must be 0; sy ≥ `$E8` → skipped silently; sy < `$10` or off-page → `$0380` b7 set (offscreen). Else `$05E0`=sy, b7 cleared, draw unless OAM full or (b2 half-rate and (X+`$07FF`) odd).
3. `ObjHideUnusedOam` `80A5`: Y=`$F0` from `$10` to end (nothing hidden when `$10` wrapped to 0 = OAM full). If `$0710` b7: clears palette bits of obj 1's 6 sprites (4 if `$0381` b6) from `$07EF` → **hit flash for obj 1 = player**.
4. `OrbitSprites` `80D6`: 8 sprites in OAM `$E0-$FC` (attr 1) centred on obj 1. `$06C1≠0`: burst — timer decrements, drawn while <`$0E`, tile `$29`, rays from `BurstDirTable` `81DE` × `BurstRadiusTable` `81FE`. Else if `$06C0≥8`: orbit — 8 points on `OrbitSineTable` `81BA` (32 s8, radius ≈`$21`) phase `$08`+k·8, tile `OrbitTileTable[$06C0>>3]` (`29 29 2A 32 20`). Points that wrap horizontally are skipped.

**Metasprite drawing (`ObjDrawMeta` `822E`)**
- Y origin `$13` = sy (+3 if `$0380` b6). If `$0380` b1: first draws overlay id `$0580,X` (temporarily `$0300`←`$0580`, b0 set, b5 clear), then the main sprite.
- id = `$0300,X` + (`$0380` b0 ? 0 : `$0360,X`). id `<$80` → `ObjMetaPtrTableA` `28:845C`, else `ObjMetaPtrTableB` `28:855C` (ptr16, targets up to `$BFxx` → bank 29). `X==1` records `$07EF`=OAM offset.
- tile add `$1A` = `$0320,X` + (`$0380` b5 ? `$40`:0); attr OR = `$0380` b4 → bit5 (behind BG).

*Metasprite record format* (all in banks 28/29):
```
FF lo hi            redirect: draw record at hi:lo HORIZONTALLY MIRRORED (x' = (~dx)-8 = -dx-9, attr ^= $40; s54, emu-checked)
                    (any byte >= $80 redirects, chains are followed; meta id $9A's table pointer is $0000 -> RAM)
00                  nothing drawn
N  M  {frames}      N = sprites per frame (1..), M = frame mask
                    frame f = ($04E0,X >> 3) & M  (anim advances every 8 ticks)
                    frame data at +2 + f*N*4; each sprite = dx(s8) dy(s8) attr tile
                    dx = $80 → end of frame early (frames padded with 80 80 80 80)
                    attr bit2 (unused by PPU) = "lower part": if $0380 b6, drawing STOPS at it
```
Example id 2 (`$865C`): `06 01` → 6 sprites, 2 frames; 16×24 body: `F8 E9 00 1A / 00 E9 00 1B / F8 F1 00 00 / 00 F1 00 01 / F8 F9 04 20 / 00 F9 04 21` (bottom row flagged b2 → hidden when sinking); frame 1 is the same body with attr `$40` (self-mirrored) rows. Id 0 (`$868E`) uses tiles `1E 1F 06 07 26 27`. Id 3 (`$86F2`) = `FF C0 86` → mirrored id 1 — facing variants are `FF` aliases.

Object RAM (32 slots, index X 0-31): `$70/$90` X lo/hi, `$B0/$D0` Y lo/hi, `$0300` meta id, `$0320` tile ofs, `$0340` b7 (palette-cycle, read but **unused** — `$1D` is computed and never read: dead code), `$0360` dir/anim id add, `$0380` flags (b0 no-dir, b1 overlay, b2 half-rate flicker, b4 behind BG, b5 tile+$40, b6 sink/half-hide, b7 offscreen out), `$04A0` active, `$04E0` anim counter, `$0580` overlay id, `$05C0/$05E0` screen X/Y out. **Obj 1 = player** (hit flash, orbit centre).

**Sprite pass port details (session 54, `web/src/game/obj/sprites.js`; VERIFIED tools.emu: 552/552 whole-RAM cases, `web/test/sprite_check.mjs`)** — byte-exact behaviour the summary above leaves out:
- **x wrap check writes first:** each entry's OAM x (`dx + $12`) is stored *before* the wrap test (`ROR; EOR dx; BMI`). A skipped entry leaves that x byte at the current OAM slot; the next entry overwrites it, or it stays when it was the object's last one (only y is reset by the hide pass).
- **Sink stop:** an attr-b2 entry of a sinking object (`$0380` b6) writes y and attr, then ends the object without writing the tile or advancing `$10`.
- **OAM full:** `$10` wraps to 0 inside an object → that object stops, every later object is skipped (`LDA $10; BEQ`), the overlay's main sprite too, and the hide pass hides nothing. The `$17` count is not decremented on the wrapping entry.
- **Frame offset** = ((N·f) mod 256)·4 (8-bit shift-add over the low 4 bits of f = (`$04E0`>>3) & M); bits 4+ of f are ignored.
- **`$07EF`** is written by every `ObjDrawMetaMain` call for slot 1, so with an overlay it ends at the main sprite's offset (the overlay is not flashed).
- **sy:** −16 on borrow (`SBC #$0F` with C=0); sy ≥ `$E8` returns before `$0380` b7 is touched (the bit keeps its old value); the +3 sink offset is applied to `$13` only (not `$05E0`).
- **Orbit ring:** pairs of points k (7…0) share phase `((k&6)·4 + $08) & $1F`, even k negated; a point whose x wraps writes nothing (y not checked); y = sine[(phase+2)&$1F] + `$05E1` − 12; tile index `$06C0>>3` is not clamped (values ≥ `$28` read past `OrbitTileTable`). `$10` ends `$FF`.
- **Burst:** `$06C1` is decremented first and the *new* value indexes `BurstRadiusTable` (`BurstDirTable[k·4] | $06C1`); drawn while the old value < `$0E`. The x byte is written before the y wrap check, so a point with a good x and a wrapped y leaves x only.
- **Meta id `$9A`** has table pointer `$0000` (`tools/meta_ptr_check.py`: the only id whose chain leaves banks 28/29), so drawing it reads a "record" from zero page. The port reads RAM there too. `sprite_capture.py fuzz` avoids it because random zero-page bytes then redirect into WRAM/fixed banks.

**Sprite CHR banks (session 6)** — PPUCTRL `$88` → 8×8 sprites from `$1000` = MMC3 R2-R5 (1KB each; tile t uses R[2 + t>>6]). Two shadow sets: `$07F0-$07F5` (play field, `ApplyChrPlay` `31:F883`, called from IRQ-side code `F779/F785/F7C8`) and `$58-$5D` (`ApplyChrAlt` `31:F64F`, inside an IRQ handler). In-game writers:
- R2 `$07F2` = `$40`, or `$41` if `$0713`≠0 (`30:C0B8`) → **player tiles**. Other scenes: `$64`/`$4D` (`30:C100/C107`), `$73` (`30:DCDF`), `$5C` (`30:DD7F`, also `$5A`), plus 9 sites in bank 19 and `17:A935`.
- R3 `$07F3` = `$41` + clamp(`$0711`, 1..5) = `$42-$46` (`30:C0C3`; clamped value also → `$07E5`); `$42` at `30:C114`.
- R4/R5 `$07F4/$07F5` = per-area: `LoadAreaChrRecord` `31:E16D` reads a record via `($10)` (ptr from `$9300,Y` table): byte0 must be 0, then `$07E6`, `$07E7`, R4, R5, … Also `$FF/$51` at `31:E832`, `$4D` at `30:DF44`, and bank 15 entity handlers (8 sites, same handlers as the music `$12` sites → boss CHR swap).

**`tools/meta_dump.py` (session 6)** — `text [ids]` prints decoded records (FF-mirror resolved); `png [out] [--chr R2,R3,R4,R5] [--cell N] [--frames N]` renders all 256 ids in a 16×16 grid (frames side by side, attr palette tinted, origin pixel marked) → `gfx/metasprites.png`. Default `--chr 40,42,50,51`. Verified: ids `00-07` (player: 4 facings + 4 sword/attack poses) render clean with R2=`$40`; ids using tiles ≥`$80` need the area's R4/R5 (the `50,51` default is a placeholder, so those look scrambled). Only 4 of the 256 ids are empty. Next: decode the `$9300` area table so each id range can be rendered with its real R4/R5.

**Per-area sprite sets (session 25, `meta_dump.py sprites`)**
- **Spawn → first metasprite.** Each spawn entry maps to a type through its handler (h0 p+`$50`, h1 bank 4/5 record, h2 `$0D/$0E`, h3 p+`$D0`, h4 p). The type's `ObjTypeTable` record then gives `$0300` (meta id), `$0320` (tile add) and `$0380`.
  - For handler 1 type `$30` (NPCs), the meta id is the record's `$23` and the tile add is `$22&$70`.
  - The spawn's b2 b7, or `$0380` b5, adds `$40` to every tile.
- **Final tile = (tile + `$0320` + b5·`$40`) & `$FF`.** The CHR register is chosen from the *final* tile (`R[2 + t>>6]`). So a tile add of `$40` moves an NPC's tiles from R4 to R5, and `$C0` (types `$32/$33`) wraps them down into R3/R4.
- **Almost every enemy type has `$0380` b0 set** (no `$0360` direction add). The record's `$0300` is only the *starting* id; the AI rewrites `$0300` for facing and poses. The sheets therefore show the spawn frame, not every pose.
- **Invisible objects:** 229 of the 711 spawned objects use an empty id, mostly `$4C` (`9163` = `00`; types `$27-$2C`, `$4D`, `$5E`, `$70`, `$D0-$D2` …). These are triggers, controllers and spawners that draw nothing until their AI sets an id. 77 distinct (meta, tile add) pairs are visible at spawn.
- **R4/R5 = `$FF` check:** no spawned object's initial frames read a CHR register holding `$FF`. This supports the session-8 guess that `$FF` marks an unused register, but only for spawn frames; AI pose changes aren't covered.
- **Animation timing is per AI, not global.** `ObjDrawMeta` picks frame (`$04E0`>>3)&M, but nothing in the engine ticks `$04E0` for all objects. About 189 `INC/DEC/STA $04E0,X` sites in banks 15/27 (plus `19:BCF1`, `26:9215/9BB0`, `30:C35B/D7F4`, `31:E805`) step it inside each AI handler. Most use `DEC`, so the frame changes every 8 handler calls, and some handlers step it twice per frame (e.g. `27:A6A8/A6AB`, `27:B422-B428` three times), which makes their animation 2-3× faster. Per-type rates belong to "gameplay 3/3".
- **Palettes used for the sheets:** sprite ids `[0, 1, e6, e7]` (sword 1 at game start; see "Sprite palettes 0/1"), colour `$FF` → the area's backdrop. Flag-item areas use the `($6C&7)+9` override for e6/e7. CHR = `[$40, $42, R4, R5]`.

**Area records (session 7)** — `AreaLoad` `31:E14A` (entries `AreaLoadA` `E144` sets `$18`=1, `AreaLoadB` `E148` sets `$18`=0; callers `30:D1C0/D1DC/D6EC` and JMP tails `30:D199/D21A/31:E6FF`; `$18` not read in this routine). `SwitchBank16(6)` → banks 12/13, then ptr = `AreaPtrTable` `12:9201`[`$6C`] (one ptr16 table — the `$9301` load is the same table's upper half, chosen by the carry from `ASL $6C`). **`$6C` = current area id**; table has **252 entries** (ends `$93F9`, where area `$3C`'s record starts). Ptr hi bit7 clear → no record (`$0000` nulls, e.g. areas 11, 13, 18, 19). `LoadAreaChrRecord` `E16D` is the record body:
```
00                       must be 0 (else whole record ignored)
e6 e7                    -> $07E6, $07E7 = sprite sub-palette 2/3 ids (session 8; flag-item handler overrides both with ($6C&7)+9)
R4 R5                    -> $07F4/$07F5 sprite CHR (1KB banks for tiles $80-$FF); $FF = CHR bank 127, not "keep" (session 8)
{b0 b1 b2 b3}*           one 4-byte entry per object slot, X starting at $0D (slots $0D-$1F)
FF                       end
```
- `FE` as b0 → slot skipped (4 bytes). b1 bit7 → slot skipped (`E19D` ASL/BCC).
- **Position:** Y = b0·16 + 12 (`$D0`=b0>>4, `$B0`=b0<<4|`$0C`); X = (b1&`$7F`)·16 + 8 + (b2 b6 ? 8 : 0) (`$90`=(b1>>4)&7). 16-px cell grid, up to 8 screens wide/16 high.
- **b2**: bits0-2 = handler index (`SpawnHandlerTable` `31:E218`, 5 valid entries — entry 4 = `$E222`, the code right after the table; bytes beyond are code, so values 5-7 are invalid); bits0-1 also → `$0540,X`; b6 = x+8; b7 → `$0380` b5 (tile+`$40`, via `E3C7`).
- **b3** = param `$2F` → `$0560,X`.
- Every handler calls `ObjInit` `30:C25D` (slot `$10`=X, type `$11`; type 1 = no-op) then common tail `SpawnCommonTail` `E28E`.

| h | Addr | Spawn |
|---|------|-------|
| 0 | `E27F` | type = p+`$50` |
| 1 | `E2B5` | indirect: `SwitchBank16(2)` (banks 4/5), 4-byte rec at `$8000 + (p>>2)·$100 + $F0 + (p&3)·4` → `$20-$23`. If `$20`=`$FF`: type=`$21`. Else type `$30`, `$0680`=`$20`, `$06A0`=`$21`, `$22` b7 → `$04A0`/`$06E0`=`$22&$7F`, else `$06E0`=`$22`, `$0320`=`$22&$70`; `$22` b3 → y\|=`$0E`; `$0300`=`$06C0`=`$23`; `$0600` from `E35F[p&3]` unless `$06E0` b2. Then bank 14 `$80A0` with `$23`=p |
| 2 | `E375` | p bit7 → type `$0E`; else type `$0D` + bank 14 `$85BF` (`$23`=p); p ∈ {`$25 $3B $3C`} → meta id `$4C` |
| 3 | `E2A3` | type = p+`$D0` |
| 4 | `E222` | type = p; if p&`$F0`=`$40` (**flag item**): `$07E6/$07E7`=(`$6C`&7)+9; in areas `$C2-$C4` (flag `$84`) / `$F5-$F7` (flag `$27`) if WRAM bit `$6480[f>>3] & BitMask[f&7]` set → p=`$4E` (already-collected replacement). `BitMask` = `30:C000` (`01 02 … 80`) |

**Palette ids `$07E0-$07E7` (session 8) (VERIFIED self-emu, s39)** — `$07E6/$07E7` are **sprite sub-palettes 2 and 3** (the enemy palettes). `$07E0-$07E7` = 8 palette ids (BG 0-3, sprite 0-3). `PaletteBuild` `26:8C0E` (sets fade `$11`=0, i.e. full brightness; 18 callers in banks 26/27/30/31; `8C10` is an A=fade-step entry with no direct xrefs) loops X=0..$1F step 4, calls `PaletteLoadOne` `26:8C6E` with A=`$07E0[X>>2]`:
- id p (+`$B0` if a sprite palette, X≥`$10`) → `SetR7(2 + (p>>7))` (banks 2/3 at `$A000`), source = `$A000 + ((p>>2)&$1F)·$100 + $F0 + (p&3)·4`: **4-byte palette records live in the last 16 bytes of every 256-byte page of banks 2/3** (**256 slots**, not 512: slot s = bank 2+(s>>7), page (s>>2)&$1F, record s&3; same page-tail layout as the bank 4/5 spawn records of handler 1). The sprite offset is `CLC/ADC #$B0` (8-bit), so sprite id p → slot (p+`$B0`)&`$FF`, and sprite ids `$50-$FF` alias BG slots `$00-$4F` (session 23).
- **Palette dump (session 23, `tools/palette_dump.py` → `gfx/palettes.png/.json`):** all 256 slots decode cleanly. Every non-`$FF` byte is ≤ `$3F` (no out-of-range values). There are 144 `$FF` (backdrop) bytes, and 17 slots are entirely `$FF` (`9F A5-AD CB F4-F7 FB FC`). Sprite slots `$B0+` mostly start with `$FF`: colour 0 of a sprite palette is transparent, and the byte just copies the backdrop. Areas reference 178 slots: 131 as BG ids (k1 `p0-p2`, plus the constant BG3 id `$7F`) and 48 as sprite ids (`$07E6/$07E7` → slot +`$B0`). Sprite palettes 0/1 (`$07E4/$07E5`, player/common) are not set per area and not traced here.
- **Upload path (session 23):** NMI `31:F40D` `LDX #0` → `JSR PaletteUpload` `31:F8CB`. If `irq_mode` `$51` bit 7 is set it returns early (no upload). Otherwise it does: `PPUMASK`=0; `PPUCTRL` = shadow with b2 cleared (+1 increment); `PPUADDR`=`$3F00`; 32 unrolled `$2007` writes from `pal_buf,X`, where the first byte of **every** 4-colour group is `$6140` (the backdrop), so `$3F04/08/0C/10/14/18/1C` always mirror colour 0 and bytes `$6144/48/…/5C` are never uploaded. It ends with `PPUADDR` `$3F00` then `$0000` (moves v off the palette so the display doesn't pick up a palette colour), and the NMI then restores `PPUMASK` from `$01`. This is the only `$3F00` writer in the ROM: `search_bytes "A9 3F 8D 06 20"` finds just the 2 hits inside this routine. Other screens (banks 16-20, `30:C87B/CE6D`, `31:F31A`) write `pal_buf` directly and rely on this upload.
- each colour: `$FF` → copy of `$6140` (backdrop); then colour − `$11` (fade, steps of `$10`), negative → `$0F` black; written to **palette buffer `$6140-$615F`** (WRAM).
- `$07E8` = per-palette "ignore fade" mask, bit i = palette i (shifted out LSB first; only 8 bits, one per palette). Also tested via `BIT` at `30:C0E5`, `31:E3FE/E4E5/E4F4`.
- So the flag-item handler's `($6C&7)+9` → sprite ids `$B9-$C0`.
- `$6040-$605F` filled with `$0F` by `26:8C32` (a second palette buffer — target/black).
- **Sprite palettes 0/1 (session 25)** — set by the per-frame player setup `30:C055-C0D0`:
  - `$07E4` (sprite pal 0, player body) = `$00`, or `$4D` when `obj1_flash` `$0710`&`$0F` = 2 (`30:C05E`). It is also reset to 0 at `31:EFF3` (clears `$0710` low nibble, then `PaletteBuild`).
  - `$07E5` (sprite pal 1, sword/weapon) = clamp(`equip_sword` `$0711`, 1..5). The same Y value + `$41` → `chr_r3_play` R3, so the sword tiles and their palette change together.
  - So the in-game sprite palettes are slots `$B0`, `$B0`+sword (`$B1-$B5`), `e6+$B0`, `e7+$B0`.
  - Other writers: `30:CA88` (both = `$4F`, a non-gameplay screen), `19:BA16`/`19:B98A` (bank 19 scenes), `26:932D`.

**R4/R5 = `$FF` (session 8)** — `LoadAreaChrRecord` stores it unchanged and `ApplyChrPlay` `31:F883` writes `$07F4/$07F5` to MMC3 R4/R5 with no check. So `$FF` is **not** a "keep" marker: it selects CHR bank `$FF` mod 128 = **1KB bank 127**. Those areas probably have no enemies that use tiles `$80-$FF`. Session 25 confirmed this for spawn frames only: no spawned object's initial frames read a `$FF` register (see "Per-area sprite sets").

**`ObjInit` `30:C25D` (session 8)** — type `$11` (1 = no-op). `SetR7($0D)` (bank 13 at `$A000`), ptr = `ObjTypeTable` `13:AC00`[type·2] (`$AD00` upper half when type ≥ `$80`, using the carry from ASL). Record:
```
snd                 if ≠0 → PlaySound(snd)
                    (then all 32 columns $0300/$0400/$0500/$0600,X+n·$20 are zeroed for the slot; type $FF stops here)
m0 {..}             bitmask, MSB first: each set bit → next byte stored to $0300,$0320,…,$03E0 (,X)
m1 {..}             → $0400 … $04E0   ($04A0 = active, $04E0 = anim)
m2 {..}             → $0500 … $05E0   ($0540/$0560 spawn b2/param, $0580 overlay, $05C0/$05E0 screen pos)
m3 {..}             → $0600 … $06E0   ($06C0 last; $06E0 only if bit 0 set)
```
So each object type is a sparse initialiser for the 32 `$0300-$06E0` column arrays. Spawn handlers write their position/param fields after `ObjInit`.
*(s55, ported in `web/src/game/obj/objinit.js`, 100/100 fuzz cases whole-RAM identical:)* `ObjInit` leaves **R7 = 13** (`$6F` = `$0D`, `$50` = 7). Callers in banks 26/27 go through `FarCall_C25D` `31:FF80`, which restores R7 and then R6, so `$50` ends as 6. `$11` gets the type back at the end (`PLA`). `PlaySound` is called with Y = 0, so `snd_saved_y` `$010F` = 0. Type `$FF` clears the 32 columns and returns, so it is an empty object with `obj_active` = 0. The clear loop is `X += $20` until the carry, so a slot index ≥ `$20` (never used) would clear fewer rows.

**Area transitions — what sets `$6C` (session 8)** — `AreaSubTable` `30:C7A5` (A=k): `SwitchBank16(5)` (banks 10/11), header ptr = `10:8300`[`$6C`·2] (`$8400` upper half), returns `($10)` = header's k-th ptr16, Y=0. **Sub-table 3 = exit list**, scanned by `ExitLookup` `31:EB09`: player (obj 0 `$70/$90`, `$B0/$D0`) → 16-px cell ($12 = x>>4 | xhi<<4, $13 = y>>4 | yhi<<4); records `xc yc area dest6D`, `FF` end. On a match: if `$41`=8 → `$6C`=area, `$6D`=dest, `$41`=1 and the two return addresses are popped (aborts the caller). Otherwise it returns Y at the record's byte 2, and the caller (`30:DAAB`, after a walk-up/door animation that runs until `$B0`=`$8F`) does the same stores itself (+ `$51`=`$4B`=0). `$6D` = entry point in the new area (meaning TBD). Other writers: `30:C96F` (=0, new game, after clearing `$6480/$6580` flags), `30:CBDC` (`AreaByIndexTable` `30:CBEC`[`$0780`&15] = `02 03 04 14 1A 20 40 42 60 64 72 78 7C 90 A8 98` — warp/continue destinations), `30:D5CB` (=`$F2`, scripted), `30:DC93` (`30:DC58`[`$22`]), `31:E5E3` (restored from stack), plus banks 10/22/23/28.

**Area header sub-tables (session 9) (VERIFIED self-emu, s39: k0-k2 only)** — header at `10:8300`[`$6C`] (banks 10/11; 252 entries, the table ends at `$84F8` = area 0's header) = **5 ptr16s (k=0..4), data in order k0..k4 right after**. Only **10 areas have a 6th ptr (k5)**: `6A 6D 6E 8A 9F A2 A5 AE AF B4` (`area_dump.py sub stats`); for every other area a k5 read would return the k0 bytes (`snd`,`W`), so `31:E573` must only run in those areas. Null areas (null in `AreaPtrTable`) also have null/garbage headers. Consumers:
| k | Reader | Format |
|---|--------|--------|
| 0 | `AreaLoadMap` `31:E628` | `snd W H f62FE f62FF {grid}`: `PlaySound(snd)` (area music); `$62FC`=W = width−1 in screens, `$62FD`=H = height−1, `$62FE`, `$62FF` (b7 → `SetR6($0A)` when reading map data at `31:EBE5`; `$62FE` = **BG CHR animation set** 0-3, see `BgChrAnimTick` below). Then **screen grid** → `$6300-$637F` (pre-filled `$80`): H+1 rows of W+1 bytes, each row at an 8-byte stride (8 screens wide × 16 high max). Each byte = screen/room id; **`$80` = no screen** (same as the prefill; grids are mostly `$80-$9F`, plus `$E0-$FF` and low ids like `$71/$72` in shafts) |
| 1 | `31:E6B9` | `p0 p1 p2 v67 v69 R0 R1` → BG palette ids `$07E0-$07E2` (`$07E3` always `$7F`), `$67`, `$69` (`$66`=`$68`=0; `$6A/$6B` = `$BE00`+(`$67`&`$3C`)·8 ptr), BG CHR shadows `$07F0/$07F1` (MMC3 R0/R1, 2KB). Then `JMP AreaLoadB` |
| 2 | `31:E686` | **entry-point table**: 4-byte records indexed by `$6D`&`$1F` → `$34-$37` = **player start X lo/hi, Y lo/hi** (*corrected session 39, emulator*: area 0 entry 1 = `0088,0078` = the new-game position, and area 1 entry 0 = `00B0,0056` = the player position saved by the area-1 autosave. The record was read as a camera before.) `$34-$37` (now `pos_x_lo`…`pos_y_hi`) is a **shared position cursor**, written several times per frame: `ObjPosToTemp`/`TempAddStep` in the movement code (`26:98AC/984F/9875`), and the camera candidate in `CameraUpdate` `31:E90A` (`$3C-$3F` → `$34-$37`, `26:9824`, `CameraClampX` `31:EA72` clamps `$34/$35` to 0..`$62FC` screens, then BG column/row render). The camera that is drawn with is `$02-$05` (`ObjSpriteDraw` subtracts it). `$6D`=`$FF` → no record, bank 26 `$98A8` X=0 instead; `$6D`&`$E0`=`$20` → keep the current position (record skipped) |
| 3 | `ExitLookup` `31:EB09` | exit list (session 8) |
| 4 | `30:C765`, `31:E702` | **screen ↔ persistent-flag list**: 2-byte `flag scr` pairs, `FF` end; scr = y_hi<<4 \| x_hi. `30:C75C` marks the player's screen in the **visited-screen bitmap `$62F0-$62FB`** (row y_hi, bit `BitMask[x_hi]`, i.e. 8 cols × 12 rows — automap) and, if the screen is in this list, sets WRAM flag `$64C0`[flag>>3] bit (flag&7). On area load `31:E702` (after zeroing `$62F0-$62FB`) restores each listed screen's visited bit from its `$64C0` flag → visited state of selected screens persists across areas |
| 5 | `31:E573` | **edge links** (only when `$20` b0 at `31:E561`): 4-byte `area a21 a22 a23`, scanned until match; match if a21>>4 = player `$90` and (a22&`$F0`)\|(a23>>4) = `$D0`. Then `$B0`+=16, new `$D0` = ((a22<<1)&`$F0`)\|(a23&`$0F`), new `$90` = a21&`$0F`, `$6C`=area (via `$E628` reload). Y wraps (256 bytes) with no match → `$41`=8, bank 26 `$8C49` A=4. No terminator byte. Example: area `$6A` first record = "x_hi 5, y_hi 1 → area `$69` at x_hi 5, y_hi 2" — **falling down a pit into another area** (s46: confirmed statically, reached via `area_entry` `$6x` → `AreaPitFall` `31:E544`, see "World interaction"; not verified in an emulator; the k5 areas are tall, W=1 H=3 shafts like `$6E`/`$9F` or multi-floor maps) |

`$6D` = **entry-point index into sub-table 2** (camera start); `$FF` / `$2x` special values above. Area 0 example: k0 `00 00 00 00 00 [FF]` (1×1 screen), k1 `7C 7D 7E 98 B9 28 00`, k2 `88 00 BF 00` / `88 00 78 00`, k3 exits cells (6..A, C) → area 1 entry 0, k4 `EF 00`. `tools/area_dump.py sub [areas]` decodes all of it.

**BG CHR animation `$62FE` (session 59, static).** `BgChrAnimTick` `31:E75E` runs once per gameplay frame from `SubstPlayTail` `31:E0BD`, but not while `boss_cam_lock` is set. If `$62FE` ≠ 0 it sets `chr_r1_play` `$07F1` (the BG 2KB bank at PPU `$0800`) to `BgChrAnimTable` `31:E779`[`$62FE`·8 + ((`$08`>>3)&7)]. `$08` is the frame counter, so the bank changes every 8 frames and the cycle repeats every 64 frames. If `$62FE` is 0, `$07F1` keeps the k1 R1 value. The table has 4 rows; `E799` onwards is code, and every area uses 0-3:

| `$62FE` | R1 sequence | areas (`area_dump.py sub`) |
|---|---|---|
| 0 | none (row is all `00`, never read) | the rest |
| 1 | `0A 08 06 04` ×2 (4-step loop, 32 frames) | `14 15 40 41 57 60 64 68-6B 70 7C 7D 7F-87 8F 91 92 94 95 B8` |
| 2 | `26 26 24 24 22 22 20 20` (each held 16 frames) | `1A 43 88-8A 90 98 9C-9E AB-B6 B9 BA` |
| 3 | `20 22 24 26` ×2 | `58-5E 6C-6E 9F-A6 A8-AA` |

Two other writers: boss record +19 (`15:B886`, `BossDefeated`) sets a new animation set once the boss is beaten, and boss AI `$E2`'s phase at `15:A699` clears `$62FE` and fixes R1=`$2C`/R4=`$78`. *Web port:* `area.js` stores `$62FE` but nothing ports `BgChrAnimTick` yet; this is part of the gameplay web-fix task.

**Screen / metatile pipeline (session 10) (VERIFIED self-emu, s38: BG of title/area 0/area 1 exact)** — no compression; all fixed-size raw tables.
1. **Screen id → page** (`ScreenPagePtr` `31:EF36`, entry `EF33` = `ScreenAtCamera` `EF1F` first): screen id S from `$6300`[(pos_y_hi<<3 & `$78`) | (pos_x_hi & 7)] (`EF1F`). **`SwitchBank16(S>>6)`** (n 0-3 → 8KB banks 0-7) and `$10/$11` = `$8000 + (S&$3F)·$100` → each screen is **one 256-byte page** in banks 0-7. Grid ids `$80-$BF` → banks 4/5, `$C0-$FF` → banks 6/7, `$40-$7F` → banks 2/3. (This is the "packed pointer" noted under `31:EF43`.)
2. **Screen page = 16×15 metatile ids** (byte (y&`$F0`)\|(x>>4), 240 bytes); the 16-byte tail `$F0-$FF` of each page is reused for other tables (palette records in banks 2/3, handler-1 spawn records in banks 4/5 — see above).
3. **Visited substitution** (`ScreenTileSubst` `31:ED99`): if metatile M < `$20` and the screen's visited bit (`$62F0` bitmap, read by `ScreenVisitedBit` `31:EC6C` → `$1E` for this screen, `$1F` for the next) is set, M = bank 9 (`SetR7(9)`) byte at `$BE00 + ($67&$3C)·8 + M` (`ScreenSubstPtr` `31:ED89`, 32-byte table per tileset). → visited screens show alternate metatiles (opened doors/broken blocks, tentative).
   - **Which areas it changes (s68/69, `tools/visited_scan.py [--render]` → `gfx/visited_scan.txt`, `gfx/areas/area_NNN_v.png`; static).** Every screen the player stands on gets its `$62F0` bit (`30:C75C`), so any area can show the substitution; k4 only decides which screens keep the bit across area loads. Per grid cell the scan counts metatiles M < `$20` whose substitute draws differently (other quadrant tiles or attribute). Of 213 areas, **79 show a visible change**: `01 03 07 09 0C 10 14 15 22-24 26-2B 2D-30 32 35 40-46 48 4A-50 54 56 57 59-5C 60 64 65 69-6C 6E 73 7C 83 84 86-89 8C 8F 91 9C 9E 9F A0 A1 A6 A8 AB AE B4 B6 BA C1 E4 EC`. 102 changed cells are listed in k4 (they persist). **47 changed cells are not in k4** (areas `15 24 29 40 41 42 4C-4F 59-5C 60 65 6C 6E 88 8C 91 9C 9E A1 B6 BA C1 EC`): they change while the area stays loaded and revert on the next load. 6 k4 entries change nothing visible (`00`:fEF, and f82 in `02 1B 1E 2C 51`, all on screen `00`). All 79 `--visited` variants are rendered, and `node web/test/world_check.mjs --visited` → **83 areas identical, 0 differ** (the web `world/bg.js` substitution matches). Not checked in the emulator per area.
4. Column/row of metatile ids is buffered at `$6000,X`; `ScreenRowRender` `31:EBC1` crosses into the next screen at Y=`$10` (`$21`, `$1F`).
5. **Metatile → tiles** (`MetatileExpand` `31:EDBA`, banks 8/9 via `SwitchBank16(4)` at `EC14`): 4 parallel 256-byte pages at `$67&$FC` + 0/1/2/3 = TL/TR/BL/BR tile of metatile M (`$66`=0, so `($66),Y` with Y=M). `$67` = k1 `v67` (tileset). Attributes: `31:EDE5` → `SetR7(9)`, ptr table `31:EEEF`[(`$67`&`$3C`)>>1].
6. PPU upload: `31:ED64` builds nametable addr from camera, queued as a 4-byte record in `$6200+$0B` (`$6200`=hi\|`$0744`, `$6201`=lo&`$E0`, `$6202`=`$20` length, `$6203`=0), ring of 8 entries (`$0B` += 4 &`$1F`).

**Background details (session 24, `tools/render_screen.py`)**
- **`MetatileAttr` `31:EDE5`** handles a row; `31:EE31` is its column twin. Both return early unless `$2F` ≠ 0, then `SetR7(9)`. The table pointer is `MetatileAttrPtrTable` `31:EEEF`[(`$67`&`$3C`)>>1], which gives `$B000 + i·$40` for i = 0..15. Tilesets in use need i 0..11, so `9:B000-B2FF` is live.
  - The helper `MetatileAttrBits` `31:EECC` computes, for each metatile M in the `$6000` buffer (already substituted), `pal = (tbl[M>>2] >> 2·(M&3)) & 3`. So each metatile carries **one 2-bit BG palette**, packed 4 per byte, LSB first.
  - `$EF17` = `00 55 AA FF` replicates pal into all four quadrants. `$EF1B/$EF1D` = `03 30` / `0C C0` are the nibble masks for the left/right (or top/bottom) half. These are merged into the attribute shadow `$6100` (row step 1 at `EE80`, column step 8 at `EEA4`). `$EF07` = `00 00 08 08 … 38 38` is the attribute-row offset for pos_y>>4.
- **`MetatileExpand` `31:EDBA`**: `$12` is the quadrant start and `$13` the step. Rows use `$12` = 0/2 (top/bottom half from pos_y b3) with step 1, giving TL,TR or BL,BR. Columns use `$12` = 0/1 with step 2, giving TL,BL or TR,BR. The low 2 bits of k1 `v67` are therefore ignored; `$67` is rewritten during the loop but keeps `&$FC`.
- **`$62FF` b7 = bank-10 screen pages.** `ScreenRowRender` `31:EBE5` does `SetR6($0A)` after `ScreenPagePtr`, so screen ids with (S&`$3F`) < `$20` read their page from **bank 10** (`$8000 + (S&$1F)·$100`) instead of bank 2·(S>>6).
  - This flag is set in 32 areas: `3C 3D BC BE C2 C3 C4 C7 C8 C9 CB CF D0 D1 D2 D3 D9 DA DC DD E5 E7 EB ED EE F0 F5 F6 F7 F8 F9 FB`. Together they have 32 cells in bank 10.
  - **Quirk:** only the row path does this. When a row crosses into the next screen (`EC04`), it calls `ScreenPagePtr` again and loops back to `EBEF`, skipping the `SetR6`. The column render `31:EC87` never checks `$62FF` at all. So in those areas, horizontally scrolled columns and the second screen of a row read the normal bank. Presumably those areas never scroll in a way that exposes this (check in the emulator).
  - **Never exposed (s68/69, `tools/bank10_scan.py`; VERIFIED self-emu: areas `$C2`, `$D0`).** All 32 flagged areas are 1×1 single rooms (`$62FC`=`$62FD`=0) whose only cell is screen `$00`, `$01` or `$02`, and for each one the bank-10 page differs from the normal bank-0 page in all 240 bytes, so a wrong read would be obvious. With `area_w`=0 the camera x is pinned to 0 (`31:EA77`), so the column path never runs and every row starts at screen x 0 and never crosses into a second screen. In the emulator (`tour.py run --only C2,D0 --shots`) the room matches `gfx/areas/` exactly at offset (0,−4) below line 88 (left: 1.5 % / 1.4 %, all inside the sprite boxes). The flat **38.6 % BG diff** reported for every bank-10 room in `docs/emu_tour.txt` is the shop-name window (e.g. "INN") that covers the top of the playfield in these rooms (both rooms checked are inns; the identical 38.5-38.6 % for all 32 suggests every bank-10 room is a shop/inn interior with the same window), not a wrong page.
- **Row render entry `BgRowRender` `31:EB91`** (callers `30:C851`, `31:E95E`, `31:FFA3`): `JSR $C72B` (A=0), `INC pos_y_hi` if `$31` ≥ 0, then on through `EB9C` into `ScreenRowRender` `EBC1`. **Column render `BgColRender` `31:EC87`** (caller `31:E945`): `JSR $C72B` (A=`$20`), then `INC pos_x_hi` if `$30` ≥ 0. The next screen down is grid index Y+8, wrapping at `$60` (`SBC #$5F`), so the grid has **12 rows** at most (`$6300-$635F`).
- **Grid ids → banks** (`render_screen.py --stats`, 213 areas with a valid k0/k1). Cells per page bank: 0:290 1:144 2:111 3:71 **4:1431** 5:244 6:125 7:201 10:32. There are 253 distinct ids. Tilesets `$67` used: `80 84 88 8C 90 94 98 9C A0 A4 A8 AC` (`A0`×67 and `88`×41 are the most common).
- **Web port check (s53, `web/test/world_check.mjs`) (VERIFIED emu: area1_enter, gameplay_first):** `world/bg.js` renders all 213 areas pixel-identical to `render_level.py` (plain, and 6 areas with `--visited`). Against tools.emu frames 2451/2353 the BG of lines 0-190 is identical (sprite boxes and the PPUMASK-hidden left 8 px excluded). **Screen line 0 shows world y = signed(`$04/$05`) + 16** and x = signed(`$02/$03`): camera `$FFEC` → line 0 = world y −4, which matches the latched PPU v (NT 1 row 29 fine 4 = 476 = −4 mod 480, a 2-screen vertical ring). The loaders run fresh for the same area/entry reproduce the emulator's `$62F0-$62FF`, `$6300-$637F`, `$07E0-$07E7`, `$07F0/1/4/5`, `$66 $69 $6A $6B`, `$67&$FC` and `pal_buf`. **`$68` is not loader state at runtime:** `AreaLoadGfx` zeroes it, but `ObjBgProbe` `26:9A30` (called by the area-load substate at `30:CA3D`) reuses `$68/$69` as its collision-page pointer (`26:9A87`, `26:9AB1`), so the emulator shows `$68` = a metatile id afterwards.
- **Row / column streaming, details found while porting (s79, static + fuzz-checked, see the web bullet below).**
  - Both renders first spin in **`PpuQueueWaitSrc` `30:C72B`** while any of the 8 ring records still has src byte = A (row: 0 = `$6000`, column: `$20` = `$6020`). Flushed records get src `$FF`, so this waits until the NMI has uploaded the previous row/column from the same buffer.
  - **Row:** 16 metatiles, or 17 when pos_x_lo b3 is set. Buffer slot = (x>>4)·2 within the 256-px nametable, so the 17th metatile (from the next screen) **overwrites slot 0**, i.e. it lands on the same nametable column one screen further right. The record is `hi` = (`$20` + pos_y_lo>>6) \| `$0744`, `lo` = row start (`&$E0`), len `$20`, src 0. **With rendering off** (`ppumask_shadow`&`$18` = 0) the row calls `PpuQueueFlush` at once (with `ppuctrl_shadow` b7 cleared around it), so full-screen draws need no NMI.
  - **Column:** 15 metatiles, or 16 when pos_y_lo b3 is set, into `$6020 + row·2` (the next screen down restarts at `$6020`). `MetatileExpand` always expands 16 slots; only `$1E` = 30 bytes are uploaded. Record `hi` = `$60` (b6 = +32 increment; `$0744` is not ORed in), `lo` = column, so a column always covers NT rows 0-29 of `$2000`. It also sets `$18`=0, `$19`=1 (meaning unknown). No immediate flush on this path.
  - **Attribute merge** runs only while `$2F` ≠ 0 after `$2F` \|= ((step>>4) ^ pos) & 8 (row: `$31`/pos_y_lo, column: `$30`/pos_x_lo). The merged `$6100` shadow is uploaded later by **`PpuQueueAttrAll` `30:C739`** (record `$23C0`, len `$BF` = attribute path, src 0), queued with `nmi_disable` held.
  - `MetatileExpand` leaves `$67` = (`$67`&`$FC`) \| `$12` + `$13` (row: +1, column: +2). `ScreenTileSubst` leaves Y in `$25`, the replacement in `$26`. `MetatileAttrBits` leaves `$20` = `$FF`, `$21` = Y, `$22` = M.
  - **`FullScreenDraw` `30:C831`** (A = attribute flag; callers `15:B93D`, `27:A480`, `SubstRestoreScreen` `30:CC70`, `31:E4BB`): saves `$6E/$6F`, pos_y_hi += 1, then 30 times: `WaitPpuQueue`, `SetR6($1A)`, `TempAddOffset` `26:9824` with (`$30`,`$31`) = (0, −8), `BgRowRender`. So it draws the screen at `$34-$37` bottom-up, one 8-px row per pass, and ends with `$34-$37` back where they started. Banks are restored, then A ≠ 0 → `PpuQueueAttrAll`.
- **Web port (s79, Ph7 web 4/8 (a)):** `web/src/game/world/ppuqueue.js` (`ppuQueueFlush` = `Machine.ppuQueueFlush`, called by the NMI; `queueHasSrc`, `queueAttrAll`) and `world/bgstream.js` (`bgRowRender`, `bgColRender`, `fullScreenDraw` as generators that `yield` where the ROM spins on the queue; `runNow()` runs one to the end and throws if it would wait). They are ported instruction by instruction, including every zero-page temp and `$50/$6E/$6F` (new `switchBank16` in `prg.js`). Checked by `node web/test/obj_check.mjs` against `web/test/obj_frames/fuzz_bg.json` (`routine_capture.py` routines `PpuQueueFlush BgRowRender BgColRender FullScreenDraw`). Those cases also record the nametables (CIRAM) and PPUCTRL and run with NMI/IRQ suppressed, so the check compares RAM + WRAM + CIRAM. Not wired yet: the callers (`CameraUpdate` `31:E90A` for the row/column, the area-load substate and `SubstRestoreScreen` for the full draw); see the gameplay web-fix task.
- **`$80` "no screen" is a real page.** Bank 4 page 0 (`4:8000-80EF`) is 240 bytes of metatile `$80`, which renders as an all-black screen in every tileset tried. The engine never special-cases `$80` in the grid; it is simply read as screen `$80`. 660 grid cells inside the W×H rectangles are `$80` (e.g. area `$04` (0,0)), and the `$6300` prefill means anything outside the rectangle also draws black.

**Controller input (session 20)** — the only `$4016` readers are in `31:FF28-FF43`. Both pads are read (X=1 = pad 2 via `JOY1+X` = `$4017`, then X=0 = pad 1), so every pad variable is a 2-byte pair (pad 1 at the even address).
- `ReadPadRaw` `31:FF28`: strobe `$4016` 1/0, then 8× `LDA $4016,X` / `AND #3` (bit 0 or Famicom expansion bit 1) / `ROL $4F`. Byte layout of `pad_raw` `$4F`: **A=b7, B=b6, Select=b5, Start=b4, Up=b3, Down=b2, Left=b1, Right=b0**.
- `ReadPadStable` `31:FF17`: reads until two reads agree (DPCM-glitch guard); `$49,X` is used as the scratch copy.
- **`ReadPads` `31:FE80`** (gameplay; was mislabelled `ColdInitSub2`, 7 callers incl. `SubstPlay` `30:CB62` and the message wait loops): `pad_dir` `$49` = `PadDirTable` `31:FED0`[d-pad nibble] (0 = up, clockwise to 7 = up-left; `$FF` = none, U+D, L+R or all four; with three keys the opposed pair cancels, e.g. U+L+R → 0, U+D+L → 6); `pad_new` `$4B` = ~`pad_prev` & raw (edge); `pad_prev` `$43` = raw. Hold counters: on a new A (B) press `$45` (`$47`) = 0; while held it counts up. A: saturates at 31 and then sets carry each frame → `pad_act` `$4D` b7 = "A held ≥31 frames" (continuous). B: wraps every 32 frames → `$4D` b6 pulses once per 32 frames. `pad_act` = those two bits \| `pad_new`.
- **`ReadPadsMenu` `31:FEE0`** (menus: `16:80D4/8494/84EC/9887/9B42`, `15:BDE1`, `22:8BA3`): `pad_new` as above, plus **d-pad auto-repeat**: a new d-pad press loads `$45` = `$0C`; while the same d-pad bits stay held `$45` counts down, and at 0 it reloads 3 and ORs the d-pad bits into `pad_new` again (12-frame delay, then every 3 frames). `$45` therefore means A-hold in gameplay and d-pad repeat in menus.
- Known uses of `pad_new`: b7/b6 A/B close or advance messages (text code `03`, `30:D354`); b5 Select replays a message; **b6 B with an item selected → `PlayerUpdate` `26:9B45`** (use the item).

### Full-ROM disassembly (session 22, `tools/disasm_all.py`)

**Method.** Recursive descent from these seeds:
- the 3 vectors;
- every labels.csv ROM label whose name is not a data name (`--labels` shows the split);
- 15 dispatch tables, each with the bank context its dispatcher sets up: `MainStateTable`, `IrqHandlerTable`, `SubstateHandlerTable` (context from `SubstateBankTable`), `TalkActionTable`, `EventActionTable`, `ItemEventHookTable` (14/15), `SpawnHandlerTable` (12/13), `SndOpcodeTable` (24/25), `TextCtrlTable`, `HitHandlerTable`, `ItemUseHandlers`, `ObjAiTable` (26/27, ids `$60-$6F` → 26/15), `BossPhaseTable` (auto count **25**), and the new `State3ModeTable`/`State3SubTable`. *(s67: + 13 more, 28 in all — see "All dispatch tables decoded" below.)*

Each path carries `ctx = (r6, r7)`:
- `LDA #n` before `JSR/JMP SwitchBank16 / SetR6 / SetR7` updates it, and so do the fixed tail-calls `C7A0` (26/27) and `E3D4` (12/13).
- Any other `JSR` applies the **callee's summarised bank effect**: the tool walks the callee's fall-through path to its `RTS`. This catches helpers like `30:DA0C` (maps 20/21 before `JSR $9500`) and `19:B9FB` (`SetR6($1A)`, `JMP $8CC0`). A path through `RestoreBanks` `FFDB` counts as no change.
- Code running from a swappable window always keeps its own bank pinned there.
- If the context is unknown, a target resolves only when exactly one bank has a code label at that address.
- Each target must pass a 10-instruction decode check before it is traced; targets that fail are reported as "rejected".
- Z/N/C are tracked through `LDx #imm`, `CLC/SEC`, flag-neutral instructions and not-taken branches. This makes `LDA #0 / STA / BEQ` and `BNE x / BEQ y` pairs unconditional (e.g. MainLoop `C91D`, `15:BF1A`).
- Code and operands may run across a window edge into the next window's bank (16KB pairs, and `$C000→$E000`).

There are no JSR-with-inline-data helpers: all 6 `PLA/STA/PLA/STA` sites restore values pushed earlier. Swappable banks have **no** JMP entry table at `$8000`/`$A000`; the call-site map's "fixed entry offsets" (`$8020`, `$8028` …) are plain routine addresses.

**Results:**
- 40,170 code bytes; 1,978 auto-named targets.
- 0 warnings, 0 rejected targets.
- 1 unresolved target: `15:AB84 JSR $9824`, after `PLA / JSR SetR6` at `15:AB6F` restores a pushed bank, almost certainly 26. `15:AB87 JMP $9897` resolves through the `TempToObjPos` label. *(s67: confirmed 26 = `TempAddOffset`; 0 unresolved now.)*
- 2 straddling instructions: `18:9FFF` (`STA $04` → `19:A000`) and `30:DFFF` (`STA abs` → `31:E000`). `26:9FF4` branches across into `27:A002`.
- 33 indirect-jump sites. All but 8 are the known tables. The 8 dispatchers with untraced tables:

| Site | Index | Table | Note |
|---|---|---|---|
| `17:A117` `JMP ($008E)` | `$0A`·2 | `17:A11A` | state 4/5 code |
| `27:A06E` `JMP ($0010)` | A·2 | `27:A072` | AI code |
| `27:BA3D` `JMP ($0010)` | `$0600,X`·2 | `27:BA40` | AI phase dispatch |
| `30:DBD9` `JMP ($0010)` | A·2 | `30:DBDC` | after `SubstTeleportMenu` |
| `30:DDBA` `JMP ($0010)` | A·2 | `30:DDBD` | after `SubstTelepathySelect` |
| `30:DF0B` `JMP ($0010)` | A·2 | `30:DF0E` | after `SubstChangeForm` |
| `31:E419` `JMP ($0010)` | A>>4 (no ASL) | `31:E3D9` | after `SwitchBank16_6`; spawn/entity path |
| `31:F6BF` `JMP ($0052)` | `irq_mode`·2 | `31:F6C2` | NMI/IRQ-mode dispatch |

**All dispatch tables decoded (session 67, static; now in `disasm_all.JUMP_TABLES`).** Counts are exact: either the index range is bounded, or the table runs straight into its first handler.

It returns C=1 and index 0-7 = **A B SELECT START UP DOWN LEFT RIGHT** when `pad_new` is exactly one button; otherwise C=0, and the caller loops.
