When `$08` & `$0F` = 0 (every 16th frame): `PlaySound($31)` (with Y = `$EC` = id·2 left by `ObjUpdateAll`'s `TAY` `30:CD07`, so `snd_saved_y` = `$EC`), slot 0 `obj_freeze` = 2, and if `mp_cur` ≠ 0: `mp_cur`−− + `HudRedraw(4)` (MP digits; X restored from `$14`). Then `obj_anim` += `$FF`, or `$FD` while `pad_new` ≠ 0 (button mashing shakes it off 3× faster); no carry → `obj_active` = 0. `27:A4E0` is a second entry with overlay `$99` and no xrefs. In the port `aiMpDrain` is a generator (`HudRedraw`'s `PpuQueueWaitSrc($7B)` spin) |

**Shots — `SpawnShot` `26:972D` + `ShotTable` (`tools/shot_dump.py` → `docs/shots.txt`):**
- The record is at `20:BC00` + id·4, with bank 20 mapped at `$A000`. Bit 6 of the id moves it to page `$BD00`, so ids `$00-$3F` are at `BC00` and `$40-$5F` at `BD00`. Fields:
  - +0 = first slot (b7 set → nothing spawned, C = 0)
  - +1 = end slot (exclusive; free-slot search `ObjFindFree` `26:98B9`)
  - +2 = object type (→ `ObjInit`)
  - +3 = count
- Each spawned object gets `obj_meta_dir` = Y (the direction passed in) and inherits the shooter's `obj_flags` b7 (offscreen). It is placed at the shooter's position + **`ShotOffsets` `20:BD80`**[(**spawned object's** `obj_behav` << 4) \| ((Y·2) & `$0F`)] = (dx, dy). *(s55 correction, emulator-checked: `97AB` reads `obj_behav,X` after `LDX $10`, so the row comes from the new object's type record, not from the shooter; a port using the shooter's value fails 192/1000 `obj_check.mjs` cases.)* The offset is added by `TempAddOffset` `26:9824`. dy gets +2 more if the shooter's `obj_flags` b6 (sinking) is set. The return value is C = 1 if at least one object spawned.
- Ids `$00-$1E` are the player's sword shots and magic (slots 2-11, types `$08-$22`; for example `$0B` = 8 × type `$16`, `$0E` = 7 × type `$1E`).
- Ids `$20-$3F` are boss and event spawns into slots `$0D-$1E` (`$27` → `BC` for boss `$A5`, `$31` → `CC` for `$9B`, `$32` → `CB` for the statues, `$3B` → `C1` for `$57`, …).
- Ids **`$40-$5F` = enemy projectile types `$E0-$FF`** (id + `$A0`, one each, slots `$0D-$1E`). The full id → users list is in `docs/shots.txt`. Enemy projectiles use AI `$10` (`AiStraightMover`) or AI `$11` `27:B0A8`. AI `$11` runs for one frame only: meta \|= dir>>1 (a directional sprite), then `DEC obj_active` → AI `$10`. Their attack values are in `docs/enemies.txt` (`$E0` 32 … `$F5` 94).
- `ShotOffsets` rows by `obj_behav`: 0 = none; 1/3/4/7 = in front of the body at 8/24/12/~66 px; 5 = a 36 px ring; 2/6 = 8-direction mouth offsets.

**`AiChase` `27:AA57`** (AI `$24`):
1. **Rate:** acts on frames where (X + `$08`) & 1 = 0 (every other frame), or & 7 = 0 when offscreen (every 8th frame). Each acting frame: `obj_anim` −= 2, `obj_timer`−−.
2. **Normal mode** (`obj_timer2` `$0620` = 0): every 16 timer steps, dir = `DirToTarget`(Y = 0, the player-body slot) & `$FE` (one of 4 directions toward the player). Meta = (dir & 6)>>1 \| `$06C0`, unless `obj_flags` b3 is set. Then `ObjMoveSlide`.
3. **Blocked** (C = 1): `$0620` = `$60` (96 acting frames of detour). `obj_phase` = ((X + `$08`)>>2) & 1 picks the turn side: 0 = dir+1, 1 = dir−1 (45° steps).
4. **Detour mode** (`$0620` ≠ 0, `$0620`−−): every 8 `obj_anim` steps it first turns back toward the original side and test-moves with `27:AAFA`. `27:AAFA` is a trial `ObjMoveSlide` at speed `$0A` that restores the position afterwards; C = 1 means blocked. If that way is free it takes it. Otherwise it turns forward again (up to twice). So the enemy follows the edge of an obstacle for up to 96 steps, then chases again.
4b. *(s86)* **`obj_flags` b3 = single-pose sprite.** It is read at only 5 places in the ROM (`search_bytes BD8003 2908`), all in the chase family: `AiShooterChase` `A9E9`/`AA31` (pose = `$06E0`/`$06C0` with no dir>>1 added), `AA11` (the shot then goes in `obj_meta_dir` & 6, because the meta has no facing bits to read it from), and `AiChase` `AA8C`/`AB2D` (no pose write at all). The sprite pass ignores it. Set (`0380` = `09`) on chasers `$5A $5F $65 $69 $71 $72 $74 $7B $86 $8C $91 $9A`, most of which have no `$06C0` pose base (round / blob sprites with one frame set); the bosses that set `obj_flags` 9/`$0B`/`$2B` in their intros never reach these AIs.
5. *(s81, details from the instruction-level port `web/src/game/obj/ai_walk.js`, 1200/1200 emulator fuzz cases identical:)*
   - **Turns:** `27:AAAB` turns by A = `obj_phase`^1 and `27:AAB0` by A = `obj_phase`. In both, A bit 0 = 1 gives dir+1, else dir−1, and the result is & 7.
   - The detour test order is: turn back, then `AiTryMove`. If that is free (`27:AAEF`), dir = `$23` (the direction `ObjMoveSlide` actually used, including a diagonal fallback), then a real `ObjMoveSlide`. If it is blocked, it turns forward and tries again. If that is free too, it falls through to `AAE0`. If it is blocked twice, it turns forward once more and returns without moving.
   - The non-test move `27:AAE0` ignores the carry. When `$20` (the last `ObjBgProbe` attr) = 0, dir = `$23`. Every detour move ends in `27:AB2D` (pose = (dir & 6)>>1 \| `$06C0`, skipped when `obj_flags` b3 is set).
   - **Harmless quirk:** at `AA9C` the `TXA; ADC $08` still has C = 1 from the `BCS`, so `obj_phase` = ((X + `$08` + 1)>>2) & 1. AiChase only acts when (X + `$08`) is even, so the +1 never changes bit 1. A port without the +1 behaves the same (mutation test: 0 cases differ).
   - **Other port notes:** `AiShellOnHit` `B364` compares the **old** `obj_param2`: old ≥ `$1F` gives anim = new, and new = `$20` sets `obj_freeze` = 8. Otherwise anim = old ^ `$FF`.
   - `AiBurrower`'s body `27:ABAC` is JSR'd, so an `AiShootCheck` pop there returns to `AB9E`, and the slot-0 `$0620` → `obj_coll` = 0 check still runs.
   - `AiShooterWalk` still updates its meta and clears `obj_phase` when `AiWalkPause` returns early (offscreen quarter rate / pause).
   - None of the part (a) AIs pops `ObjUpdateAll` (every `AiShootCheck`/`AiLungeCheck` is reached by a JSR), unlike the fan turret.

#### Hit handlers 0 / 2 / 3 + gold drop (session 78, static RE)

`HitHandlerTable` `26:8FBB` = `90AA 9357 94B0 8FC4`; the `CollPairTable` handler byte is index·2 (0, 2, 4, 6). X = attacker slot, Y = target slot (`$0D-$1E`). Handler 1 = `HitPlayer` (see "World interaction").

**Handler 0 `HitByPlayerShot` `26:90AA`** (attackers = player shot slots 2, 4-11). Checks in order:
1. Area `$5F`: `obj_active` of slots 4, 5, 6 = 0 on every hit (extra shots removed in that area).
2. `ThunderTransform` `26:9229`: if `equip_sword` = 4 (thunder), the target's `obj_spawn_b2` = 0 and its `obj_spawn_param` is 0 or 3 → `ObjInit`(target, its `obj_behav` `$06E0`) and the handler returns (double `PLA`). *(s86: who it is for.)* `obj_spawn_b2` = 0 means spawn handler 0 (or 4, but h4 params 0/3 = no-op types `$00`/`$03`), and `SpawnH0` `31:E27F` stores `$0560` = p with type = p + `$50`. So the filter selects exactly **types `$50` and `$53`** (the first two `AiWalkPause` blobs, HP 2 / 3), whose `obj_behav` = `$5F` / `$69`. A thunder-sword hit (before the level gate and any damage) turns a `$50` (area `$03`) into a **`$5F`** chaser (HP 9, atk `$19`, XP 6) and a `$53` (areas `$06 $09 $85`) into a **`$69`** chaser (HP `$5A`, atk `$78`, def `$0D`, need level 6, XP 20 decoded). Neither `$5F` nor `$69` is placed by any area (`docs/enemies.txt` areas column is empty), so this is their only source. Their records set `obj_spawn_param` = 1, so they can't transform again. Not emulator-checked.
3. Target AI `$52` (sparring duel): every 4th hit (`DEC obj_aux_s0D` & 3 = 0) slot 0 and the target move up 8 px; SFX `$21`; no damage.
4. Target AI `$10` (a projectile) → nothing.
5. Target `obj_active` = `$87` (types `$D0-$D3`, AI `$07` with b7) → `26:9094` **element walls**: if `target.elem & shot.elem & $0F` ≠ 0, or the shot's `obj_hitflags` = 1 (a level-0 tap shot), nothing; else `EnemyKilled` (→ death type `$D4`/`$D6`). Their element masks are `$0E $0D $0B $07`, so `$D0` breaks only with wind (elem 1), `$D1` fire (2), `$D2` water (4), `$D3` thunder (8), and only with a charged shot. Crystalis (elem 0) breaks all four.
6. Target meta `$A7` (type `$7B`, area `$6D`, HP 0) → `26:9082`: only a shot with `obj_hitflags` = 3 (full charge) does anything: `ObjInit`(target, death type `$4F`), which is the hidden enemy (`AiDormant`, HP `$32`). Other shots pass through it.
7. **Normal damage:**
   - need = target `obj_hitflags` & `$1F`. If `player_level` < need → **miss**. So the hitflags low bits are the **player level needed to hurt the enemy**, not an enemy level.
   - dmg = `$03E1` (player slot 1 `obj_attack`) + shot `obj_attack`.
   - `target.elem & shot.elem & $0F` ≠ 0 → miss. So an enemy's element nibble is an **immunity mask** against the sword elements (`SwordElemBit` 1/2/4/8). `AiPatrolGunner` sets `$0F` while patrolling = immune to every sword, and 0 while attacking. Crystalis and the player's non-sword shots with elem 0 are never blocked.
   - dmg −= target `obj_defense`; borrow → miss.
   - HP `$03C0,Y` −= dmg; borrow → `EnemyKilled`. Else `HitKnockbackSet`, SFX `$21`. **The shot survives a damaging hit** (it can hit other targets; the same target is protected by the knockback b7 that `ObjOverlapScan` skips).
   - **Miss:** the shot is removed (`obj_active,X` = 0), the target gets knockback unless its `obj_active` b7 is set, SFX `$3A` ("clink").
- Player shot `obj_hitflags` = charge level (1 = tap / level 0, 2 = level 1, 3 = full charge; types `$08-$22`), and their elem nibble = sword element.

**Handler 2 `TalkProbeHit` `26:94B0`** (attacker = slot 3, the talk / item-use probe in front of the player; `obj_act_timer_s03` `$0643` = last touched slot):
- Target AI `$78` (types `$0D $0E`) or `$6E` (type `$0F`, the boss reward drop): only while `sub_state` = 8 (item use, which sets `$0563`/`$0623`=3) → `obj_timer2_s03` `$0623` = target, `sub_state` = 7. That is how an item is used on an event object.
- Target AI `$30` / `$B0` (`AiNpcWander`, test `obj_active·2` = `$60`), otherwise nothing:
  - `pad_dir` b7 set → nothing.
  - `obj_behav` b2 set (**solid NPC**) → `NpcPushBack` `26:9535`: 4 passes of: overlap test player (slot 1) vs this NPC with its group mask forced to `$F0`; when they touch, slots 3, 2, 1, 0 each `ObjMoveSlide` one step **opposite the player's facing** (dir = (meta&3)·2 + 4 & 6), then a sprite pass (`FarCall_B14_800E`). Special case: spawn_b2 = 1, param `$3A`, player facing up → player meta &= `$FC`. If they no longer touch: `obj_phase_s03` = 0, and if slot 0's `$0620` (jump) ≠ 0, `$0640` = `$FF`; no talk. If the push ran, the talk starts (`9508`).
  - Else: no talk while slot 0 `$0620` ≠ 0 (mid-jump), or when `obj_phase_s03` ≠ 0 and `$0623` is already this NPC (talk cooldown), or when the player body (slot 1) itself overlaps the NPC.
  - **Talk start `26:9508`**: `obj_phase_s03` = `$10` (cooldown), `$0623` = NPC slot, `sub_state` = `$11` (`NpcTalk`). If the NPC's `obj_freeze` = 0 and `obj_behav` b1 set, it turns to face the player (meta low bits = (player meta + 2) & 3).

**Handler 3 `HitHandler3` `26:8FC4`** (attacker = slot `$0C`):
- Attacker `obj_phase` = 1 → `26:906C` **shot eater**: if the target is an enemy shot (`obj_spawn_b2` = `$FF`, test `26:907C`) → target `obj_active` = 0, SFX `$3A`. Nothing else.
- Otherwise: enemy shots and AI `$78` targets are ignored. The attacker is removed; if the target's `obj_hitflags` b5 is set, that is all. Else target `obj_freeze` = `$FF` (frozen ~4 s), and for `obj_spawn_b2` = 1 targets: params in `HitImmuneParams` `26:9058` (20 bytes) get `obj_freeze` = 0 instead, the rest run `HitSetFlag` with `$12` = `$FF` (set the param's flag; `ObjUpdateAll` clears it when the freeze runs out). So slot `$0C` is a **freeze shot** (paralysis magic, inferred from the `$FF` freeze).
- *(s86: who sits in slot `$0C`.)* Only shot ids `$10-$12` use slot `$0C` (`docs/shots.txt`, first = end = `$0C`), and all three are player magic (`PlayerMagic` table under "Player"): **magic 2** → `SpawnShot($10)` = type `$20` (AI `$11` → straight mover, speed 8, `obj_coll` `$9E`, no phase field → phase 0 = the **freeze shot**, lifetime `$60`); **magic 6** (held) → `SpawnShot($11)` each frame = type `$21` (AI `$04`, `obj_coll` `$CB`, **phase 1 = the shot eater**: a barrier that deletes enemy shots it touches and nothing else); **magic 1** (heal) → `SpawnShot($12)` = type `$22` (AI `$04`, no `obj_coll` → never collides; a heal sparkle). Magic 2's "slot `$0C` free" test is what blocks a second cast while the first shot is alive.

**Gold drop:** `EnemyKilled` with death type 0 → `EnemyDeathFx` → AI `$7D` `DeathPuff` `27:B9AA`. Phase 0: x jitters ±4 (`27:BA2C` `FC 04`) for 16 frames. Then meta `$AC`, `obj_anim` = `$3F`, phase ≠ 0 (fade: `obj_anim` −= 2 → despawn). At the switch, if `obj_elem_drop` hi ≠ 0: `SpawnShot($3F)` = one **coin type `$0C`** (AI `$7B`: lifetime `obj_timer`, then `ObjBecomeNext`). If no slot is free, the puff frees its own slot and tries again. The coin gets the enemy's `obj_elem_drop` and meta = `CoinMetaTable` `26:8BFE`[class] (`$A8` for classes 0-10, `$A9` for 11-14, `$AA` for 15). Picking it up (`HitPlayer` AI `$7B` → `PickupGold` `26:946D`) adds `GoldDropTable` `26:8BDE`[class] = `0 1 2 4 8 16 30 50 100 200 400 50 100 200 400 500` (saturates at `$FFFF`), SFX `$24`. Classes 11-15 are the big-coin versions of 50-500.

#### Boss phase bodies, bank 15 (session 78, static RE)

Phase = `obj_phase` `$0600`; handlers from `gfx/boss_phases.txt`. Every boss AI first calls `26:9B07` (palette arg) and refreshes `boss_cam_lock` while phase ≠ 0. Common intro pattern ("phase 0"): `ClearEnemySlots` `15:BB29`, set palettes/CHR, `obj_freeze` holds the boss, `boss_cam_lock` = `$FF` (align); once `$07D5` = 1 (camera aligned) → freeze 0, `PlaySound($12)` (boss music), next phase.

- 1 `B3BC` **sway**: speed 1, moves left/right (turns at x < `$40` / ≥ `$C0`) via `BossEBMove`, hands idle, BG cycle running. Every 32 `obj_anim` steps an odd `Rand64` → `obj_timer2` = `$C0` (hand attack). When `$08` b7 is clear, the player's (x ^ y) parity picks instead: odd → phase 6 (`obj_timer2` = `$80`), even → phase 5.
  - **Hand attack** (`obj_timer2` ≠ 0, `15:B425`): every 8 frames the hand poses cycle (`15:B48B`), and every 16 frames `SpawnShot($2E)` = type `$BF` (slots `$1A-$1E`) in dir `Rand64` + 4 (down half), from the left or right hand (x ∓ `$18`, y − `$1C`). At 0: hands idle, and an odd `Rand64` → phase 2 with dir `15:B4BB`[`Rand64`] (5-B, downward) and speed `15:B4C4`[((x + player x)>>1) & 3] (`$0D $0E $0F $0C`).
- 2 `B4DD` **dive**: moves (`BossEBMove`, bouncing off x `$38`/`$D8` with the mirror table `15:B50C`); at y ≥ `$C0` → `obj_timer2` = `$20`, dir `15:B51C`[`Rand64`] (D-3, upward), phase 4.
- 4 `B552`: holds; at `obj_timer2` = `$10` one aimed `SpawnShot($46)` (type `$E6`); at 0 → phase 3. 3 `B524` **rise** until y < `$50` → phase 5 with `obj_act_timer` = `DirToTarget`·2.
- 5 `B587` **mouth open** (vulnerable): elem 0 (see above). Every 16 `obj_timer2` steps a PPU block `$3D` rewrites 4 BG tiles (`$6000-$6003` from `15:B60C` via `15:B5F9`/`B609`; also slot `$0F` meta), which opens and closes the mouth. Every 4 frames `15:B623` fires 2 × `SpawnShot($30)` (type `$CD`, AI `$1E` `27:B094`) at x ∓ `$12`, y + 8, in dir `obj_act_timer` + wobble `15:B672`[(`$08`>>5) & 7] (`+1 +2 +1 0 −1 −2 −1 0`) and the opposite-phase wobble. At `obj_timer2` = 0 (255 frames after phase 4, or `$C0` from phase 1): elem `$0F`, random dir 2/6, phase 1.
- 6 `B67A` **rain**: every 16 frames `SpawnShot($2F)` = type `$CE` (AI `$10`, atk `$78`, slots `$14-$1E`) straight down (Y = 4), 16 px higher. 8 shots, then back to phase 1 (`15:B599`).

**Bank 15 bosses (web port, session 100) — `web/src/game/obj/ai_boss.js`, `AI_BOSS` in `AI_TABLE`.** Ported instruction by instruction: AI `$60` `BossAiE0` (8 phases + `BossSmokeRing`/`BossE0Fire`), AI `$61` `BossHomingShot` (+ `HomingMove`, exported for boss `$E8`), AI `$63` `BossAiE3` (5 phases + `BossE3NewDir`/`TurnToward16`), AI `$64` `BossRockShot`, plus `ClearEnemySlots` and `BossPhaseDispatch`. Corrections / details found while porting (the s78 notes above stay as the summary):
- `BossPhaseDispatch` leaves `$10/$11` = the handler address and **Y = (base + phase)·2**, which reaches `PlaySound` in `BossE3Slam` (`$3D`, Y = `$1A`). The intro handlers call `PlaySound($12)` with Y = `$0C` (left by `ClearEnemySlots`). The port keys `BOSS_PHASES` by handler address, so a phase past a boss's run takes the next boss's handler as in the ROM (or logs "AI boss phase handler 15:xxxx not ported").
- The "`obj_freeze` holds the boss" in the intros is **`obj_freeze` of slot 0** (`STA $05A0`, no X): the *player* is frozen (`$10`) until `$07D5` (camera aligned).
- `BossE0Teleport` phase 4 also ends when `obj_anim`+1 reaches `$80` (before `obj_aux` ≥ `$0C`). `BossE0Linger` (phase 7) aims with `DirToTarget(Y = 1)`; its `obj_anim & 7` test after the `DEC … BNE` is always 0.
- `BossE3Approach`'s retreat does `INC obj_phase` then `BossE3NewDir` (`DEC obj_phase`), so it **stays in phase 1** with a new downward dir; likewise `BossE3Slam`'s end does `DEC` + `NewDir`'s `DEC` → phase 1, and `BossE3Sweep`'s end sets 2 then `NewDir` → 1. Phase 2 is entered only from the close-range branch (timer `$30`). The slam aims `obj_meta_dir` = (slot-1 `obj_meta_id`·2 + 4) & 7.
- `BossRockShot` phase 0: the PRG offset is ((r·2 + C) + x lo) ^ y lo ^ `$08` with C = r b7 from the `ASL` (r = first `Rand64`; the second `Rand64` is the R6 bank). The port reads it with `setR6` + `cpuRd`, then restores R6 from `$6E`.
- Test: `routine_capture.py` `BOSS_AIS` (bank = (R6, R7) = (`$1A`, `$0F`) as `ObjUpdateAll` leaves it, `$50` = 7; `_fix_boss` picks a phase in range and steers `$07D5`, Rand64 = 0, timers and arena edges) → `web/test/obj_frames/fuzz_boss.json`: **1200/1200** identical (300 per routine). The boss ids are now in `PORTED_AIS`, so `fuzz_update.json` (regenerated) runs them inside `ObjUpdateAll` with `SetR7($0F)`: 200/200. Boss slots there get an in-range phase (a random one jumped into RAM: illegal opcode at `$2230`). `STACK_SCRATCH` (capture) and the `obj_check.mjs` stack window are now 32 bytes: `ObjUpdateAll` → boss → `BossSmokeRing` → `SpawnShot` → far `ObjInit` writes 16 bytes below the old window (the port has no stack). All routine cases: **6200/6200**.
- **Session 104: AI `$66` `BossAiE6`, `$67` `AiBoss88`, `$68` `BossAiE8` ported** (same file; handlers in `BOSS_PHASES`, shared `introMessage`/`stepMove16` = `BossE7Step`). Details the s78 summary leaves out:
  - `BossE6Intro` calls `PlaySound($12)` (Y = `$0C` from `ClearEnemySlots`) **on every aligned frame** while `obj_timer2` counts up to `$40`, not once. `BossE6Speeds` `15:AD82` is read only for HP < `$30` (HP>>4 < 3); above that `obj_flags2` keeps its type speed. The fly turn uses `TurnSign16` `15:AD86` on the 16-dir `obj_meta_dir` directly (no `act_timer`), with slot 0's `obj_y_lo` swapped for `$30`/`$70` around the `DirToTarget(Y=0)` call. The fire fan's last shot comes on the frame `obj_timer2` hits 0, so 10 shots from `$50`.
  - `BossE7Bounce` passes the edge value (0 / `$10` / `$18` / `$08`) as Y to `PlaySound($3D)` and keeps it in `$10` across `PlaySound` + `Rand64` for the `BossE7BounceDirs` index. `act_timer`−− happens on frames where the decremented `obj_timer2` is odd. `BossE7Volley`'s `$1F` = the shot's 16-dir; `SpawnShot` gets dir16>>1 and only the last spawned slot (`$10`) gets `obj_meta_dir` = `$1F`.
  - `BossE8Fly` keeps the boss slot in `$1F` (`STX $1F`) while it writes the waypoint into slot `$1F`'s position. A `FE` entry resets `obj_anim`/`obj_timer2` and reads entry 0 in the same frame. `BossE8Attack` (`15:B051`; the s78 listing at `B04F` was misaligned: `FE FF` are the route's last bytes) always runs `BossE8AttackPose` (overlay `$F1`, meta `$DE`, `obj_anim` −= 2) and decrements `act_timer` before the `aux` test; `BossE8LegEnd` `15:B077` sets phase 1 / dir 8.
  - `$E6`'s phases 2/3 are `$0000` in `BossPhaseTable`: the capture keeps them out (a jump to `$0000` runs RAM).

**Boss shot / part AIs (session 86, static RE; type fields from `gfx/objtypes.txt`).** All of them share the tail of `AiStraightMover` at `27:B0BE`: offscreen (`obj_flags` b7) → `ObjDespawn`; `obj_timer`−−, at 0 → `ObjBecomeNext` (type `$04C0`); `ObjPosToTemp` + `ObjStepVector`(dir) + `TempAddStep`; then BG test only if `obj_bg_mask` ≠ 0 (`ObjBgProbe($C0)`, attr & mask & 6 → `ObjBecomeNext`), else `TempToObjPos`.
- **AI `$14` `AiCountdown` `27:B2C5`** (smoke `$C0` of `BossSmokeRing`, and types `$13 $17 $1B $D5 $D7 $DF`): `obj_anim` −= `obj_phase` each frame, borrow → `ObjDespawn`. `$C0` has phase 2, anim `$1F` → a 16-frame puff (meta `$9B`, no movement); the `obj_freeze` 8…1 set by `BossSmokeRing` only delays each puff's start.
- **AI `$16` `AiCurveShot` `27:B0F6`** (type `$FC`, boss `$E7` volley: speed `$0E`, lifetime `obj_timer` `$60` = 96 frames, atk `$58`, then `ObjBecomeNext` → type 3 = nothing): `obj_anim` −= 2; whenever `obj_timer` & `$0F` = 0 (tested before the mover's `DEC`, so at `$60 $50 … $10` = 6 times) the 16-dir heading turns one 22.5° step: **+1 for even slots, −1 for odd slots** (`DEC DEC INC`). So each shot curves 135° over its life, alternating sides by slot. Combined with the "aim − 2" quirk of `BossAiE7` phase 5, the even-slot shots curve back toward the player and the odd-slot ones away.
- **AI `$17` `27:B11B`** (type `$FE`): `obj_anim` −= 2 from `$0F`; on going negative (8 frames) meta++ and `obj_active` = `$10` (`AiStraightMover`). A shot that hangs still for 8 frames, changes sprite, then flies.
- **AI `$1B` `AiBounceShot` `27:B12D`** (types `$CA` boss `$E8` atk `$58`, `$BF` final-boss hand shot atk `$64`; speed `$0E`, `obj_bg_mask` 6, `obj_anim` `$FF`): offscreen → `ObjDespawn` (its only despawn; `obj_timer` is decremented but never tested). Up to 5 tries per frame: `obj_timer`−−, `obj_anim`−− (anim 0 → `obj_bg_mask` = 0 and return without moving), step + **commit** the move (`TempToObjPos`), then `ObjBgProbe($40)`; if attr & mask & 6 ≠ 0 → heading += 4 (90° turn in 16-dir) and retry. So it **ricochets off walls** for ~255 frames, then flies straight through everything until it leaves the screen.
- **AI `$1E` `27:B094`** (type `$CD`, final-boss mouth shot: speed `$0B`, `obj_timer` `$12`, atk `$78`, no BG mask): each frame `obj_anim` = (dir & 7)·8 (a fixed animation frame per direction, meta `$F3`), then the mover tail. Range = 18 frames, then `ObjBecomeNext` → type 0 (gone).
- **AI `$1F` `27:B0A2`** (types `$EB`/`$F3`): `obj_anim`−− then the mover tail (lifetime `$3F`, no BG test).
- **Boss `$7D` in area `$6E` (s86).** No spawn entry places type `$7D`; the only `ObjInit($7D)` in the ROM (`search_bytes A97D`) is `27:A56D` in **AI `$3A` `AiNpcBossReveal` `27:A555`** (type `$2A`: meta `$4C` = invisible, `obj_coll` `$10` = hittable, HP `$FF`, AI byte `$BA` = no knockback slide). Type `$2A` is placed only in area `$6E` slot `$0E` (h4 p=`$2A`), at the same spot (128, 364) as the NPC in slot `$0D` (h1 p=`$84`); slot `$0F` is h1 p=`$8E`, slot `$10` is the arena trigger `$29`. Each frame: if slot `$0D` is gone (`$049F,X` = **`obj_active` of slot X−1**; the `obj_timer_s1F` label there is a slot-offset alias) → despawn. Otherwise it keeps `obj_meta_dir` = ((player meta + 2) & 3)·2, counts `obj_timer2` down, and when it is 0 and it was **hit** (`obj_flags2` b7) while the player is on screen row 1 with y lo `$28-$AF`: HP = `$FF` again, `obj_timer2` = `$10`, `obj_phase`++, message group `$10` id `obj_phase` + `$0F` (`$10`, `$11`, `$12`) and `sub_state` = `$10`. So the NPC **speaks after each of the first 3 sword hits**. On the frame after the third (phase 3): slots `$0D` and `$0F` get `obj_active` = 0 and this slot becomes **type `$7D`** = `BossAiE6` (CHR R4 `$75`, intro without the area-`$AC` message and orbiters), whose death type `$CF` makes it flee (`BossFleeSpeech`) rather than die.
- **AI `$39` `AiArenaLock` `27:A519`** (type `$29`: meta `$4C`, invisible, no collision; h4 p=`$29` in areas `$6E $AA $AC $B9 $BA`): if slot X−1 is inactive → despawn and `boss_cam_lock` = 0 (releases the camera when the boss in the slot below dies). Phase 0: once onscreen with screen y < `$C0` → phase 1, `boss_cam_lock` = `$FF` (align). Phase 1: while `$07D5` = 0 (not aligned yet) `boss_cam_lock` = `$FF` and `$05A0` (`obj_freeze` of slot 0, written absolute as the boss intros do) = 5; then `boss_cam_lock` = `$10`. It is the camera lock for boss rooms that place their boss in the slot below it (`$AC` `BossAiE6`, `$B9` `AiBoss88`, …).
- **Type `$C2` body part**: AI `$BF` = `$3F` with b7 (no knockback) → `27:A414` = `irq_mode` = 7; `RTS`. It never moves itself: `BossEBMove` `15:A8FF` and the boss phase code position and animate it; its only per-frame job is to keep the boss's IRQ split mode on. Record: `obj_attack` 0, `$0400` = `$FF`, `$0500` = `$0F`, `obj_spawn_param` 1.

#### Player — movement, jump, sword, magic (session 44, `tools/player_dump.py` → `docs/player_tables.txt`) (VERIFIED self-emu, s45: walk speed, charge, swing, jump arc)

**The player is two objects.** Slot 0 (type `$06`, AI `$03` `PlayerMove` `26:9D9A`) is the **ground body**: it reads the pad, moves, does BG collision, holds the jump timer, and is the position that `ExitLookup`/edge links/camera use. Slot 1 (type `$04`, AI `$01` `PlayerUpdate` `26:9B45`) is the **drawn body**: each frame it is placed at slot 0's x/y plus the jump height, picks the facing and pose, and fires the sword. AI `$02` (`PlayerMoveLocked` `26:9D81`) is `PlayerMove` with all pad vars zeroed (cutscenes). In area `$58` it changes itself to AI `$03` and forces `pad_dir`=4 (walk down). Both AIs run every frame in slot order (0 then 1).

`PlayerMove` `26:9D9A` (slot 0, X=0), in order:
1. Unless status `obj1_flash` b7 or (`obj1_flash`&`$0F`)=4: `obj_behav`(`$06E0`) = `pad_dir` (slot 1 reads it for the facing). Meta id `$A7`, or `$4C` (empty) when `obj_flags` b6 (sinking) is set **and** the jump timer is 0, i.e. **on the ground in a sink cell** *(s77 correction from `26:9DAC-9DB7` + emulator; s44 said "in the air")*.
2. **Jump timer** `$0620` (slot 0 `obj_timer2`): if ≠0, DEC. At `$11` it plays sound `$31`. While ≥`$1E`, a held direction is latched into `$0640`, and while airborne `pad_dir` is replaced by that latched value (**the jump direction is locked for the whole jump**).
3. `PlayerMagic` `27:A022` (A button, below).
   *(s111 port notes, all checked by `fuzz_player.json`: the ROM's step 9 test is "`$0620`&`$F0` = 0", so the freeze + MP drain also runs in the last 16 frames of a jump, and the INC + `SpendMp` only when `$0620`≠0; a failed `SpendMp` there returns from `PlayerMove`. The final probe at `9EFD` is `LDA #0 / LDY obj_timer2 / BNE`, and `ObjBgProbe`'s cache test is a `BPL` right after `STA $14` (the caller's N flag), so a jump timer ≥ `$80` (never in play) probes in cached mode.)*
4. **Sword charge** (B held = `pad_prev` b6, sword equipped, `obj_phase` <`$0C` or =`$11`): `obj_phase`=`$12`. Every 4th frame (`$08`&3=0) `orbit_level` `$06C0`+1. At 8 `orbit_burst_timer`=`$0E`; every multiple of 8 plays sound `$26`. The cap is `SwordChargeCap` `26:9D7E` = `08 10 18`[`sword_power`]. So level 1/2/3 takes 32/64/96 frames.
5. `obj_phase` (swing counter, `26:9E39`): while B is held it is reset to `$12` every frame. With a direction held (`pad_dir` b7=0) the pose code is skipped (walk pose stays). With no direction: on the ground → meta 4 (swing pose) + DEC, so it reads `$11` at end of frame; in the air → slot 1 meta 0. *(s45 correction: s44 said "meta 0 while held"; the emulator shows meta 4+facing on the ground.)* When B is released, slot 1 meta 4 (swing) with `obj_anim_s01`=phase, and phase DECs to 0 (18-frame swing). On the ground `obj_bg_mask`=`$32`. Walking with no swing: `obj_anim_s01` DEC (walk cycle).
6. Status b6 (`obj1_flash`): `obj_bg_mask`=`$34`.
7. **Move**: `obj_meta_dir`=`pad_dir`, `obj_timer` DEC (it is the 8-frame step phase), `ObjMoveSlide` `26:98D7`. Blocked → `obj_param2`=8 and keep trying the old direction for up to 8 frames (corner slide). If `obj_param1` b7 (forced move), the diagonal is reduced by `DiagToCardinal` `27:A017` (`FF 02 02 02 FF 06 06 06`: up/down are ignored).
8. Every 4th frame `26:8D59`. Then `ObjBgProbe` at the new position (A=`$40` on the ground, 0 in the air) → `$20` = metatile attribute bits.
9. Ground only: `$20` b1 or `obj_spawn_param_s01` b1 → `$0640`=`obj_behav`, and if `$0620`≠0 INC it (cancels the DEC: the timer is frozen). If MP≠0, every 16 frames `SpendMp(1)`. `$20` b6 → `obj_bg_mask` b6 → `obj_flags` b6 (**sink**: `ObjStepVector` then uses speed−2).
10. `$20` b5 (**conveyor/current**) → `PlayerConveyor` `26:9FD2`: move in `obj_param1`&`$0F` at speed `obj_aux` `$0660` (it ramps up 0→9, +1 per 16 `$08` ticks; while `pad_dir` = 4 (DOWN held) it jumps to at least 6; for the player the direction is always 4 = down, s77), using `$08` as the step phase. Without b5, `obj_aux` decays every 4 frames, and the last `$20` is kept in `$0641`.

`PlayerUpdate` `26:9B45` (slot 1, X=1), in order:
1. B new + `use_item_sel`≠0 + `sub_state`=8 → `sub_state`=6 (`ItemUseMenu`).
2. `PlayerSetSpeed` `26:9BF6` (A → slot 0 `obj_flags2` low nibble = **move speed**): normally slot 1's own speed (type init). Status 4 (`obj1_flash`&`$0F`=4) → speed 2 + meta `$53`. Status b6 → speed 9 + the `9B41` pose (`88 C2 8A EF` by facing; ends when `$0561`\|`$0641` & 6 = 0). Status b7 → speed 5 + meta += `2C/30/34/38`[`obj1_flash` b5-4].
3. `PlayerPlaceBody` `26:9CEF`: height = `JumpHeightTable` `26:9D4D`[`$0620`]. In the air `obj_coll`=`$60` and `obj_flags` b6 cleared; on the ground `obj_coll`=`$50` and slot 1 takes slot 0's `obj_flags` b3-7 and keeps its own b0-2 *(s111 correction from `26:9D16`; s44 said "copies b0-2")*. Height `$F9` (index `$21`) → sound `$56` + `boss_cam_lock`=`$07D6`=`$10`. Slot 1 = slot 0 pos + (0, height) via `26:9824` (signed add with the 240-line page wrap).
4. `PlayerFacing` `27:A1C9`: slot 1 meta low 2 bits = facing (0 up, 1 right, 2 down, 3 left). If `obj_behav`≠`$FF` and `FacingKeep` `27:A211`[(2·facing − dir)&7] = 0 (the pad is more than 45° off the facing), facing = slot 0 `obj_meta_dir`>>1. So **diagonals keep the current facing**. *(s111 correction: `obj_behav`=`$FF` does not skip the update. `BEQ $A1F9` lands on `BNE $A210` with Z=1 from the `CMP #$FF`, so "no direction" always takes slot 0's `obj_meta_dir`; found by `fuzz_player.json`.)* In area `$5F` with `obj_phase`≠0, facing is forced to 0.
5. Overlay (`obj_overlay_id`): `$0714`=0 → `08+facing` (`$4C` if meta b2); else `$0C`+meta&7.
6. **Sword**: `obj_phase` in `$04-$0F` (and slot 0 AI `$0B` only at `$0F`): `SpawnShot($19)` (slash), slot 2 `$0502` = `SwordElemBit` `26:9C66`[`equip_sword`] (`00 01 02 04 08 00`), `$03E2` = `26:8BD2`[sword] (`00 01 02 04 08 10`). `obj_phase`=`$10` (release frame): **charged shot**. With no sword → nothing. With Crystalis (5), only in area `$5F` (`SpawnShot($13)`); the charge is otherwise ignored. That answers the session-20 question: Crystalis never reads its charge level outside area `$5F`. Otherwise level = `orbit_level`>>3 (+1 if 0 and `$0716`=`$0F` and status ≠1). Level 3 needs MP > `SwordMpCost` `26:8BD8`[sword] (`00 08 10 10 28 00`), else it drops to 2 (and `orbit_level`−1) *(s111: `CMP mp_cur / BCC`, so MP = cost also drops to 2; a failed `SpendMp` returns from `PlayerUpdate` before the `orbit_level` reset)*. Shot id = (sword−1)·4 + level, Y=facing·2. A successful level-3 shot calls `SpendMp(cost)`. Then `orbit_level`=`orbit_burst_timer`=0.

**`PlayerMagic` `27:A022`** — Y = `$0712` (selected magic 0-11; Y=9 if `$0712`=0 and `$0716`=`$12`). The button var is `$43`+`27:A092`[Y] (`$08` → `pad_new` edge, `$00` → `pad_prev` held); b7 (**A**) must be set. Blocked by status 4 (sound `$4E`), and while airborne except for Y=8/9. Status b7 → `sub_state`=`$19`. Dispatch `JmpTable27_A072`:

| Y | handler | effect |
|---|---|---|
| 0, 10, 11 | `A0A2` | none |
| 1 | `A0A3` | MP≥2: HP+1 twice per frame up to `player_hp_max`. Every 4 HP (or at max): `SpendMp(2)` + `SpawnShot($12)` |
| 2 | `A0D7` | slot `$0C` free: `SpendMp(4)`, `SpawnShot($10)` |
| 3 | `A18E` | not in areas `$58-$5F`, MP≥8 → `sub_state` `$15` `SubstTelepathySelect` |
| 4 | `A1A1` | MP≥`$14` → `sub_state` `$14` `SubstTeleportMenu` |
| 5 | `A17D` | status ≠0: `SpendMp($18)` → `sub_state` `$18` (`30:BB39`) |
| 6 | `A0F4` | ground only (else sound `$4E`). Held: `SpendMp(1)` every 8 frames + `SpawnShot($11)` each frame |
| 7 | `A161` | not in areas `$58-$5F`, MP≥`$14` → `sub_state` `$17` `SubstChangeForm` |
| 8 | `A112` | held: `SpendMp(1)` every 16 frames. Starts a jump (`$0620`=`$20`, `obj_bg_mask`=`$34`, sound `$30`). It **re-INCs at `$12`, so it hovers at the peak (−21 px) while A is held** (flight) |
| 9 | `A143` | on the ground: jump (`$0620`=`$20`, latch `pad_dir`, sound `$30`) |

`SpendMp` `26:9AE8` (A=cost): if MP=0 or MP<cost → `sub_state`=`$1C` (`30:CC17`, out of MP) and return from the *caller*. Shared fail sound: `27:A1BB` = `PlaySound($4E)`.

**Constants** (all from the ROM; full tables in `docs/player_tables.txt`):

| what | where | value |
|---|---|---|
| step vector | `ObjStepVector` `26:8480`, ptr `84BC`/`84CC`, dx offset `84DC` | speed n, dir d: dy=T[d·8+(`obj_timer`&7)], dx=T[…+off]. Pixels per 8 frames along an axis: speed 0-10 = 4 6 8 10 12 14 16 20 40 52 64 (8-dir tables, `$50` bytes = 10 dirs × 8, dx offset `$10` = 2 dirs ahead). *(s86 correction:)* speeds 11-15 are **16-direction** tables (`$A0` bytes = 20 dirs × 8 at `$885C/$88FC/$899C/$8A3C/$8ADC`, dx offset `$20` = 4 dirs ahead = 90°), used with 16-dir headings (`AiHomingFlyer`, bosses `$E3 $E6 $E7 $E8 $EB`, `AiCurveShot`, `AiBounceShot`). Radius per 8 frames: 11 = 56, 12 = 14, 13 = 20, 14 = 24, 15 = 16 (`tools/player_dump.py` speed section). So `AiHomingFlyer`'s levels 0-3 (`0E 0F 0D 0C`) = 24 / 16 / 20 / 14 px per 8 frames, and `BossAiE6`'s HP speeds `0E 0D 0F` = 24 / 20 / 16. **Quirk:** `obj_flags` b6 (sinking) subtracts 2, which turns 16-dir speeds 12/13 into the 8-dir tables 10/11 (a 16-dir heading then indexes past the 8-dir table, and 13 → 11 is the fast 56 px one); no flyer is expected to sink |
| sink | `26:848B` | `obj_flags` b6 → speed−2 (min 0) |
| jump arc | `JumpHeightTable` `26:9D4D` | idx `$20`→0 (DEC/frame): 0 −3 −6 −8 −10 −12 −13 −15 −16 −18 −19 −20 −20 −21×6 −20 −20 −19 −18 −16 −15 −13 −12 −10 −8 −6 −3 0 0 0. Entries `$21-$30` = `F9 F0 … 92 76 84` = **fall-in from a pit above** (`AreaPitFall` sets `$0620`=`$30`; s46 correction, was "rising exit"); `$21` plays the landing sound `$56` |
| jump length | `27:A14D` | 32 frames, sound `$30` start, `$31` at `$11` |
| charge rate | `26:9E0D` | +1 per 4 frames, cap `08 10 18` |
| swing | `26:9E08` | `obj_phase`=`$12`, slash shot spawned at phases `$04-$0F` |
| corner slide | `26:9ED1` | 8 frames |
| facing keep | `27:A211` | `01 01 00 00 00 00 00 01` |

**Emulator check (session 45, `tools/emu/player_probe.py`, self-emu, from `gameplay_first.state`):**
- Walking: speed nibble 6 → +30 px in 16 frames down (the first frame is idle) = 2 px/frame = 16 px per 8 frames, which matches the step table. Left/right hit walls in that room.
- Slot order: slot 0 (`PlayerMove`) runs before slot 1 (`PlayerUpdate`) every frame. In the first ~5 frames after loading the state, `9D9A` is not reached (slot 0 is still on its locked/cutscene path), so input there is ignored.
- B with no sword: nothing happens (no phase, no charge).
- B with `--poke 0711=01 0719=00` (sword 1, power 0): `orbit_level` +1 every 4 frames, capped at 8 after 32 frames; phase `$11`, meta 6 (4 + facing down). On release the phase goes `$11`→0, one per frame, then meta goes back to 2, and `orbit_level` is reset to 0.
- Jump (`--poke 0712=09`, A): `$0620`=32 counts down to 0 over 32 frames. Slot 1 y offset per frame = −3 −6 −8 −10 −12 −13 −15 −16 −18 −19 −20 −20 −21×6 … 0, exactly `JumpHeightTable`. Slot 0 y never changes.

**Emulator check, magic + charged shot (session 76, `tools/emu/magic_probe.py`, self-emu, from `gameplay_first.state`; hooks `SpawnShot` `26:972D` and `SpendMp` `26:9AE8`):** every case below matches the `PlayerMagic` table and the sword step above. Input acts 1 frame after the pad frame (pad latency).
- Magic 1 (HP 32/48): HP +2 per frame, `SpendMp(2)` + `SpawnShot($12)` every 4 HP. 32→48 cost 8 MP (4 groups; the 4th lands on max).
- Magic 2: `SpendMp(4)` + `SpawnShot($10)` dir 4 on the A edge (held A casts once). A second press is ignored while slot `$0C` is still in use (about 20 frames after the cast; a press 21 frames later works).
- Magic 3/4/7: `sub_state` → `$15`/`$14`/`$17`, no MP spent at the press. Magic 5 with status 3 (poison): `SpendMp($18)` (34→10), `sub_state` `$18`.
- Magic 6 held 33 frames: `SpawnShot($11)` 32× (every frame), `SpendMp(1)` 4× (every 8 frames).
- Magic 8 held 60 frames: `$0620` starts at `$20`, slot-1 offset follows `JumpHeightTable` to −21, then holds at `$0620`=`$13` / −21 px while A is held. `SpendMp(1)` every 16 frames. On release the rest of the arc plays out (−21 … 0 over 19 frames).
- Sword shot at B release (`sword_power` P, cap `08 10 18`): level = `orbit_level`>>3, shot id = (sword−1)·4 + level. Sword 1: tap → `$00`, 40 f at P0 → `$01`, 100 f at P2 → `$03` + `SpendMp(8)`. With MP 4 that drops to `$02` (no spend). Sword 3, P1, 70 f → `$0A`. Sword 4, P2, 100 f → `$0E` (level 3 refused: cost `$28` > MP 34). Sword 2 tap → `$04`. Accessory `$0716`=`$0F` turns a level-0 tap into level 1 (`$01`), but not with status 1 (`$00`). Crystalis (5) outside `$5F`: no release shot. Every release also spawns 12 slash shots `$19` (phases `$0F`→`$04`). **Level 0 is a real shot id** (the plain release shot), not "none".

**Static quirks:** speed 4's DL and UL entries are not mirror images of DR/UR (DL sums to (−12,+9), UL to (−9,−12) per 8 frames), because the `$862C` table and its +16 dx offset overlap the next direction. Speed 9 has the same problem (DL −53/+37). **s76: both are used diagonally, so the port must keep the table bytes exactly as they are.** It reads them from `prg.bin`, so it does. `tools/speed_users.py`: 14 types start at speed 4 (`$4B $4F` AI `$22`, `$7B $8C` `$26`, `$99 $9A` `$27`, `$82` `$29`, `$84 $95` `$2E`, `$7C` `$40`, `$92 $5D $77 $87` bank-15 AIs `$C1/$CC/$CD/$CE`), and `15:B1B7` sets speed 4 at runtime. `AiShooterChase`/`AiBurrower`/`AiDormant` end in `AiChase`, and its detour mode turns dir±1 (45°), so they walk diagonally at speed 4. The sink rule (speed−2) also turns the player's speed 6 into 4 on a b6 tile. Speed 9: types `$1F $32 $33 $FD` and the player in status b6 (`PlayerSetSpeed(9)` `26:9B82`). `tools/emu/step_probe.py` (hook `26:8497`, counts (effective speed, dir, AI)) with `--poke 0710=40 0561=02 0641=02` and DOWN+LEFT logs 2 speed-9 dir-5 (down-left) steps for AI `$03` before b6 clears. From `gameplay_first.state` alone (no enemies in the room) only speed 6 shows.

#### World interaction — BG collision, hazards, damage, exits, camera (session 46, `tools/bg_coll_dump.py` → `docs/bg_coll.txt`) (s46 static; **self-emu checked s77**, see the "s77 emulator check" list below)

**BG collision attributes are a separate table, not `MetatileAttr`.** `MetatileAttr` `9:B000` holds only the 2-bit BG palette. Collision uses one **256-byte attribute page per tileset in bank 9**: page hi = `$69` (k1 byte 4), lo = metatile id. 11 pages are in use, `9:B300-BDFF` (`$69` = `$B3` + tileset index; tilesets `$08` and `$28` share `$B5`). This also answers the session-21 question about `9:B300-BDFF`: it is live collision data.

**`ObjBgProbe` `26:9A30`** (A = mode, position in `pos_*` `$34-$37`, X = slot) → `$20` = attribute byte:
- A b7 set (**cached mode**): if the probe point is in the same 16×16 cell as the object's current position, return `obj_flags & $50` with C=1 (no lookup).
- Lookup: grid byte G = `screen_grid` `$6300`[`pos_y_hi`·8 + `pos_x_hi`]; R7 = G>>5 (bank 0-7), page = `$A0` \| (G&`$1F`); M = page[(`pos_y_lo`&`$F0`) \| (`pos_x_lo`>>4)]; `$68`=M; R7=9; attr = (`$68`) = `9:($69·256 + M)`.
- If attr b3 and the screen is visited (`visited_map` `$62F0`[y_hi] & `BitMask` `26:9AE0`[x_hi]): M = (`$6A`)[M] (the `$BE00` substitution table, same as the renderer) and attr is re-read for the new M. So **opened/destroyed walls are b3 metatiles whose substitute has no wall bit**.
- R7 is restored to `$1B` (bank 27). **Quirk:** the probe always reads the page from bank G>>5 at `$A000`, so in `$62FF` areas whose row render takes the `$8000` half from bank 10, collision still comes from the normal bank.
- A b6 set (**update mode**): `obj_flags` b4/b6 = attr & `obj_bg_mask` & `$50` (b4 = draw behind BG, b6 = sink). Returns C=0.

**Attribute bits** (counts over all 11 pages, `docs/bg_coll.txt`; values in use: `00 01 02 06 08 0A 0E 10 11 16 18 20 22 30 40 42 46 4A 4E 62 C0`):

| bit | count | meaning | evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| b0 `$01` | 14 | **pit** (fall to the area below) and wall for non-player objects | `31:F06D`: on the ground, slot 1 `$0641` b0 and `obj_spawn_b2_s01`=0 → `sub_state`=1, `area_entry` = (`area_entry`&`$1F`)\|`$60`; object mask `$27` |
| b1 `$02` | 1743 | **low obstacle**: blocks the player's body on the ground *and during a normal jump*; only flight (magic 8) and the warp-exit status cross it *(s77 correction, emulator)* | player `obj_bg_mask` `$32` (`26:9E67/9E79`, kept through a magic-9 jump `27:A143`, which never touches the mask); `$34` (no b1) only from flight `27:A12C` and status b6 `26:9E8A`. Ground probe over b1 freezes the jump timer (`PlayerMove` step 9), so the player cannot land on it |
| b2 `$04` | 1536 | **wall**: blocks in the air too | in both player masks; `$06` = plain wall |
| b3 `$08` | 129 | substitutable metatile (visited/opened) | above |
| b4 `$10` | 211 | foreground: object drawn behind BG | copied to `obj_flags` b4 |
| b5 `$20` | 18 | **conveyor / current** for the player; wall for objects | `PlayerConveyor` `26:9FD2`; object mask `$27` |
| b6 `$40` | 107 | **sink** (speed −2, sinking pose) | copied to `obj_flags` b6 |
| b7 `$80` | 17 | **damage floor** (always with b6: `$C0`, e.g. a poison swamp) | `PlayerStatusTick` below |

**Move step `ObjMoveStep` `26:9947`** (the inner step of `ObjMoveSlide`): copies the object position to `pos_*`, masks `obj_meta_dir` to 0-7, adds the `ObjStepVector` step, saves the candidate position in `$1C-$1F`, then probes a list of points around it from **`BgProbePoints` `26:9907`** (8 bytes per direction: signed (dx, dy) pairs, cumulative, `00 00` ends):

| dir | points relative to the candidate position |
|---|---|
| 0 up | (−4,−5) (4,−5) |
| 1 up-right | (−4,−5) (4,−5) (4,3) |
| 2 right | (4,−5) (4,3) |
| 3 down-right | (4,−5) (4,3) (−4,3) |
| 4 down | (−4,3) (4,3) |
| 5 down-left | (4,3) (−4,3) (−4,−3) — **quirk: dy −6, not −8**, so the top-left corner is not tested |
| 6 left | (−4,−5) (−4,3) |
| 7 up-left | (−4,3) (−4,−5) (4,−5) |

So the BG footprint is an 8×8 box from (x−4, y−5) to (x+4, y+3). Per point:
- **Slot 0 (player ground body)** first runs `ObjScreenEdge` `26:99FF`: blocked (attr = `obj_flags`&`$A0` \| 6, C=1) if x − `scroll_shadow0` is < `$0A` or ≥ `$FC`, or y − `cam_y_lo` (240-line wrap) is < `$18` or ≥ `$D0`. Otherwise `ObjBgProbe`(`$80`), `$25` \|= attr. Then blocked when attr & `$06` & `obj_bg_mask` ≠ 0. Special case: knockback (`obj_flags2` = `$08`) with status b6 skips cached points and stops (C=0, no move) on an empty attr or one with b2/b5.
- **Other slots**: `ObjBgProbe`(`$80`), blocked when attr & `$27` & `obj_bg_mask` ≠ 0.
- All points clear → `PLA PLA` (returns straight to `ObjMoveSlide`'s caller, skipping its retries), commits `$1C-$1F` to the object position, `ObjBgProbe`(`$40`) updates `obj_flags` b4/b6, swaps `$23`/`obj_meta_dir`, C=0. Blocked → RTS to `ObjMoveSlide`, which tries dir±1 for diagonals and returns C=1.
- *(s80, found while porting; fuzz-checked 200/200)*: `ObjMoveSlide` ignores the carry `ObjMoveStep` returns. Only the `PLA PLA` commit counts as success, so the slot-0 knockback early stop (C=0, no move) acts exactly like "blocked": the diagonal retries still run and `ObjMoveSlide` ends with `SEC`. The only user of `obj_flags2` = `$08` here is `ObjUpdateAll`'s knockback (it forces speed 8 for slots ≠ 1), so that special case only fires for **slot 0** knocked back during flight / warp exit (`obj1_flash` b6).

**`PlayerStatusTick` `31:EF55`-`F0A3`** (called once per frame from `SubstPlay` `30:CB68`) — hazard part:
- Every 128 frames (`$08` = 0 or `$80`) with status (`obj1_flash`&`$0F`) = 3 (**poison**) → damage.
- Else, unless `equip_accessory` `$0716` = `$13`: on the ground (`$0620`=0) and (`obj_spawn_param_s01` \| `$0641`) b7 (**damage floor**) → damage every 32 frames.
- Area `$1A`, unless `$0716` = `$0D`: damage every 8 frames (an area-wide hazard).
- Damage = `player_hp` −4 (floor 0), SFX `$36`, BG colours `$6151-$6153` = `$30` (white flash) for one `WaitFrame`, then `26:8CC0` + `PaletteBuild` restore the palette.
- `$0716` is set only by `30:C99C` (new game, 0) and read by the stat recalc `30:C010` (`$0E` doubles the level part of attack, `$10` of defence, `$14` of the `$0714` stat), `PlayerMagic` (`$12`) and here, so it is the **equipped accessory** item id. (Item names → the item tables in "GiveItem / item-use dispatch".)

**Player damage — `HitPlayer` `26:9357`** (`HitHandlerTable` handler 2; X = 1 = player, Y = the touching object `$0D-$1E`):
1. Y `obj_spawn_b2` = `$FF` (an **enemy shot**) → `HitPlayerShot` `26:92BF` by Y `obj_spawn_param` (= the shot's effect):

   | param | effect | blocked by |
   |---|---|---|
   | 0 | normal damage (step 2) | — |
   | 1 | status 1 (**paralysis**), msg `$20`/`$1A`, SFX `$44`, `sub_state`=`$10` (message) | `$0714` = 6 or 8 |
   | 2 | status 2 (**stone**): slot 0 `obj_freeze`=`$FF`, sprite pal 0 `$07E4`=`$4D` (grey) + `PaletteBuild`, msg `$20`/`$1B`, SFX `$45` | status already 2, `$0714` = 4 or 8 |
   | 3 | the shot turns into type `$0B` (`ObjInit` via `FarCall_C25D`) | — |
   | ≥4 | `obj1_flash` = 4 (**status 4**: speed 2, meta `$53`, no magic; the hi nibble is cleared too), stat recalc `30:C008` | — |

   Params 1/2/≥4 despawn the shot (`26:92F5`). *(s113, fuzz-verified: when param 2 actually stones the player, the `PaletteBuild` call leaves Y = 4 and `92F5` clears slot 4's `obj_active` instead — the stone shot is not removed.)* So `$0714` is the **shield** (it also feeds the third stat in the recalc) and `$0713` the **armor** (R2 player CHR + defence table `30:8BC0`).
2. `PlayerPoisonRoll` `26:927E`: if the player is not invulnerable, armor `$0713` ≠ 7 and Y `obj_hitflags` b7 (**poisonous** enemy): `Rand64` < 4 (1 in 16) → status 3 (**poison**), msg `$20`/`$19`, SFX `$36`, `sub_state`=`$10`, start invulnerability.
3. Slot 0 AI `$0B` (player in the **sparring duel**, s77 — was "held/captured"): `obj_aux_s01`−1; every 4th: player and slot `$0D` y += 8 (both pushed back); SFX `$5F`; no damage.
4. By Y's AI (`obj_active`&`$7F`): `$06`/`$07` → nothing; `$7B` → `PickupGold` `26:946D`; `$38`/`$3C` → **`RidePlatform` `26:943F`**: on the ground and the platform not frozen → `obj_spawn_b2_s01` `$0541` = Y (cleared every frame by `ObjCollideAll`) and slot 0 is moved one step in the platform's direction at speed 2 (`ObjMoveSlide`). `$0541` ≠ 0 also **cancels the pit fall** (`31:F073`), so these AIs are platforms over pits.
5. **Invulnerable** (`obj_flags_s01` b2, the half-rate flicker draw flag) → return.
6. `HitKnockbackSet` (player gets the toucher's direction; area `$1A` with `boss_cam_lock` b7 → no knockback direction), copied to slot 0 `obj_meta_dir`/`obj_flags2`; slot 0 `obj_act_timer` `$0640`=`$FF`.
7. **damage = Y `obj_attack` − player `obj_defense` `$0401`** (8-bit). If the result has b7 set (defence ≥ attack, **or damage ≥ 128**) → SFX `$3A` (no damage), no invulnerability. Else, unless `player_level` `$0421` = `$11`: `player_hp` `$03C1` −= damage (floor 0), SFX `$5F`, **`PlayerInvuln` `26:9431`**: `$071A` = `$1E` and `obj_flags_s01` b2 set. Damage 0 still gives the 30 frames.
8. Shots (`obj_spawn_b2`=`$FF`) despawn on contact. Then `HudHpBar` `26:8CC0` redraws the HP bar (`$6060-$6070`).

**Rest of `PlayerStatusTick` `31:EF55`** (after the hazard part, in order):
- Status 1: when `orbit_level` reaches 4 it is reset to 0 + SFX `$31` (no sword charge while paralysed).
- Status 2: when slot 0 `obj_freeze` has run out → status cleared, `$07E4`=0, `PaletteBuild`. While still frozen, magic 5 + A (new) in `sub_state` 8 → `SpendMp($18)`, `sub_state`=`$18` (the cure, same as `PlayerMagic` 5).
- Accessory `$11`, standing still (`pad_dir` b7): every 64 frames MP+1 up to `mp_max` (HUD redraw `26:8E46` A=4).
- Armor `$08`, standing still: every 16 frames HP+1 up to max (`HudHpBar`).
- Area `$52`: `DEC $04FE` (slot `$1E` `obj_anim`) every frame.
- **Pit fall** `31:F068`: on the ground, `$0641` b0 and `$0541`=0 → `sub_state`=1, `area_entry` = (`area_entry`&`$1F`)\|`$60`, `$0641`=0.
- **Invulnerability** `31:F089`: `$071A` ≠ 0 → DEC, keep `obj_flags_s01` b2; at 0 → clear b2. So **invulnerability = 30 frames of half-rate flicker**.

**Death**: `30:CB84` (start of `SubstPlay`'s per-frame body, before `PlayerStatusTick`): `player_hp` = 0 → `sub_state` = 3 (the death substate seen in s43).

`SubstPlay` `30:CB62` frame order: `ReadPads` → `30:CB84` (death / START / SELECT menus) → `PlayerStatusTick` → `ObjUpdateAll` → `CameraUpdate` → sprite pass `28:800E` → `ObjCollideAll` `26:8F6E` → `JMP 31:E0B8`.

**Area entry dispatch — `JmpTable31_E3D9`** (8 entries, index = (`area_entry` `$6D` & `$E0`)>>5, dispatcher `31:E40A`). This resolves one of the s22 untraced dispatch tables:

| `$6D` | handler | meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `$00-$1F` | `E422` | normal: player at k2 entry record `$6D`&`$1F` |
| `$20-$3F` | `E51C` | keep the current position (s9) |
| `$40-$5F` | `E533` | `SaveGame` (`23:BC00`) + `E845` + `E61B` (teleport path; "teleporting saves the game") |
| `$60-$7F` | `AreaPitFall` `E544` | **pit fall** (below) |
| `$80-$DF` | `0000` ×3 | null (would crash) |
| `$E0-$FF` | `E41C` | no position record (`$6D`=`$FF`) |

**Pit fall → `AreaEdgeLink`** (k5): `PlayerStatusTick` sets `sub_state`=1 and `$6D`\|=`$60` when the player stands on a b0 metatile. `AreaPitFall` `31:E544` then loops one frame at a time: probe 8 px below slot 1 (`ObjBgProbe`(`$40`)); while the attr still has b0, move slots 1 and 0 down 8 px and run the sprite pass. Once below the pit tiles → `AreaEdgeLink` `31:E573` (k5 record match, format in the sub-table list above). On a match: y += 16, the new screen x/y hi, `$6C`=area, `AreaLoadMap`, slot 0 `$0640`=`$FF`, and **jump timer `$0620`=`$30`**, so the player drops in from above: `JumpHeightTable` entries `$30`→`$21` are the **fall-in heights** (s44 called them a "rising exit"), and index `$21` (`$F9`) plays the landing thud (sound `$56` + 16-frame camera shake). Status 4 is cured by the fall. No k5 match within 256 bytes (also every area without k5, where the read starts at the k0 bytes) → `sub_state`=8 and `JMP $8C49` (A=4), meant as the `26:8C49` fade-in. **s116 correction:** `AreaSubTable` has switched `$8000` to banks 10/11, so this actually jumps into bank-10 area data at `10:8C49` (see the s116 note under "Web Port Fixes").

**Exits** (k3, `ExitLookup` `31:EB09`, s8) run every frame from `CameraTick` `31:E8F6` after the camera, **except while `boss_cam_lock` is 1-`$7F`**.

**Camera** — `CameraTick` `31:E8F6` → `CameraFollow` `31:E9AD` (step) → `CameraUpdate` `31:E90A` (apply):
- `CameraFollow` computes the step (`$30`, `$31`) = (dx, dy). `boss_cam_lock` b7 → align mode (s28). Otherwise, with player rel = slot 0 position − camera `$3C-$3F` (y with the 240-line wrap):
  - **Y**: dead zone rel y `$68-$77` (104-119); outside it, dy = rel − `$68` (above) or rel − `$78` (below), clamped to ±3 by `CamStepClamp` `31:EA2F`.
  - **X while left/right is held** (`pad_prev` & 3) **or during knockback** (slot 0 `obj_flags2` b7): dead zone rel x `$68-$9F` (104-159), else ±3 the same way.
  - **X otherwise (re-centre)**: target = (x & `$F0`) + (8 if slot 0 `obj_screen_x` is outside `$08-$87`); rel = target − cam x; outside `$68-$98` the camera drifts **1 px per frame** toward it.
- `CameraUpdate`: candidate = `$3C-$3F` + step (`26:9824`), then `CameraClampX` `31:EA72`:
  - X: area width 0 → 0. Otherwise range **−8 (`$FF:F8`) … `area_w`·256**.
  - Y: area height 0 → 0 and `$3B`=`$FC` (`$3B`=0 in area `$5F`). Otherwise `$3B`=8 and range **0 … `area_h`·240 + `$28`** (the bottom screen scrolls 40 more lines because the HUD covers the bottom of the view).
  - Clamping an axis zeroes its step.
- BG column/row render (`BgColRender` `31:EC87` / `BgRowRender` `31:EB91`) when the camera crosses an 8-px boundary on that axis; `$2F` ≠ 0 → `30:C739`.
- `$3A` = **horizontal view offset**: when the camera steps right (dx > 0) it moves 1 px/frame toward −8 (`$F8`), otherwise toward +1; it is 0 in one-screen-wide areas. The drawn camera `$02-$05` = committed camera `$3C-$3F` + (`$3A`, `$3B`).
- `boss_cam_lock` 1-`$7F` skips all of this (DEC, then the `$07D6` shake at `31:EADF`).

**s77 emulator check (`tools/emu/world_probe.py`, tools.emu from `gameplay_first`/area pokes; not FCEUX):**
- **Collision overlay (f)**: `python3 tools/render_level.py AREA… --coll [--visited]` tints each 16×16 cell by class and writes `gfx/areas/area_NNN[_v]_coll.png` plus `area_NNN[_v]_coll.txt`, one char per cell: `.` 00, `D` b7, `p` b0, `c` b5, `#` b2, `o` b1-only, `s` b6, `f` b4-only, `v` b3-only. `world_probe.py --map` prints the same legend from live tools.emu RAM (`$6300` grid + `$69`), so a port-side dump of the same chars can be diffed per area. `coll_attr` follows `ObjBgProbe`: the page always comes from bank G>>5, never the bank-10 render page (the s46 quirk). Checked: area `$6A`'s static rows match the emulator's `--map` rows.
- **Wall blocking** (start room area 0, `$06` cells): walking left stops at x=`$84` against a wall cell ending at `$7F` and at x=`$64` against one ending at `$5F` → the left edge of the BG box is x−4, as in `BgProbePoints`.
- **Camera X** (area 14, 8×8 screens): holding RIGHT (2 px/frame), the camera starts moving the frame rel x reaches `$A0` and then keeps rel = `$A0` (dead zone ≤ `$9F`). `cam_ofs_x` goes `01 → 00 → … → F8` at 1 px/frame while scrolling right and back `F8 → … → 01` at 1 px/frame once stopped. After release the **re-centre drift is 1 px/frame** and stops exactly when (x&`$F0`)+8 − cam = `$98` (x=`$153`, cam `$B3`→`$C0`). Walking left from there does not move the camera while rel ≥ `$68`.
- **Camera Y**: walking down, the camera holds rel y = `$78` (dy = rel − `$78`), and page wrap is 240 lines (`$3E4` + 12 → `$400`).
- **Clamps** (camera poked out of range, area 14 `area_w`=`area_h`=7): x `$FF80` → `$FFF8` (−8), x `$0980` → `$0700` (`area_w`·256), y `$FF10` → `$0000`, y `$0850`/`$0780`/`$0729` → `$0728` (`area_h` page, lo `$28`). A poked y lo ≥ `$F0` is not caught (invalid input, never produced by the game).
- **Low obstacle `$02`** (area 14, player poked to (`$318`,`$2B8`) below a band of `$02` cells starting at y `$2A0`): walking UP stops at y=`$2A6` (the next 2-px step would put y−5 at `$29F`). A **magic-9 jump** with UP latched (`$0640`) runs the full `$20`-frame arc on slot 1 while slot 0 stays at `$2A6`: mask `$32` still blocks b1 in the air. **Flight** (magic 8, A+UP held) writes `obj_bg_mask`=`$34` at `27:A131`; slot 0 then moves 2 px/frame straight through the `$02` rows (`player_bg_attr` reads `02` in the air) and the timer hovers at `$13`.
- **Damage floor** (area 14, player poked onto a `$C0` cell at (`$288`,`$258`), standing): `player_hp` −4 at `31:EF9F` when `$08` (the frame counter, **decremented** at `30:CAC5`) hits `$E0`, `$C0`, … = every 32 ticks; `$6151` = `$30` at `31:EFAB`, restored by `26:8CB6` next frame. The hit frame's `WaitFrame` and the restore frame do not tick `$08`, so in real frames the period is **34** (f2429/2463/2497). Accessory `$13` (`$0716`): no damage over 100 frames. **Area `$1A`**: −4 every 8 ticks = **10 real frames** (f2402/2412/2422); accessory `$0D`: no damage over 60 frames.
- **Enemy contact** (area 14 after RIGHT×100 + DOWN×100: slot `$11` AI `$26`, attack `$16`, poked onto the player at (`$153`,`$43E`)): `HitPlayer` `26:9357` runs once (X=1, Y=`$11`); `player_hp` 48 → 27 (−21 = `$16` − player defence `$01`) at `26:9413`, `$0640`=`$FF` at `26:93D7`, `invuln_timer` = `$1E` and counts down to 0 over 30 frames at `31:F091`. **Knockback** = 7 frames × 5 px in the toucher's direction (here up, y `$440` → `$41D`). With player defence poked to `$20`, or the enemy attack poked to `$90` (damage `$8F` ≥ 128): `PlaySound($3A)` at `26:93F7`, no HP change, no invulnerability, **but the knockback still happens** (step 6 runs before the damage test). `player_level`=`$11`: no damage and no sound or invulnerability at all (`26:9408` skips to `9422`).
- **Pit fall** (k5 area `$6A`, player poked onto the pit column at (`$578`,`$168`), screen 5): next frame `31:F07C` `sub_state`=1 and `31:F084` `$6D`=`$60`. `AreaPitFall` drops slots 0/1 by 8 px per frame to y `$1C8` (the first point 8 px below that is not b0). At f2432 the k5 record matches: `31:E5E5` `area_id`=`$69`, slot 0 at (`$578`,`$2D8`), and `31:E600` `$0620`=`$30` (slot 1 is drawn at `$25C` = −124). `sub_state` 8 follows at f2458 (`30:CA53`). The timer then counts `$2F`→`$21` 1/frame, and at `$21` (f2476) the 16-frame camera shake starts (`cam_ofs_x` alternates `00/FC/00/04`). **Quirk:** `JumpHeightTable[$2F]` = `$76` = **+118**, where the series (−102 −110 ? −124) wants −118 (a sign slip in the ROM byte). So on the first fall-in frame of `sub_state` 8, slot 1 is drawn 118 px *below* the body (f2462: y `$35E` vs body `$2D8`). Here that is off the bottom of the view. The port reads the table from the ROM, so it keeps this.
- **Pits outside the k5 areas** (`render_level.py --all --coll` → `gfx/areas/coll_index.txt`): b0 cells exist in the 9 k5 areas `6A 6D 6E 8A A2 A5 AE AF B4` (k5 area `$9F` has none) and in three areas **without k5**: `$91` (2 isolated cells), `$B9` (192) and `$BA` (144). `$B9`/`$BA` have the same pit counts as k5 areas `$AF`/`$B4`. In `$B9` (pit column at (`$178`,`$518`)) and `$91` (cell (`$668`,`$B68`)), `AreaEdgeLink` `31:E573` runs, the read starting at the k0 bytes **never matches**, and `31:E607` sets `sub_state`=8 with the fade-in. But the body is still standing on a b0 cell, so `PlayerStatusTick` restarts the fall ~25-46 frames later, forever: **an endless fall/fade loop (soft-lock)** if a player ever stands on those cells. (Whether `$91`/`$B9`/`$BA`'s pit cells can be reached in play is not checked.) The port must keep the k0 read and the re-trigger.
- **Conveyor / current (g)** (area `$20`, b5 column at x `$260-$29F`, player poked to (`$278`,`$228`)): the push is **down**. Slot 0 is type `$06`, whose init sets `obj_param1` `$0680` = 4, and no absolute write to `$0680` exists, so a player current always flows down. With no pad input the body drifts at speed `obj_aux` 0 (1 px per 2 frames), and `obj_aux` +1 every 16 `$08` ticks (`26:9FC4`, cap 9 at `26:9FCE`; every frame while status b6). **Holding DOWN** (`pad_dir` = 4, literally, not "the current's direction") lifts `obj_aux` to ≥ 6 at the next 16-tick step (`06` then `07` at once, f2410). **Holding UP** against it: the climb slows as `obj_aux` ramps 1…5, stalls at 6 (f2490) and is pushed back from 7. The player got 92 px up (`$228` → `$1CC`) before being carried back.
- **Sinking (g)**: on a `$40` cell `obj_flags` b6 is set at `26:9F57` (and copied by the sprite pass `28:80A2`), and walking speed drops to 1.5 px/frame (above). Slot 0's meta is `$4C` on the ground in the sink and goes back to `$A7` as soon as the jump timer is non-zero (it was `$4C` only on the frame the jump started).
- **(e) static answers:**
  - **`player_level` = `$11` is unreachable in normal play.** Level-up `26:9181` only INCs while `player_level`&`$F0` = 0, so the max is `$10` (16). The other writers are `LoadGame` `23:BD88` (a checksummed save image), `30:D969` (restores a value it pushed itself) and the switched-off `DebugPauseCheat` `15:BF47` (`$0F`). So `CMP #$11` at `26:9406` is a **developer invincibility value**: you only get it by a RAM or save edit.
  - **AI `$0A` `AiDuelStart` `27:A70E`** (set on slot 0 by the `AiPathWalk` `$FD` terminator): first frame msg group 3 / 3 ("I'm Stom training under Tornel. NAME! Let's have at it!"), `sub_state`=`$10`. After that, slots 0/1 move up 1 px/frame until slot 0 y = `$86`. Then: slot `$0D` → AI `$52` `AiDuelOpponent` `27:A753` with `obj_coll`=`$60`, slot 0 → AI `$0B`, CHR R5 = `$4E`, `story_flags_F7F8`+1.
  - **AI `$0B` `AiPlayerDuel` `27:A24A`**: the player cannot walk (dir 0, meta `$A7`, jump timer and orbit level held at 0); only B (sword charge/swing) works. The `HitPlayer` AI-`$0B` branch pushes both fighters 8 px down every 4th touch, with no damage. That matches Tornel's hint (msg 3.0, "force your opponent against the back wall").
  - **Platforms**: AI `$38` `AiPitShuttle` `27:AFA9` (types `$7E`/`$7F`, meta `$B6`, speed 2) steps while the probe point ahead (`PlatformProbeOfs` `27:AFED`, by dir&6: (0,−24) (12,0) (0,8) (−12,0)) is still a b0 pit cell; otherwise it reverses (dir+4) and pauses 32 frames (`obj_freeze`=`$20`). So it shuttles across a pit. AI `$3C` `AiCrumblePlatform` `27:AFF5` (type `$9F`) is the same mover, but once ridden (`ride_platform_slot` = X → `obj_phase`=1), each frame without the player sets `obj_coll`=0 and meta `$B7` and DECs `obj_anim` (SFX `$64` at `$1E`). At 0 it despawns: a **one-use platform**.
  - **Status messages** (group `$20` = 32 in `gfx/text.txt`): `$19` "You're poisoned!", `$1A` "Your body is paralyzed!", `$1B` "Your body is turned to stone!".
- **Sink** (attr `$40`): walking speed drops from 2 to 1.5 px/frame (6 px per 4 frames) as soon as `player_bg_attr` = `$40`.

Hit handlers 0 (`26:90AA`, player attacks enemy), 2 (`26:94B0`, talk probe) and 3 (`HitHandler3`) are in "Hit handlers 0 / 2 / 3 + gold drop" (s78). The emulator checks of the rules above were done in s77 (list above). The two corrections were b1 mid-jump and the sink meta; the new quirks are the `JumpHeightTable[$2F]` sign slip and the non-k5 pit loop.

**Player status** (`obj1_flash` `$0710` low nibble, named from the code above): 0 none, 1 paralysis, 2 stone (grey palette `$4D`, slot 0 frozen), 3 poison (−4 HP / 128 frames), 4 transformed (speed 2, meta `$53`, magic blocked). The hi nibble is the s44 b6/b7 status (b7 + b5-4 → meta `2C/30/34/38`; b6 → speed 9). **s76, named:**
- **b7 = changed form** (magic 7 Change). Set only at `ChangeFormApply` `30:DF2F`, the A key in `SubstChangeForm`'s menu: SFX `$39`, a 31-frame CHR R4=`$4D` effect, then `obj1_flash` |= (`$21`<<4) \| `$80`, then `SpendMp($14)`. So the MP is spent on confirm, not on the magic-7 press (the s76 probe shows no spend at the press).
- **b5-4 = form index 0-3** (the menu cursor `$21`). `PlayerSetSpeed` gives speed 5 and meta `2C/30/34/38`[form].
- b7 makes `PlayerMagic` go to `SubstFormRevert` `19:BB9D` (any magic press turns you back). That one clears b7/b5-4 with `AND #$4F` at `19:BBD8` and plays SFX `$39`.
- **b6 = warp exit.** Set only by event act 24 `EvActWarp` `30:D280`: slots 0/1 are moved to slot `$1E`'s position, and `$0561`=`$0641`=2 (low-obstacle attr). While b6 is set (`26:9B7D`): speed 9, spin pose `9B41`, `obj_bg_mask`=`$34`, the jump magics 8/9 refuse (`27:A112`/`A143`), and `ObjMoveStep` `26:99AF` has a b6 case. The bit clears as soon as (`$0561`\|`$0641`)&6 = 0, i.e. once the player has walked off the obstacle/wall tiles it landed on.
- Save drops the low nibble only (`SaveGame` masks it), so a form change survives a save. New game (`30:C996`), the inn (`16:9632`, low nibble only), death (`19:B900`) and `19:BC76`/`19:BE26` (cutscenes) clear it.

Otherwise the param is looked up in the 0-terminated list `26:9045` = `26 27 5E 5F 62 6D 6C 34 25` and flag `26:904F`[i] is **set** (byte = id>>3, so only F000-F0FF): param `$26`→F058, `$27`→F059, `$5E`→F06D, `$5F`→F06E, `$62`→F06F, `$6D`→F070, `$6C`→F071, `$34`→F0A1, `$25`→F099. The 5 params `$5E $5F $62 $6C $6D` (`$5E` = Zebu) are also the `NpcTalk` special talkers. Of the immune list, `$74 $73 $69` have no h1 placement. |

#### Text window + font (session 12)

- **PPU upload queue** `TextQueueUpload` `20:8836`: with NMI disabled (`20:8871`/`8869` clear/set PPUCTRL b7), append a 4-byte entry at `$6200`+`$0B`: `PPU hi ($21), PPU lo ($20), length ($24), source offset in page $60 ($25)`; `$0B` = (`$0B`+4)&`$1F` → **8-entry ring `$6200-$621F`**, write index `$0B`, read index `$0A` (NMI consumer). Callers spin on `$0A`≠`$0B` until the queue drains.
- **Param blocks** (16 bytes → `$20-$2F`): `20:8879` (window open) = `00 28 40 00 40 00 …` → dest `$2800`, row stride `$22/$23`=`$0040`, len `$40`, src 0; `20:8889` (print) = `20 28 00 00 40 00 …` → dest `$2820`, len `$40`.
- **`TextWindowOpen` `20:8500`**: load `20:8879`, `TextDrawFrame` `20:87B7`, wait for the queue, fill `$6200-$621F` with `$FF`. `87B7` calls `TextFrameRows` `20:87EF` 5× (X = 0,3,3,3,6) — each builds 2 rows of 32 bytes in `$6000` from the 3-byte templates `20:8899` (`left fill×29 right`, every row starts with tile `$FE`), queues them to `$20/$21` and adds the stride. Templates: `18 19 1A` (top), `1E 20 1F` (sides), `1B 1C 1D` (bottom). Result: **nametable 2 rows 0-9** (`$2800-$293F`) = top border, 8 text rows, bottom border. Then `$6001-$6041` = `$FF` → 64 bytes to `$2BC0` (attribute table of NT2: palette 3 everywhere).
- **Font** (`tools/font_dump.py [R0 R1] [--out] [--scale]`, run session 12 → `gfx/font.png`): with `ShowMessage`'s R0=`$3C`/R1=`$38`, **BG tile id = ASCII code** for `$20-$7E` (A-Z `$41-$5A`, a-z `$61-$7A`, digits also at `$00-$09`/`$30-$39`); `$18-$1D` frame corners/edges, `$1E/$1F` left/right border, `$40` = ▼ page prompt (`TextPageBreak` draws it at `$2910`), `$FE` = blank (R1 bank `$39`). R1 `$38-$39` (tiles `$80-$FF`) holds the HUD set (`LIFE FORCE`, bars, `EX:`, `$`, `LV:`), so `TextPrint` writes raw ASCII bytes into the nametable with no translation table.

#### Menu strings — bank 16 `MenuPrint` `16:8933` (session 14)

The bank 16 menu (shops, inn, pawn shop, inventory drop/use, save/load) does not use `TextPrint`. It has its own printer:
- **`MenuPrint` `16:8933`** (A = string id → `$17`; 9+ callers in `16:81A4-8246` and others): `JSR $C676`, then clears `$22-$25`/`$28`. The 4-byte record at **`MenuStrings` `16:8C1B`**`[id]` = `PPU lo, PPU hi` → `$20/$21`, `string ptr` → `$10/$11`. Each string byte is stored as-is (BG tile id) to `$6000[$28]` by `MenuPutc` `16:8A02` (`$24` = length, `$28` = cursor). On `FF` it runs `JSR $C676`, `JMP 16:8AB0`, which queues one entry `($21, $20, $24, $25)` in the PPU ring `$6200` (same format as `TextQueueUpload`) and waits via `$C67D` while rendering is on (`$01`&`$18`).
- **Escape bytes:**

| Byte | Handler | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `FF` | `898E` | end + upload |
| `FE s` | `8994` | 6 chars of the **save-slot name** from `$7118` (s even) / `$7718` (s odd), i.e. offset `$18` of the slot image = WRAM `$6418` (6-char name-entry buffer written by bank 19 `19:A3E7/A45F/A552`; the dialogue code 04 uses the zero-terminated copy at `$6400`) |
| `FD s` | `89B8` | **save-slot level**: `$71F7` (s=0) / `$77F7` (s≠0) as 2 digits from `16:89E0` (`"00"`…`"16"`, 17 pairs). Offset `$F7` of the slot image = `$7DF7` = `$0421` (`SavePlayerState`) → this **confirms `$0421` = player level, range 0-16** |
| `FC` | `8A0D` | item name `MenuItemNames` `16:9086`[`$19`] (`$FF` → `$49`), `FF`-terminated; same 74-entry order as `20:8A5C` |

- **Save slots** (`16:9CE0`, A = descriptor, table `16:9D32`: `src, dst` words): 0 `$7100→$7D00`, 1 `$7400→$7D00`, 2 `$7700→$7D00`, 3 `$7A00→$7D00`, 4-7 the reverse. Each slot image is `$300` bytes (`$7D00-$7FFF`). So **slot 0 = `$7100` + backup `$7400`, slot 1 = `$7700` + backup `$7A00`**. `MenuSave` uses 4,5 (slot 0) / 6,7 (slot 1).
- **The menu font is not pure ASCII**: letters/digits/punctuation are ASCII tile ids, but `0A 0B 0C` = "Sword", `7B 11 12` = "Armor", `0D 0E 0F` = "Shield", `5C 5D` = " of " (e.g. item 0 `0A 0B 0C 5C 5D Wind` = "Sword of Wind"), `3C 3D 3E 5B` after spell-ball names (item 6 "Tornado <=>[") is a 4-tile glyph, likely "Bracelet". Record 1E's raw text is `You Durchad` (record 2E has `You purchased`). In the menu CHR, `D`/`c`/`d` may be glyph tiles, so the dump shows the bytes as-is.
- **Menu CHR (session 70).** The shop/inventory setup at `16:8203` sets `chr_r0_alt` = `$3C` and `chr_r1_alt` = `MenuChrR1` `16:8232`[`$6424`] = `3E 3A 3A 38` (index 0-3, so `$6424` picks the menu kind; it also chooses the exit in `SaveLoadExit`). Save/load `16:9D67` uses `3C/38`, the same pair as the dialogue box (`ShowMessage` `30:D356`). So **every menu glyph is in R0 `$3C`** (tiles `$00-$7F`, shared with the dialogue font). R1 (`$80-$FF`) only holds the backdrop pattern, which differs per kind. Dumps: `gfx/font_menu_3C_3E.png`, `gfx/font_menu_3C_3A.png` (`font_dump.py 0x3C 0x3E/0x3A --scale 2`); `3C/38` = the existing `gfx/font.png`. Glyph map (R0 `$3C`): `$00-$09` digits; `$0A-$0C` "Sword", `$0D-$0F` "Shield"; `$10-$1F` the multi-letter pieces "mor"/"Power"/"Ite(m)" plus window-frame lines; `$20-$2F` punctuation and small glyphs; `$30-$39` a second digit set, `$3C-$3E` + `$5B` = **"Bracelet"** (confirms the session-14 guess); `$41-$5A` A-Z, `$5B` "et", `$5C-$5D` " of "; `$61-$7A` a-z, `$7B` "Ar", `$7C/$7D` ◀ ▶ arrows, `$7E` "SP". Read by eye from the PNG at scale 2, not pixel-checked tile by tile.
- **Records** (59, `16:8C1B-8D06`; full list from `tools/menu_dump.py --items --out gfx/menu_strings.txt`, run session 14): `00-05` equipment-category headings (Sword/Armor/Shield/…) at `$28E3-$2A03`, `07` "Do you wish to drop?", `08` "You cannot drop!", `09/0A` "Now using {item}.", `0B` "Sell {item}?", `0C` "Anything else?", `0D` "I won't buy that.", `0E/29` "YES NO", `0F/28/2A/2B` 64-byte line clear, `14/15/38` "1:/2:{name} LV:{lv}", `16` "Cannot save!", `18-1B` shop titles ARMOR SHOP / TOOL SHOP / INN / PAWN SHOP, `1C` "Do you wish to buy?", `20/26/2C` "Please come again.", `21` "You have too many already.", `22/2F` "You haven't enough money.", `23` "Need to rest? Please stay.", `24` "It will cost you $", `25` "How do feel today?" (sic), `27` "Is there anything you wish to sell?", `2E` "You purchased {item}.", `36` "Load this game?", `37` "Save this game?", `39/3A` "GAME:{name} LV:{lv}". `MenuSave`'s ids (`$0F`,`$16`,`$38`,`$37`,`$0E`) match: clear / "Cannot save!" / slot line / "Save this game?" / YES NO.

**Tool:** `tools/text_dump.py [dict|tables|msgs|all|at BB:AAAA [n]] [--json f] [--out f]`. Run session 11 → `gfx/text.txt` + `gfx/text.json`: **511 messages in 34 groups** (group sizes 1-32), no unknown control codes, and every message ends in `00`. Some pointers are shared, e.g. 0.2/0.5/0.8 → `21:A036`.

### Save / load module, bank 23 `$BC00` (session 11) (VERIFIED self-emu, s39: SaveGame + current image; slots/checksums not exercised; s91: `SaveLoadPage` save/load screens + the current image's WRAM VERIFIED vs FCEUX by state, see "Reference screens §4")

Bank 23 `$BC00-$BEFF` is code, not text. Jump table: `BC00`→`SaveGame` `BC09`, `BC03`→`LoadGame` `BC8E`, `BC06`→`LoadGameAndInit` `BCDA`. Bank 23 is mapped at `$A000` by `SetR7($17)` at **7 sites** (not one): `30:C9DF` (state-8 entry `C9DA` → `BC06`; s63: it is state 8, not 5), `30:D4EC` (`JMP $BC00`), `30:D8DF` (together with `SetR6($10)`, then `JSR $8000` = bank 16 menu, which calls `BC00` at `16:9C4B/9C64` and `BC03` at `16:9BC6/9BD6`), `31:E500/E535` (`BC00`), `31:F0B0/F1DC` (boot: compares the WRAM copies `$7100-73FF` vs `$7400-76FF` and the `AMI` tag at `$71FD`; this is the boot slot check `BootSaveCheck`, see "Save data + WRAM layout" (session 31)).

- **`SaveGame`**: saves `$10-$2F` → `$6000` and keeps `$0710` hi nibble only. `SaveAllowed` `BDF6` (C = bit 7 of `23:BF00`[`$6C`]) decides what happens:
  - **Save allowed:** zero `$7D00-$7FFF`, then `SavePlayerState` `BD1F`: `$7DF0`=`$6C` area, `$7DF1-F4` = player x lo/hi, y lo/hi (`$70/$90/$B0/$D0`), `$7DF5/F6`=`$03C0/$03C1`, `$7DF7`=`$0421`, `$7DF8`=`$0620`, and the tag `$7DFD-$7DFF` = `41 4D 49`. Then 5 block copies from descriptors `23:BDC0` (6 bytes: src, dst, count lo/hi): `$6400→$7D00` (128), `$6480→$7E00`, `$0702→$7D80`, `$07DC→$7DB0`, `$7D00→$6D00`. Then `SaveChecksum` `BD92`: an 8-bit sum of `$7D00-$7FFF`, stored in `$70F5` (copy `$70F4`).
  - **Both paths:** copy `$7D00-$7FFF` → `$6D00-$6FFF` (backup mirror), then restore `$10-$2F`.
- **Copy-length quirk** (`BlockCopy` `BCF7`): the count loop copies **lo + 1 + hi** bytes, not the 16-bit count + 1. For example `FF 01` → 257, `FF 02` → 258, `7F 00` → 128, `1C 00` → 29, `23 00` → 36. The port must copy exactly these lengths.
- **`LoadGame`**: 4 reverse descriptors `23:BDDE` (`$7D00→$6400`, `$7E00→$6480`, `$7D80→$0702`, `$7DB0→$07DC`), then `LoadPlayerState` `BD60` (inverse of `BD1F`). No checksum check here.
- **`LoadGameAndInit`**: `LoadGame`, then copy `$7E00→$6480` and `$7F00→$6580` (512 bytes, overwriting part of the `$6480` block), `$6D`=`$FF`, `$41`=1.
- `23:BF00` `AreaSaveFlags` (256 bytes, one per area `$6C`; only 3 readers: `23:BDF8`, `16:9BE8`, and the dead copy `31:FDF8`): **bit 7** = `SaveGame` writes the record (`SaveAllowed` `BDF6`); **bit 0** = the **menu Save command is allowed here** (session 12). `MenuSave` `16:9BE1` (bank 16 menu, bank 23 at `$A000`): `$7000`=`$FB`; if bit 0 = 0 → menu strings `$0F`,`$16` via `16:8933` (+ SFX `$4E` via `16:8A9F`), wait 48 frames, `$4B`=0, `$7000`=`$AB`, exit. If bit 0 = 1 but `$0710`&`$0F` ≠ 0 (player status/hit state) → strings `$0F`,`$38`, same wait. Otherwise strings `$37`,`$0E`, confirm `16:84DA` (A=`$A1`; C set → cancel, string `$0F`, back to `16:9B3A`), then `SaveGame` and slot pick by `$646B` bit 0: slot 0 → `16:9CE0`(4),(5), checksum `$70F5` → `$70F0/$70F1`; slot 1 → `9CE0`(6),(7), → `$70F2/$70F3`. So `$80` = autosave-only (record written but menu refuses), `$81` = both.
- **How the menus are reached in gameplay (session 43, `tools/emu/probe_screens.py`, screens in `gfx/compare/screens/`):** START → `sub_state` 9 (status screen: name, condition, LV/attack/life/defense, armour/shield; `irq_mode` 2); START again returns to 8. SELECT → `sub_state` `$12` (bank 16 equipment grid Sword/Armor/Shield/Power, `irq_mode` 6); UP past the top row switches to the Item/Magic grid. **START inside the SELECT menu** (`16:8149`, only while `$07DD`=0) → `16:821F`(X=0) then `JMP 16:9AEE` = **`SaveLoadPage`**: clears `$7000`/`$646A`/`$646B`, prints strings `$39`,`$17`,`$14`,`$15` (GAME / slot 1 / slot 2 rows). In its loop (`16:9B3A`), SELECT/START exit (`9C7A`), LEFT/RIGHT toggle `$646A` b0 (SAVE ↔ LOAD icon), UP/DOWN step `save_slot_sel` `$646B`, B exits with `$7000`=`$AB`, A with `$646A` b0 = 0 → `MenuSave`, with b0 = 1 → "Load this game?" (`$36`, `$0E`, confirm `16:84DA`). In area `$01` A gives "Cannot save!" (`AreaSaveFlags` bit 0 = 0), as documented above. HP `$03C1` = 0 → `sub_state` 3 `PlayerDeath`, and about 450 frames later `main_state` 3 (the intro/title replays: there is no separate game-over screen in this run).
- **YES/NO confirm `16:84DA` (session 85):** `$22` = string id (`$41` → `$49`), `$23` = 1 (cursor on **NO**). Each frame `WaitFrame` + `16:850D` (cursor draw) + `ReadPadsMenu`; any d-pad edge (`pad_new`&`$0F`) does `INC $23` + SFX `$4B`; A or B (`pad_new`&`$C0`) exits with `$02DC`=`$F0` and `LSR $23` → **C=1 = NO/cancel, C=0 = YES**. So A pressed straight away cancels; one d-pad press first selects YES (B on YES also confirms).
- **Save / load page (web port, session 102)** — `web/src/game/states/saveload.js`, called from `states/equip.js` (START in the SELECT menu, the `$7000` = `$FB`/`$AB` exit, and the `$FA` load tail). Findings while porting:
  - **`SaveSlotCopy` `16:9CE0` copies `$2FF` bytes, not `$300`**: `$14`=`$FF`, `$15`=2 with a DEC/BNE count gives 255 + 256 + 256. So the last byte of each slot image (`$73FF`, `$76FF`, `$79FF`, `$7CFF`, or `$7FFF` on a load) is never copied. `SaveSlotDescs` `16:9D32` holds 8 × (src, dst): 0/1 = `$7100`/`$7400` → `$7D00`, 2/3 = `$7700`/`$7A00` → `$7D00`, 4-7 = the reverse. Load slot 1 uses only descriptor 0 (slot 2 uses 2); save slot 1 uses 4 + 5 (slot 2 uses 6 + 7). Each slot is kept twice, and only the first copy is ever loaded from.
  - **`SaveGame`'s not-allowed path mirrors from offset X = `area_id`**: `SaveAllowed` `23:BDF6` does `LDX $6C`, and the `BCC` to the mirror loop `BC65` skips the `LDX #0`. So `$7D00-$7FFF` → `$6D00-$6FFF` copies only offsets `area_id..$FF` of each page. (`MenuSave` never reaches this path for areas without bit 7; the autosave callers `30:D4EC`/`31:E500`/`31:E535` do.)
  - **SAVE page layout `SaveLoadSetup` `16:9D52`:** 6 × `MenuFrameRows(9)` from `$28C0`, two 3×3 pictures (`MenuPicture3x3` `16:88A8`, tiles `$E8`+, at cols 7/10 row 6), strings `$39 $17 $14 $15`, the mode cursor `SaveModeCursor` `16:9C85` (OAM slot `$39`) and the slot arrows `SaveSlotCursor` `16:9CAE` (OAM `$027C`/`$0280`). UP/DOWN first re-print the old slot's row (string `$3A` if `$646B` b0 = 0, else `$39`), then step the slot.
  - **Lag frames** (measured with `web_state.py --until`): YES on SAVE → `SaveGame` + 2 × `SaveSlotCopy` runs from L251 to L33 five frames later (4 NMIs). YES on LOAD → `SaveSlotCopy` covers 1 NMI, then `LoadGame` ends before the next one (1 NMI). The port yields `SPIN` that many times, because the first NMI does the DMA and the later ones skip it.
  - **`MenuExitFade` `30:C80C`** (`$FB` after a save attempt, `$AB` after B / "Cannot save!"): `SwitchBank16(13)`, `FadeOut(3)`, 1 frame, `irq_mode`=0, 30 frames, `FadeIn(3)`, `SwitchBank16(8)`, then RTS to `30:D8E5` (the normal menu tail). **Load tail** `30:D930`: `$7000`=0, BG + sprites off, WaitFrame, `ObjInit(1,4)`/`(0,6)` keeping level/HP/max HP, `$07E8`=0, `StatRecalc`, `SwitchBank16(9)` + `HudWindowSetup` `19:BA65` (HUD fill + blocks 0-4, `HudHpBar`, `HudChargeMeter` `26:8D59`, `HudRedraw` 5..0), then `$6D`=`$FF` and `sub_state` 1. It all fits in one frame because rendering is off, so every queue flush is immediate.
- **Dead code copy:** `31:FDE0-FDFD` is byte-identical to `23:BDE0-BDFD` (tail of the load descriptors + `SaveAllowed`; `FDC0-FDDF` differ), 0 xrefs to `FDF6` — build filler, like `20:8466` and `21:BF32`. The stale-copy pattern (leftover bytes from a previous build at the same in-bank offset) should be expected in other bank tails too.
