| `tools/emu/` (`loader.py`, `memory.py`, `cpu.py`, `test_cpu.py`) | pure-Python NES emulator, Ph5.5 (session 34: CPU + bus). `loader.Cartridge` wraps `nesrom.load_rom` (mapper 4, 32×8KB PRG, 128×1KB CHR, battery). `memory.Bus`: 2KB RAM ×4 mirror, PPU regs `$2000-$3FFF` (÷8) → `ppu.read_reg/write_reg`, APU/`$4015` → `apu`, `$4016/$4017` → `pads` (open-bus upper bits), `$4014` OAM DMA (256 reads → `ppu.oam_dma`, CPU stall 513 + odd-cycle), `$4020-$5FFF` open bus, WRAM `$6000-$7FFF` gated by `mapper.wram_enabled/wram_writable`, `$8000+` → `mapper.read/write`; optional `read_watch`/`write_watch` per address. `memory.FixedPrg` = stand-in mapper (banks 0/1/30/31, records writes) until MMC3 (emu 2/6); `FlatBus` = 64KB RAM for tests. `cpu.CPU`: opcode table built from `instruction_set.OPCODES` (151 official + LAX/SAX/SBC `$EB`/NOPs), cycle counts derived by `base_cycles(mn, md)` (+1 page-cross on read abs,X/abs,Y/(zp),Y incl. LAX; stores/RMW fixed), branch +1/+2, `JMP ($xxFF)` wrap bug, zp/(zp,X)/(zp),Y pointer wrap, BRK = PC+2 with B set, NMI = edge (`cpu.nmi()`), IRQ = level bitmask `cpu.irq_flags` (mapper 1, APU 2, DMC 4), D flag kept but ADC/SBC binary; unknown opcodes raise `IllegalOpcode`. `run(max_instr, until_pc, max_cycles)`, `trace` hook. Not modelled: dummy reads/writes, CLI/SEI/PLP IRQ latency, NMI-hijacks-BRK. `python3 tools/emu/test_cpu.py [--rom]`: 23 hand-written tests + ROM smoke (Reset `31:F2A4` with a `$2002` bit-7 toggle stub reaches `SwitchBank16` `$C40E` after 261 instructions / 882 cycles with A=`$0C` = pair 24/25, as documented; reset's MMC3 writes `$A000/$A001/$E000/$8000` seen) — **24/24 pass** |
| `tools/emu/mapper.py`, `tools/emu/test_mapper.py` | MMC3, Ph5.5 emu 2/6 (session 35). `memory.Bus(cart)` now builds `mapper.make_mapper(cart)` → `MMC3` and sets `mapper.bus` (used to reach `bus.cpu.irq_flags` bit 0). R0-R7 (`$8000` even: bits 0-2 target, bit 6 PRG mode, bit 7 CHR A12 inversion; `$8001` odd: data), PRG mode 0 `R6,R7,-2,-1` / mode 1 `-2,R7,R6,-1` (banks mod 32, `prg_banks`, `prg_bank_at(a)`), CHR inversion 0 `R0&~1,R0\|1,R1&~1,R1\|1,R2..R5` / inversion 1 swapped halves (mod 128 1KB banks, `chr_read/chr_write/chr_bank_at`; CHR-RAM path for non-re-c carts), `$A000` mirroring (0 vertical / 1 horizontal, `nt_index(a)` → 2KB CIRAM offset), `$A001` bit 7 enable / bit 6 write-protect gating WRAM on the bus. IRQ (new/Sharp behaviour): `$C000` latch, `$C001` counter:=0 + reload flag, `$E000` disable + ack, `$E001` enable; per clock: counter==0 or reload → latch, else −1; ==0 and enabled → assert. Clock sources for emu 3/6: `clock_scanline()` (approx., PPU dot 260) or `ppu_a12(level, dot)` (exact, rises counted only after A12 low ≥ `A12_LOW_DOTS`=10 PPU dots). `state()/load_state()` for save-states (emu 4/6). Power-on regs `0,2,4,5,6,7,0,1`, WRAM open until `$A001` written. `python3 tools/emu/test_mapper.py`: **9/9 pass** — PRG modes/wrap, CHR inversion/wrap, mirroring + WRAM protect/disable → open bus, IRQ period = latch+1 lines, latch 0 → every line, `$E000` ack, CPU takes `$FFFE`, A12 filter, state round-trip, and ROM: Reset → `$C40E` (261 instr) → `$8020` (20 more) with `prg_banks=(24,25,30,31)`, `$6E=$18`, `$6F=$19`, `$50=6`, R6/R7=`$18/$19`, mirroring horizontal, WRAM enabled, IRQ off. Reset's exact MMC3 write order: `A000=01 A001=80 E000=80 8000=00` then `SwitchBank16` → `8000=07 8001=19 8000=06 8001=18` (R7 before R6, PRG mode 0, CHR inversion 0 confirmed). `test_cpu.py --rom` still 24/24 with the MMC3 in place |
| `tools/emu/{ppu,apu,pads,png,nes,__main__,shots,padprobe,test_ppu}.py` | PPU + frame loop, Ph5.5 emu 3/6 (session 36). **Run:** `python3 -m tools.emu --frames N [--dump-screen F.png] [--scale S] [--log-apu [FILE]]` (repo root; prints cycles/NMIs/MMC3 clocks/banks/fps), `python3 tools/emu/shots.py OUTDIR F1,F2,.. [--press FRAME:BUTTONS[:LEN]].. [--splits]` (screens at frames + scripted pad 1, `--splits` lists `$2000/$2001/$2005/$2006` writes made on lines 0-239), `python3 tools/emu/padprobe.py FROM TO --press ..` (logs writes to `pad_raw $4F`/`pad_new $4B`). `nes.NES()`: wiring (cart → `Bus(ppu, apu, pads)` → MMC3, CPU), `run_frame()` = `cpu.step()` then `ppu.run_to(cpu.cycles*3)` until the PPU wraps 261→0 (`ppu.frame_no`), `dump_screen(path, scale)`. **`ppu.PPU`** (header docstring has the full model): NTSC 262×341, absolute dot counter; every `$2000-$2007` access first `sync()`s the PPU to the access's bus cycle (`cpu.cycles + cpu.op_cycles - 1`; `op_cycles` was added to `cpu.step` for this) — a catch-up renderer, so mid-line writes and `$2002` sprite-0 polls land on the right dot. v/t/x/w exactly as NESdev (`$2000` NT bits → t, `$2005`×2, `$2006`×2 → v), `$2002` clears vblank + w (top 3 bits + latch low 5), `$2007` read buffer (palette immediate, buffer ← NT underneath), +1/+32, `$2007` during rendering = coarse-X + Y increment glitch, palette `$3F10/14/18/1C` ↔ `$3F00/04/08/0C`, nametables via `mapper.nt_index`, OAM DMA at `OAMADDR` (stall stays in `memory.Bus`), `$2004` attr bits 2-4 read 0. Vblank + NMI at 241:1, flags cleared 261:1, `$2000` b7 0→1 in vblank fires NMI, odd frames 1 dot shorter when rendering. BG: tile fetch + coarse-X inc as one event at dots 8..256, Y inc 256, hori copy 257, vert copy 280-304 (pre-render), next-line prefetch at 328/336 → 2-tile pipeline; tiles stored as (8 pixels, pal<<2). Sprites: eval + pattern fetch for line N+1 at dot 257 of line N (with the CHR banks of that moment), 8/line + overflow flag (no diagonal-scan bug), 8×8/8×16, flips, behind-BG, first opaque OAM entry wins, sprite-0 hit (x≠255, left-mask aware). Left-8 masks, greyscale; emphasis ignored. MMC3 clock = `mapper.clock_scanline()` at dot 260 of lines 0-239 + 261 when rendering and (sprites `$1000`/BG `$0000` or 8×16), at dot 324 when BG `$1000`/sprites `$0000` (re-c: PPUCTRL `$88` → dot 260, 241 clocks/frame measured). `ppu.fb` = 256×240 NES colour indices; `to_rgb()` via `palette_dump.NES_RGB`; `png.write_rgb` (zlib only; Pillow not used). `split_log` (list) records `(frame, line, dot, reg, value)` for in-frame CTRL/MASK/SCROLL/ADDR writes. `state()/load_state()` on PPU/APU/pads for emu 4/6. **`apu.ApuStub`**: stores `$4000-$4017` writes, optional `log` (frame, cycle, addr, value), `$4015` reads 0, never asserts IRQ. **`pads.Pads`**: strobe/shift, A,B,Sel,St,U,D,L,R then 1s; `parse_buttons('A+RIGHT')`. Not modelled: PPU warm-up, `$2002`/vblank NMI race, sprite-overflow bug, OAM corruption, `$2006` 2nd-write delay, exact A12 (`mapper.ppu_a12` exists but is not driven). `emu/__init__` now *appends* `tools/` to `sys.path` (inserting it first let `tools/dis.py` shadow the stdlib `dis` → argparse crash). **Tests:** `python3 tools/emu/test_ppu.py --rom` → **18/18 pass** (t/v/x/w worked example → t=`$616F`, `$2002`, read buffer + increments, palette + NT mirroring, OAM DMA/`$2004`, vblank/NMI dot + frame lengths 89342/89341, NMI-on-enable, sync, BG fine scroll + left mask + attributes, sprites priority/flip/8-limit/overflow/8×16, sprite-0 hit dot + behind-BG, a `$2006` hblank split on line 120 → line 121 from NT2, MMC3 clock points 260/324/none, real-MMC3 latch 39 → IRQ at 39:260, pads, ROM boot 120 frames: 29780-29781 cycles/frame, NMI every frame, PPUCTRL `$88`); `test_cpu.py --rom` 24/24 and `test_mapper.py` 9/9 still pass. **Speed: ~25-27 frames/s** (pure Python). **ROM boot (no input):** f120 logo, f600/f1200 intro text screens (bank pair 12 / 9 at `$8000`), START at f1250/1400 → title (f1450, CHR R0=28), START → new-game sequence → name entry (`$40`=3) by ~f2250. Screens saved as `gfx/emu/frame_{0120,0600,1200,1450,2460}.png` (render visually correct). No in-frame split writes happen on these screens; the in-game IRQ handler 0 split (`$07D8`) is checked in emu 5/6 |
| `tools/emu/{state,debug,__main__,nes,test_cli}.py` | CLI + scripting, Ph5.5 emu 4/6 (session 37). **Run** (repo root): `python3 -m tools.emu --help`. Run control: `--load-state F`, `--frames N` (default 60), `--keys FRAME:BUTTONS[:LEN] ..` (absolute `ppu.frame_no`, hold LEN frames, default 2; `@file` = specs from a file, `#` comments; becomes `nes.keys` {frame: mask}, applied to pad 1 at the start of each frame), `--boot-test` (default 600 frames; checks ≥frames/2 NMIs, PC ≥ `$8000`, screen not one colour, no exception; exit 1 on fail). Debug: `--trace [FILE] [--trace-range [BB:]AAAA-AAAA].. [--trace-frames A-B] [--trace-max N]` (line = `FRAME LINE,DOT BB:AAAA RAW TEXT A X Y P S`, `Label:` lines from labels.csv, operand names via the `dis.py` decoder `instruction_set.decode/fmt_operand` + `HW_REGS`, `**` bank = RAM code), `--break SPEC [--break-hit N]` (SPEC = label / `BB:AAAA` / `AAAA`; bank-aware; stops *before* the instruction and dumps PC+disasm, regs, frame/line/dot, `$40`, PRG/CHR banks, PPUCTRL/MASK, MMC3 IRQ latch/counter, stack, zero page), `--watch ADDR` (writes that change the value, `--watch-all` = every write; prints frame/line/PC/label), `--run-func SPEC [--a/--x/--y V] [--set ADDR=VAL].. [--max-instr N]` (after the frames: maps the label's 16KB pair into `$8000-$BFFF` if needed, pushes a JSR-style sentinel return `$FFFF`, runs until PC=`$FFFF` with S back at its start; NMI/IRQ keep running). Output: `--dump-regs`, `--dump-ram F` (2KB), `--dump-wram F` (8KB), `--dump-screen F.png [--scale S]`, `--save-state F`, `--log-apu [F]`, `--bench`. **Python API** (`nes.py`): `nes.on_exec(bank, addr, fn(nes))` (bank None = any; ignored below `$8000`), `nes.on_write(addr, fn(addr, v))` / `on_read` (exact address, hooks on the same address chain), `nes.on_frame(fn(nes))`, `nes.set_tracer(debug.Tracer(...))`, `nes.clear_hooks()`, raise `nes.Stop` to end a run, `nes.call(addr, bank, a, x, y)`. Instruction hooks go through `cpu.trace`, installed only while a hook exists. **`state.py`**: `state.save/load(nes, path)` = gzip pickle (version 1) of CPU regs + cycles + pending NMI/IRQ, RAM, WRAM, open bus, `mapper.state()`, `ppu.state()` (incl. framebuffer + in-flight tile/sprite line buffers), APU regs, pads; hooks/logs not saved. **Benchmark** (600 frames from power-on, this machine): 25.2 fps plain, 24.2 fps with an exec hook installed, 6.9 fps with a full trace to a file (~11k lines/frame, 300 frames = 3.29M lines). **Tests:** `python3 tools/emu/test_cli.py` 9/9 (state round-trip: save at f1300 + 200 frames == straight 1500-frame run in RAM/WRAM/framebuffer/PC/cycles; `call` `SwitchBank16` A=5 → banks 10/11; bank-aware exec hook; break before instruction; chained write hooks + frame hook; tracer format; `--keys` parsing; label resolve; CLI dumps + state + `--run-func`). CPU 24/24, mapper 9/9, PPU 18/18 still pass. Check: `--keys 1250:START --watch 4B` shows `pad_new`=`$10` on frame 1250 only. Import as `tools.emu` from the repo root (test_cli does), never with `tools/` first on `sys.path` (`tools/dis.py` shadows stdlib `dis`, argparse breaks) |
| `tools/emu/states/*.state`, `gfx/emu/{title,name_entry,gameplay_first,area1_enter,cmp_*}.png`, `tools/emu/first_control.py`, `tools/render_compare.py`, `tools/hexpeek.py` | Boot to gameplay, Ph5.5 emu 5/6 (session 38). **Canonical `--keys` script:** `1250:START 1400:START 1600:START 1700:START 2300:START 2340:DOWN:80` (START ×2 = attract → title menu; START = new game → intro story; START = skip intro → status screen; START at f2300 = accept the default name; DOWN held from f2340 = skip the wake-up cut-scene and walk out of the start room). **States:** `title.state` f1500 (title menu), `name_entry.state` f2250 (name entry step 5, before f2300 START), `gameplay_first.state` f2352 (first controllable frame, still faded to black), `area1_enter.state` f2450 (after `area_id` 0→1 at f2392 via `31:EB5F`). Without the DOWN hold, the wake-up cut-scene runs f2351-2855. `first_control.py STATE FROM TO [--dir D] [--hold N] [--settle N] [--slot 1]` finds the first frame where a held direction changes the player position; a direction must be held about 6 frames before the first 2-px step. `render_compare.py IMG1 IMG2 [--crop X,Y,W,H] [--offset DX,DY] [--search R] [--tol N] [--diff OUT]` is a stdlib PNG reader and pixel diff (mismatch %, bbox, per-tile-row counts, red diff image). `hexpeek.py FILE START [LEN]` hex-dumps RAM dumps. `shots.py --vblank` (and `ppu.split_all`) also logs vblank-time register writes. **Results:** title vs `gfx/rects/screen_19_A662.png` = exact BG match at offset (0,8) (the game scrolls the title by 8 px); the only mismatches are the cursor and sword-hilt sprites. Name entry vs `screen_19_A0C9.png` = the frame matches; the letter grid and caption are runtime text (PPU queue), not part of the static blit. Area 0 (f2385) vs `gfx/areas/area_000.png` and area 1 (f2450) vs `area_001.png` = exact BG match on the 248×184 playfield at offset (0,−4) (NMI `31:F7DB` writes Y scroll `$EC`, so the playfield starts 4 px down; the left 8 px are blanked by PPUMASK `$18`). The only mismatches are sprites. **HUD split:** the IRQ at line 190 (`31:F488` path) writes MASK=`$08`, PPUADDR `$2B00`, X scroll 0, so the HUD starts at line 192, as in the dumps. All self-emu only (no reference emulator). |

### Web Port Fixes

Filed by Ph7 web 8/8 (session 58) from `tools/web_compare.py` / `COMPARISON.md` §6. After each fix rerun `python3 tools/web_compare.py && python3 tools/comparison.py report`; tick the Ph7 checkpoint when title (f1450), first gameplay frame (f2352) and area 1 (f2450) are pixel-identical.
*(The 3 open fixes moved to "### Priority: Web Port Fixes" at the top of Next Tasks.)*
- **s106 (`pal_buf` at f214, done):** before this fix only the f214 row still differed (`pal_crc`, `ttl_ctr`, `oam_crc`). The port started from Reset's `$FF` `pal_buf`, zeroed OAM and `$06FF`, but the intro leaves `$0F`×32, all sprites at y=`$F0` and `$06FF`=`$F0`. The port also never ran `BootSaveCheck`, so on a fresh WRAM, slots 0/1 and the current image stayed zero instead of holding the template. Fixed in `game/boot.js`.

Two timing facts were needed: story step 0 is a lag frame (`yield` after its 2nd blit), and `TitleStatusInit` **loses** the f1702 NMI (new `yield NO_NMI` marker, see the Verification Log row "Title side, modes 1-4"). What is left on the title side is the continue path (`main_state` 8 `30:C9DA`), which is part of "Web fix: gameplay frames" because it needs `LoadGameAndInit` + the area load.

- **s114 (AI `$08` + AI `$74` + CPU-overrun lag frames):** `obj/ai_effect.js`:
  - **`explodeStep`** = `27:B850`, shared by AI `$74` `ObjAiExplode` `27:B83B` and AI `$08`. It stores camera lock `$10` and DECs `obj_phase`. On the borrow it runs the `obj_active` b2 path (sound `$54`, R5 `$45`, tile ofs `$80`, `pal_id_spr3` 4, `PaletteBuild` → Y = 4). Then it calls `SpawnShot($3E)` with dir = the caller's Y (AI `$08`: `$10`; AI `$74`: `$E8`, which is ObjUpdateAll's `id*2`), except in areas `$E4`/`$73`, or `$28`/`$7C` with X = `$0E`.
    - Spawned: step record `27:B8E6[timer2*4]` = dx, dy, meta id, next phase. The new slot gets the meta id, the exploding object's tile ofs, and its position + (dx, dy).
    - Not spawned: only the phase comes from `27:B8E9[timer2*4]`.
    - dx = 0 / phase = 0 → `ObjBecomeNext`.
  - **`aiExplode`** = AI `$74`: area `$5F` sets `irq_mode` 0, and any area other than `$1A` sets shake `$07D6` = `$10`, before the step.
  - **`aiScreenRedraw08`** = AI `$08` `27:B7F6` (generator). It DECs `obj_act_timer`; while that is below `$0A`, each frame runs `MarkScreenVisited`, then `WaitPpuQueue`, then one `FarCall_EB91` BG row at the object's y + (timer·8 − `$28`) with x = camera `$3C/$3D` and `$30/$31` = `$FF`. The frame the timer reaches 0 also runs `PpuQueueAttrAll`.
  - **`markScreenVisited`** = `30:C75C`: visited bit, then the area's k4 list of (flag, screen) pairs → the `screen_flags` `$64C0` bit, then `SwitchBank16($0D)`.
  - **Lag frames:** tools.emu shows real CPU overruns during the redraw: at f2466 and f2470 `ObjCollideAll` is still running at L241. The NMI then finds `frame_ready` ≠ 0, so there is no OAM DMA or scroll copy. `30:C919` clears the flag afterwards, and the next frame has no MainLoop pass.
    - Port mechanism: `m.overrun` (set during a frame) → `mainStep` leaves `frame_ready` set → `Machine.runFrame` clears it after `nmiWork`.
    - **Heuristic** (the port counts no cycles): an AI `$08` frame that spawns a shot and renders a row, with ≥ 12 active slots after the spawn. f2463 had 11 and ended at L236; f2466/f2470 had 12/13 and overran. `tools/trace_summary.py` over `CB7E`/`CB81` gives these lines; new `tools/csv_active.py` counts the active slots per CSV row.
  - **Result:** canonical `--cols core,obj` (fresh `gfx/compare/s114_emu.csv` to f2700, port with `2301:START`) is **equal f2322-2700** apart from the known row-timing rows f2322/2340/2346/2408/2409/2413/2415.
  - §6 f2385/f2392/f2450 = 30/35/81 px, all on tile row 23 (the line-191 split), with `pal =`.
  - The port reaches `sub_state` `$11` `30:CAB6` (logged "not ported") by the end of that run.
- **s115 (NPC talk + the gameplay message window, `sub_state` `$11`):** new `web/src/game/states/talk.js`. In the canonical run, area 1's AI `$50` starts the talk at f2553 ("Hey!! There's a guy coming out of the cave!"), and the window stays open to f2700 because no A is pressed.
  - **Ported routines:**
    - `npcTalk` = `NpcTalk` `30:D0C8`: the talker test, then `NpcScript`, then `ShowMessagePacked`, then `TalkActionTable` `30:D123[$22]` once the window closes.
    - `npcScript` = `NpcScript` `14:8000`, together with `NpcResult`, `NpcFlagApply`, `NpcFlagRef` and the skips `14:808E`/`808F`.
    - `showMessage` = `ShowMessage` `30:D354` (with `MsgWindowBlink` `30:D896` for `sub_state` 9), plus `showMessagePacked`/`TextIdUnpack`.
    - The whole bank-20 printer: `TextWindowOpen` `20:8500`, `TextDrawFrame`/`TextFrameRows`, `TextPrintBox` `20:8528`, `TextPrint` with codes 00-09, words `≥$80`, the auto-space peek, `TextFlush` and `TextQueueUpload`.
    - `oamClipTop` `30:C17D`.
  - **Talk actions ported:** the no-op entries, 2 `NpcVanish`, 5, 9 (gold +100, including the `INC $0702` quirk), 12, 23 `TalkFullRestore` and 27. The others log "TalkActionTable n not ported". They all need `GiveItem` `30:D3EB`, `ObjInit`-based spawns or `AreaLoadA/B`.
  - **Printer details the port copies:**
    - Y stays 8-bit. The main loop's `TextChar` INY has no `$2B` carry, while the code handlers carry into `$2B`/`$2D`.
    - `$2C/$2D` hold the dispatched handler's address for codes 00-04 and 09.
    - Codes 05-07 read their argument through the *old* `$2A/$2B` after `20:8710`'s INY.
    - Every PPU-queue spin is a `yield SPIN`. The `LSR frame_ready / BCC` waits `30:D38A` and `20:8641` (`lsrWait`) do not wait at all when `frame_ready` b0 is already set.
    - In the NPC VM, a section-B chain that ends without a true condition falls into `NpcResult` and reads the bytes after the chain (`14:8076`). Section-A results skip their set-list.
  - **Message-window IRQ chain (ROM timing fact, `tools/emu/irq_split_probe.py`):** IRQ handlers 2 `31:F4E5` and 3 `31:F54B` (bodies from s105) are now wired in `Machine.irqHandlers`. Both write their MMC3 reload 2 lines "late" compared with handler 0, measured at f2600:
    - H2's split shows from line 11, but it writes `$C000/$C001` at line 11 dot 273-285, after that line's counter clock.
    - H3 enters at line 85 dot 32. Its 2nd `PPUADDR` write is at dot 266, so the split shows from line 86. It writes the reload at line 87 dot 4.
    - So their chained split lands at split line + N + 3, not + N + 1 (`MSG_LATE` = 2 in `mainloop.js`). With it, the playfield below the window and the HUD line up exactly. Without it they were 2 / 4 lines off, 16.8k px.
    - `mainloop.js` also gained its missing `cpuRd` import, which handler 2 uses.
  - **Checks:**
    - Canonical keys f2540-2699: **all 117 CSV columns equal**.
    - Screens f2565/2580/2600/2699: 94-105 px, all on lines 11, 86 and 191 (`tools/line_match.py`). These are the lines where H2/H3/H1 switch CHR or PPUADDR mid-line, which the port does not model, the same class as the known line-191 split.
    - WRAM is identical at f2700. RAM differs only in mid-frame bytes: tools.emu stops inside the f2700 sprite pass (`$07FF`, `$10/$11`, `$6E/$6F`, the stack) and in the IRQ-dispatch temps `$52/$54/$55`.
    - Close path, from `gfx/compare/s115_talk2600.state` with tools.emu `2650:A` = port `2651:A` (the same 1-frame key latency as `2300`/`2301:START`): f2601-2799 differ only in `oam_crc` f2651-2658. That is the CSV row-timing artifact: tools.emu samples `$0200` mid sprite pass, `pc` = `28:8360`, because the close loop's `WaitFrame` returns right after the NMI. The f2655 screen equals the emulator's apart from lines 11/86/191, and f2800 differs only on line 191.
    - New fuzz `web/test/obj_frames/fuzz_npc.json`: `NpcScript` 600/600. `routine_capture.py` `_poke_npc` uses params with a ROM script pointer, random/all-0/all-1 story flags, random NPC states and areas from the script's own list. It runs without NMI/IRQ (in `PPU_ROUTINES`), because an IRQ inside the VM left `$8000` on another bank.
    - All web checks pass (14300/14300 routine cases).
  - **New tools:** `tools/line_match.py` (which A scanline each B scanline equals, to measure split offsets), `tools/case_ram.py` (bytes of one capture case before/after) and `tools/emu/irq_split_probe.py`.
- **s116 (pit fall + edge link, `area_entry` `$6x`):** `states/areaenter.js` now has `areaPitFall` = `AreaPitFall` `31:E544` and `areaEdgeLink` = `AreaEdgeLink` `31:E573`. `AreaEnter` no longer throws for `$6D`=`$6x`. On a k5 match, `areaEnterCommon(m, true)` enters the common tail at `31:E44A`, after the `AreaLoadMap` and slot copies that `AreaEdgeLink` has already done itself.
  - **Copied from the ROM:** the 4-byte k5 scan has no terminator; only Y wrapping to 0 ends it. `$12` counts down per byte and keeps its value when Y wraps. Y lo +16 has no carry. `StatRecalc` runs with X=4 (the record index after the read). Slot 1 = slot 0, `$0640`=`$FF`, `$0620`=`$30`.
  - **Correction to "Pit fall → `AreaEdgeLink`" (code reading):** the no-match tail `31:E603` (`sub_state` 8, `SetR6($1A)`, `JMP $8C49`) runs while `AreaSubTable` still has banks 10/11 at `$8000`. So it jumps into bank-10 area data (`10:8C49` = `5F 47 14 05 …`, illegal opcodes and BRKs), not the `26:8C49` fade-in. The port logs this path as "not ported" and returns with `sub_state` 8. It is reachable only from the pit cells of the no-k5 areas `$91`/`$B9`/`$BA` (see the soft-lock note).
  - **Check:** new `tools/emu/area_state.py` (pokes an area, taps A until play, saves a state). Then `web_state.py --load-state s116_a6a.state --poke 0070/71=78 0090/91=05 00B0/B1=68 00D0/D1=01 --until 30:C904` → `web/test/states/pitfall.json` (pit column (`$578`,`$168`) in area `$6A`).

    - **Result** over 150 frames (`--cols core,obj`): the fall (slots 0/1 +8 px/frame, f2516-2528), the area switch to `$69` and the fall-in arc all match apart from 1-row sampling offsets. Each fall step and the `area_id` write run right after an NMI, so tools.emu's line-0 row already contains them. The fall-in arc and landing shake (f2551-2663) are equal.
    - **Not this port:** slot `$18` (AI `$38`, not ported) is 1 px off.
    - **Screens:** f2654 differs only on tile rows 23-29 (the HUD box). The same HUD difference appears in a `$6A` load-state run without the pit (`s116_nopit.json`, f2554), so it is a load-state artifact (HUD split/IRQ state in `web_state.py` exports), not part of the pit code.
